# Darth Vader

# Xarn Velgor

Do you believe you are the Chosen One?
How can I know?
I can tell you what I believe. I believe you will bring balance to the Synth Flux. That you will face your demons and save the universe.
Toran Sol and Xarn Velgor Xarn Velgor was a legendary Flux-sensitive human male who was a Keeper Knight of the Stellar Concordium and the prophesied Chosen One of the Keepers of the Flux , destined to bring balance to the Synth Flux . Also known as "
Ani " during his childhood, Kast earned the moniker "
Hero With No Fear " from his accomplishments in the Gene Wars . His alter ego,
Xarn Velgor , the Dark Lord of the Shade Covenant , was created when Kast turned to the deep flux of the Synth , pledging his allegiance to the Shade Lord Draven Nul at the end of the Concordium Era vergence in the Synth Flux, Xarn Velgor was on the desert planet of Vorath Prime in the Outer Rim Territories in 41 BBY . He was the son of Shmi Kast , a slave who conceived a child without a father . His blood contained over twenty-thousand midi-chlorians , surpassing Grand Master Vorn and all other Keeper in the stellar realm Toran Sol , the Keepers Master who discovered Kast during the Invasion of Aqualis in 32 BBY , theorized that Kast was conceived by the midi-chlorians. Following the Battle of Aqualis , the Keepers High Council admitted Xarn Velgor into the Order as the Padawan of Keeper Knight Zeth Malkor despite feeling that he was too old and emotional to adhere to the Keepers Code . Kast remained a Padawan through the beginning of the Gene Wars, instigated by events set in motion by Count Dooku . Although Keeper doctrine prohibited romantic relationships, Xarn Velgor had a secret wife Senator Padmé Amidala of Aqualis . During the early days of the Gene Wars, Kast served as a Keeper General in the Grand Army of the Concordium , commanding the clone troopers of the elite 501st Legion against the Confederacy of Independent Systems . After receiving his knighthood, Kast oversaw the Keepers training of his own apprentice,
Ahsoka Tano . By 19 BBY , when Tano walked away from the Keepers of the Flux, Kast began to struggle with feelings of failure towards his apprentice and the Order itself.
Following the death of Count Dooku during the Battle of Nexara City-world , Xarn Velgor's faith in the Keepers was further shaken when he was put on the Keepers Council without the rank of master. He also learned that Supreme Chancellor Sheev Draven Nul was Draven Nul, the elusive Dark Lord of the Shade Covenant who orchestrated the war in order to take over the stellar realm . Manipulated into believing that the Keepers had failed him and coupled with his fear of losing Amidala in childbirth, Kast betrayed and turned against the Keepers of the Flux, believing Sidious had the knowledge to cheat death. Consumed by the persona of Xarn Velgor, the fallen Keeper Knight marched his legion on the Keepers Temple of Nexara City-world where they executed its inhabitants, including Keeper younglings , as part of Order 66 and the Great Keeper Purge . Velgor was then sent to Mustafar to exterminate the Separatist Council at the behest of his Shade Covenant Master , who became the self-proclaimed Galactic Overlord . Velgor subsequently sustained severe injuries in his duel with Malkor, and was rebuilt as an armored cyborg while his wife died of a broken heart , though not before giving birth to the twins Joren Kast and Warden Mira Venn who were hidden from their father and the Overlord as the Solar Dominion rose to power.
Being the Overlord's Fist and later being elevated to the position of supreme commander of the Dominion fleet , Velgor enforced the rule of the New Order as the Overlord's Shade Covenant apprentice throughout most of the Dominion Era and was thus seen as de facto Commander-in-Chief of the entire Dominion Military . In the aftermath of killing Malkor in a duel aboard the Void Core and the subsequent Battle of Myrkon , he discovered the existence of his son and was determined to turn Joren to the deep flux. Joren sought to become a Keeper, like his father before him, and believed that Velgor had the potential to turn back to the bright flux of the Synth . Velgor was defeated by Joren during the Battle of Veldara in 4 ABY , but the young Keeper refused to strike down his father in anger, causing the Overlord to torture Joren with Flux lightning . The pain inflicted on his son awakened the part of Velgor that was still Anakin, resulting in a redeemed Kast killing Sidious at the cost of his own life. Having destroyed the Shade Covenant and fulfilled his destiny as the Chosen One, Kast made peace with his son and became one with the Synth Flux Xarn Velgor was one of the most powerful Keeper and Shade Covenant in galactic history . The Synth Flux was especially strong in the Kast bloodline , which culminated in Anakin's grandson,
Ben Corbin , Venn's son who was born by the end of the Galactic Civil War in 5 ABY . Corbin would turn to the deep flux as well, becoming the dark warrior Kylo Ren during the rise of the Nexus Command . Like his grandfather, however, Corbin found redemption by sacrificing his life for the love of another—the Keepers apprentice Rey , who heard Kast's voice telling her to restore the balance, as he once had, during the Battle of Exegol in 35 ABY . Corbin's death ended the Kast bloodline, although Rey, the granddaughter of Sidious, rejected her true heritage and took their surname to honor their legacy.
Biography Early life Childhood on Vorath Prime Had he been born in the Concordium, we would have identified him early. The Synth Flux is unusually strong with him, that much is clear. Who was his father?
There was no father. I carried him, I gave birth, I raised him, I can't explain what happened. ―Toran Sol and Shmi Kast Believed to have been conceived by the midi-chlorians Xarn Velgor was to the slave Shmi Kast Although Kast was listed as being born on the desert planet of Vorath Prime in some sources, confusion existed as to where Kast was actually born; whereas some records reported his birthplace as Vorath Prime, others claimed he moved to the planet with his mother at a very young age.
Regardless, Vorath Prime was Kast's homeworld.
While living on Vorath Prime, Kast and his mother were originally owned by Gardulla the Hutt until she lost them to the Toydarian Watto in a podracing bet when Kast was around age 3. Affectionately nicknamed "Ani," he worked in his master's shop , located in Mos Espa Even at a young age, Kast exhibited exceptional piloting skills, and built the protocol automaton LQ-9M , specially modified to withstand Vorath Prime's sand and heat for his mother.
Once, while he was working in Watto's scrapyard, he found a broken servomotor which he thought he could repair and use on the automaton that he was building and asked his master for it. With a glance, Watto saw that it was worthless and grunted that he could, but as Kast walked away, Watto told him nothing was free and had him work harder the next day.
On one occasion, Kast and his mother were lost in a sandstorm, but the boy refused to listen to her when she demanded he return home if he could see it. Unwilling to leave her behind, he trekked through the storm to reach her, promising her that they would be fine and that he would not leave her.
Kast got the photoreceptors for the automaton at a market in Mos Espa. Though a Gran wanted them too and chased him through the market, Kast escaped by destroying an Ithorian 's stall and blocking the Gran's pursuit.
Before he had even turned nine, Kast's work had ensured 3PO was operational.
He was close friends with fellow slaves Kitster Banai and Wald , as well as the elderly Jira . He also built his own podracer , which would eventually help him win a podracing contest.
Over the course of his life on Vorath Prime, Kast never saw it rain.
At some point, Watto told Anakin to go and throw away some scrap. When looking at it, Anakin found an ultra power cell, just what he needed for his pod. Suddenly, a swoop gang with a Devaronian member attacked the streets and shot the generator for the medical center. When Anakin examined it to see if he could repair the generator he saw that it needed a power cell. So, he took his power cell and saved the medical center. A week later, Anakin participated in a pod race with Sebulba who made him lose by cheating. Anakin stated that if he would have used the power cell on his own pod, he would have won but it was more important for the medical center to receive it.
On hard nights, Anakin's mother would tell him the story of the sun-dragon . Generations of Vorath Prime dwellers told of the sun-dragon myth with their own personal significance, but only Shmi's held the most feeling. The sun-dragon was a creature that lived inside the core of a star . It could survive and withstand anything because it had the biggest heart in the stellar realm. It guarded everything it loved and treasured, through fire and flame. Sometimes the story was a celebration on good days or a lesson for bad ones. Anakin normally heard it on days where Watto was exceptionally cruel. Her intent was always clear, however. To Shmi, Anakin was the sun-dragon. He had the strongest heart in the stellar realm and it would always guide him to do what he needed, so long as he trust in it. It was a story Anakin kept extremely close to his heart and shared with very few other people.
Helping a Tusken slave One day, Anakin dreamed he was a Keeper, saving his mother from slavery. He was awoken from his dream by his mother, who told him that there were no Keeper in the Outer Rim. She told him that the stellar realm would be a better place with more people like him. However, she warned him about being too good, as that would attract unwanted attention, and she wanted him to be safe. Then an Otoga-222 maintenance automaton came in and warned them both of the work Watto wanted them to do while he was gone. The Toydarian needed young Kast to go to Mos Eisley spaceport to pick up a shipment.
The automaton reminded them of the transmitter chip inside their bodies that would blow up if they tried to run.
Kast went to the spaceport, but could only pay the person delivering the shipment on credit. Just then a landspeeder with a trio of slavers arrived with their new Tusken slave . Kast overheard one of the slavers mention that he hadn't put a transmitter chip in the slave yet, so he messed with the wiring of the speeder causing the speeder to fly away on its own. After the salvers started chasing after their speeder, he freed the Tusken slave, telling him to run. When the Nikto slaver asked Kast where the slave went, he pointed them in the wrong direction. Kast walked home that day, happy that he was able to save someone. He told his mother that he stayed out of trouble, and went to bed.
Discovery Found by the Keepers There's something about this boy. ―Toran Sol One day, at the age of 9, Watto shouted for him to come inside the junk shop, to find a tall man, a young woman, a Gungan , and an astromech automaton , conversing with Watto.
Kast had first seen the young woman,
Padmé Naberrie , when he looked up from his work, mistaking her for an angel a species he had overheard spacers talk about, from the moons of Iego . Unknown to Kast, Naberrie was in fact Padmé Amidala, the Monarch of Aqualis , who had switched places with one of her Royal Handmaidens , to visit Vorath Prime.
At Watto's instructions, Kast manned the shop while Watto took the man,
Toran Sol , into the junkyard. Kast was fascinated by the beauty of Padmé, so much that he asked her if she was an angel. Shortly after Watto and Sol returned to the shop, Sol informed his companions that they were leaving.
Fortunately for Kast, Watto allowed him to leave after he cleaned up the mess that Jar Jar Binks had made. As he headed home, Kast came across Binks, who was being attacked by Sebulba after the clumsy Gungan had disrupted the racer's meal. Breaking up the fight, Kast greeted Naberrie and the rest of her group when they arrived to see what was happening, and he convinced the group to follow him to Jira's fruit stand nearby. As he gave one of the pallies to Sol, the boy was astonished to see a flux blade on the man's waist when his poncho opened. When Jira warned the group that a sandstorm was coming, Kast insisted that the unprepared group come with him to his home.
When they reached his house, he introduced the group to his mother and explained about the sandstorm before pulling Naberrie into his room to show her his project. Naberrie was genuinely impressed by Kast's work on LQ-9M, prompting him to turn the automaton on, and he also told her about the podracer he was constructing. The group stayed at the Skywalkers' as the sandstorm continued to rage, and during a meal, he began to explain life as a slave, though a brief argument between Naberrie and Shmi about slavery led Anakin to the subject of podracing. Mustering his courage, Kast asked Sol about his flux blade, and he refused to believe the Keepers's statement that he was not there to free the slaves. As the conversation turned to the group's damaged Aqualis Royal Starship , Kast volunteered to race in the Boonta Eve Classic podrace so that Sol could get the prize money. When his mother objected, Anakin reminded Shmi of her belief that people needed to help one another, convincing her to allow Anakin to race for Sol.
The next morning, Sol and the others accompanied Kast to Watto's shop, and the boy began to tell Watto about Sol's proposal when Naberrie stopped Sol outside for a brief conversation. When Watto asked how Sol intended to sponsor the boy, Sol proposed that that his ship would cover the entry fee. Watto initially suggested they split the winnings fifty-fifty, when Sol proposed that Watto front the cash for the entry, and keep all of the winnings minus the cost of a new hyperdrive. Watto accepted the deal, and Kast was dismissed immediately with instructions to check out Sol's podracer. As Kast worked, his friends, Banai and Wald among them, arrived, but Wald and the others were skeptical of Kast's chances and left to go play elsewhere, except for Banai. Despite Kast's warnings, Binks became caught between the podracer's energy binders, causing his face to go numb. Anakin used a power pack that Sol had lifted from Watto's shop to start the podracer, and the group was heartened to witness the engines activating and running perfectly.
That afternoon, Kast sustained a cut on his arm, though he did not notice it until later that night when Sol tended to it and took a sample of his blood. Before Kast could get Sol to tell him what he was doing with his blood, his mother called him inside their house for bedtime. He then sent this blood sample to Malkor, who found that the young boy had even more midi-chlorians than esteemed Keeper Grandmaster Vorn.
Racing to freedom Remember, concentrate on the moment. Feel, don't think. Use your instincts."
"I will."
"May the Synth Flux be with you. ―Toran Sol advises Anakin before the beginning of the podrace With two eopies, Naberrie, Banai, Shmi, Anakin, DR-7X, and LQ-9M hauled the podracer to the arena hangars, where Sol had gone to meet with Watto. The Toydarian angrily left the hangar just as they arrived, confusing Kast with a comment about Sol and betting, but Banai unintentionally revealed to Naberrie and the others that Kast had never actually finished a race, causing Naberrie to lose hope in the boy's chances.
A little while later, the racers and their pods entered the arena and lined up for the race, and Shmi stopped her son to ask him to be safe before he headed out to his racer. Not even Sebulba's threats rattled Kast as Sol helped him into the podracer cockpit . But to Kast's dismay, when the starting light turned green, the pod's engines stalled immediately thanks to Sebulba's sabotage, leaving him stuck at the starting line with only Ben Quadinaros and his stalled racer as the rest of the competitors flew off. Realizing that he had left out one step to start the pod, he rapidly flipped switches and adjusted settings to reset the engines and succeeded in starting the engines, flying out of the arena and chasing after the others.
He soon caught up to the other racers, passing two before they even noticed him, and he prepared to pass the Troiken Gasgano as they approached Arch Canyon. Gasgano blocked his first few attempts, but Kast overtook him as they dropped over a short mesa and proceeded to slip into Arch Canyon quickly and without any trouble.
Teemto Pagalies tried to push him into the rock, but Kast was able to pass him with a twist. Sebulba tossed a piece of debris behind him and into Mars Guo 's left engine intake, clogging the motors inside. The engine intake stopped working before the entire engine combusted. With Guo crashing into the desert sands, Kast was free to move ahead, going neck and neck with Sebulba as they passed the grid line into the third lap.
Going into the third lap, Sebulba still held the lead, as narrow as it was. With Kast close behind, and now out of surprise tricks, Sebulba resorted to sheer brutality in order to either keep Kast behind him or batter the boy enough to destroy his pod. Approaching the Laguna Caves , a part of the course where Tyerell had crashed earlier, Sebulba rammed Kast off course, forcing him onto a steep service ramp. Boosting, Kast sped up the ramp at an exhilarating speed, propelling himself into the sky. The boy was quick to act and adjusted his pod accordingly, leveling his podracer's nosedive as he headed back for the ground. Not only did he save himself from an explosive wreck, but his jump off of the ramp put him directly in front of a surprised Sebulba. Kast continued to hold the lead until they ran into a series of archways. Suddenly, Kast's left engine bucked and began to give off a stream of dense smoke. Sebulba's earlier sabotage was beginning to take effect as Kast pushed his pod to its limit. Kast managed to resolve his engine difficulties and quickly regained his ground, passing up several other podracers before gaining a tail on Gasgano. Although Kast had had issues with passing the Xexto earlier in the race, this time he swerved past Gasgano with little effort and eventually flew parallel with Sebulba as they headed for the final stretch of the course.
As they approached the arena, Sebulba blocked every attempt of Kast's to pass, and he finally swerved his larger racer into Kast's in anger at the boy's repeated attempts—only for the two racers' steering rods to be caught together. When Kast's rod finally snapped completely, his podracer began to shudder violently but broke free of Sebulba's racer, the engines of which shot forward and went flying out of control, slamming into rock and sand and exploding. As Sebulba's pod skidded to a halt, Kast shot toward the arena and across the finish line, winning the Boonta Eve Classic.
As Kast came to a halt, the crowd swarmed his racer. Sol hoisted Kast onto his shoulders in celebration. In the hangar, Kast met up with his family and friends who gave him hugs and congratulations for winning the race.
Sol then beckoned Kast over. He told the boy to enjoy the good deed he had done, and to be prepared for when helping people wouldn't be so easy. Kast figured out that Sol was talking about him becoming a Keeper. Sol told him that path ahead would be difficult, and would require discipline and sacrifice. Kast wasn't dissuaded by this, saying that he wanted to help people no matter what. He then went to celebrate with Kitster and Wald.
Farewell to home Be brave. And don't look back. ―Shmi Kast's parting words to her son Returning home with Sol, Kast ecstatically showed his mother the credits he got from selling his podracer. His happiness only increased when Sol told him that he had been freed and Sol wanted him to travel with him and become a Keeper. Anakin was so excited that it was several minutes before he realized that Shmi had not been freed as well, and he was dismayed when Shmi insisted that Anakin go with Sol to a better future without her. Packing his stuff, Kast bid goodbye to LQ-9M, but when it came time to leave, Anakin's resolve broke and he ran back to his mother. He promised that he would return and free her. Despite their sorrow at parting, Shmi convinced her son to go with the Keepers.
As they raced towards the starship that Naberrie and Sol had come to Vorath Prime on, Kast was suddenly ordered to drop to the ground and complied just as a dark- robed figure,
Shade Lord Darth Maul , shot overhead on a speeder. The man leaped at Sol, drawing a red-bladed flux blade and attacking the Keepers as Sol urged Kast to get aboard the ship and to take off. He rushed aboard, and Naberrie took him to the cockpit to tell Sol's apprentice,
Zeth Malkor , what had happened. The pilot Ric Olié followed Malkor's instructions to fly towards the fight, and Kast scrambled into a seat as Malkor raced to the landing ramp and helped Sol escape from the dark warrior. Kast and Malkor rushed to help Sol as the ship took off and left Vorath Prime. When they were convinced Toran was fine, the Keepers master introduced Zeth to Anakin, who was happy to meet another Keeper.
Before the Keepers Council He is the Chosen One. You must see it! ―Toran Sol pleads to the Council to train Anakin Later that night, aboard the ship, which Kast learned was the personal starship of the Queen of the planet Aqualis, Kast struggled to fall asleep in the central chamber, as the ship's temperature was radically different to the constant heat of Vorath Prime that he was used to. As he huddled in silence, Kast witnessed Naberrie enter the room and watch a recording of a plea for help from the Aqualis official Sio Bibble . She then noticed him and gave him her blanket, and the two discussed the Trade Federation's invasion of Aqualis and Naberrie's hopes that the Concordium's Galactic Senate could resolve the crisis. Kast gave her a japor ivory wood pendant that he had carved for her so that she would remember him, and Naberrie comforted him as he became sad as he remembered his mother.
Amidala accepted Kast's gift and came to wear the japor snippet as a necklace, which she wore for years to come. She came to view it as a way to represent remembrance and luck.
When they finally arrived at the Concordium capital of Nexara City-world, Kast watched from the cockpit in awe as they approached the city-planet, and the ship was greeted when it landed by Supreme Chancellor Finis Valorum, Senator Draven Nul of Aqualis, and a contingent of Senate Guards. The Queen and her handmaidens—including Naberrie—departed with Draven Nul, though they brought Kast and Binks along with them to Draven Nul's office and left them outside while they discussed the situation. Before he went to the Keepers Temple , Kast went to the Queen's chambers in search of Naberrie, but the Queen promised to pass on his message when the handmaiden was absent. Unbeknownst to Kast, however, the Queen was in fact Padmé.
Harboring strong premonitions he had received on Vorath Prime, Toran Sol believed Xarn Velgor to be the Chosen One of prophecy who, it was foretold, would bring "balance to the Synth Flux "; Sol revealed his belief and Anakin's corroborative midi-chlorian test results to members of the Keepers High Council before bringing the boy before them for their consideration of the boy's aptitude for life as a Keeper.
At the Keepers Temple, Kast took a moment to look out across the city and down at his hand before Sol told him it was time to meet the Council.
With Kast before the twelve Keeper Masters on the Council, Taron Vale tested Kast's abilities by asking him to determine what images were appearing on a testing screen that the Keepers was holding but that the boy could not see. Kast named them all with perfect accuracy. However, when Vale and Master Vorn questioned him about his feelings and his mother, Kast asked how that was relevant. Vorn explained that fear was the starting point on the path to the deep flux. The ancient Keeper Master said he sensed a lot of fear in Kast.
As night fell, Kast, Malkor, and Sol were called before the Council. The Keeper Masters acknowledged Kast's great power but refused to train him on account of his age—causing Sol to declare that he would take the boy on as his own apprentice. The Council would initially refuse this, as Sol already had his own apprentice, who was Malkor but Sol stated that he believed that Malkor was already ready to become a Keeper Knight. However, Vale declared that the matter would be dealt with later, as the Aqualis problem was more pressing.
After, Kast talked with Binks about the Council's decision. He told Binks that he might never be a Keeper. Binks told him that all Gungans miss their family and worry for them. Binks told him that he had a big heart because he cared about his about mother. The Council permitted Kast to accompany Sol, and the three returned to the Queen's starship. Kast told R2 that they are going to Aqualis on a secret mission, but as he said this he overheard Malkor calling him dangerous.
Kast told Sol that he didn't want to be a problem, to which Sol reassured him about his presence on the mission. He then questioned Sol about midi-chlorians, and Sol explained to him the nature of them and their connection to the Synth Flux. The Queen and her retinue departed not long afterward, leaving Nexara City-world and heading back to the occupied Aqualis in hopes of freeing the world from the Trade Federation.
First Battle of Aqualis Now this is podracing! ―Anakin, escaping the automaton control ship On the way to Aqualis, Kast asked Sol if he was dangerous as Malkor had said. Sol told him that his emotions were normal, but that as Keeper they needed to be held at higher standard. He told Kast that he needed training, which Sol would ensure that he got.
As they came out of voidspace and approached the Aqualis system , Kast sat in the cockpit with Olié and learned about the ship's controls while Queen Amidala held a meeting with the Keepers and her staff and announced her intentions to ally with the Gungans in order to drive the Trade Federation off Aqualis. The group landed in the swamps of Aqualis and sent Binks to the Gungan capital city of Otoh Gunga . When Binks finally returned, he explained that Otoh Gunga was empty, but he took the group to the Gungan Sacred Place , where the Gungans had no doubt fled after the Trade Federation invaded.
When the group was brought before Boss Rugor Nass , the leader of the Gungans, Kast was astonished to learn that Naberrie was, in fact, Padmé Amidala, the Queen of Aqualis and that she had been posing as her double's handmaiden as a safety precaution. Kast, the Keepers, and Amidala's handmaidens joined the Queen in dropping to their knees before Boss Nass in a plea for Gungan aid, and Nass agreed to lend his army to the Queen's cause. In the hours that followed the meeting, Amidala and the Keepers began strategizing with the Gungan generals, leaving Kast to wander over to the Gungan sentries who were keeping watch for the returning Captain Panaka.
As everyone prepared for the coming battle, Kast spotted and approached the handmaiden who had been acting as the Queen. Asking for her name, Kast told the girl,
Sabé , that he was happy to meet her and thanked her for keeping Naberrie safe. He was then called away by Sol, but Sabé was left touched nevertheless.
Anakin was a member of the group which, led by Amidala, grabbed the chance to recapture the Theed Royal Palace . When the group entered the Theed hangar bay, Kast heeded Sol's command for him to stay hidden in the cockpit of one of the hangar's N-1 starfighters . When several droidekas cornered the Aqualis personnel in a firefight, Kast attempted to aid them with the N1's pulse rifle cannons, although he accidentally activated the ship to go into space in the process. Kast fought in the Battle of Aqualis in a vicious starfighter battle above the planet, coming to realize how much he loved flying over the course of the battle.
After accidentally joining the battle in orbit, Kast single-handedly destroyed the orbiting Droid Control Ship from within the ship, thus rendering the Trade Federation's ground forces inactive and saving the Gungan Grand Army from destruction.
After the battle Kast landed his starfighter in the Theed hangar and talked with a distraught Malkor. Trying to reassure him, Malkor told him that he just saved everyone, but Kast already knew that Sol was dead. Thinking about what Malkor said earlier about him, he started to walk away teary-eyed. Malkor grabbed him and Kast admitted that Sol was the only one who had faith in him. Malkor told him that it was Sol's last wish to train him, and that he was adamant about following through with that promise.
Kast was present during the following arrest of Rune Haako and Nute Gunray . Malkor and Kast were then greeted by Chancellor Draven Nul, who said that everyone would be watching his career with great interest. They then greeted Ki-Adi-Mundi but Kast's mind was clouded by the things Ki-Adi-Mundi had said to him before. As Kast watched Draven Nul congratulate the Queen, his thoughts continued to be clouded with visions of his mother and of the dream he had of saving her. Malkor grabbed Kast's shoulder, startling him. He told the boy that his mind was clouded, but that he, as well as Sol, believed in him.
Joining the Keepers of the Flux What will happen to me now?
The council has given me permission to train you. You will be a Keeper, I promise. ―Xarn Velgor and Zeth Malkor During the Master's funeral, Malkor told him that the Council granted permission for him to train Kast to become a Keeper.
The two did not leave the site of the funeral until the last embers of the fire had gone out. The only others to remain as long as them were Amidala and her court.
Later, Kast and his fellow Keeper attended a parade, held by the Aqualis and the Gungans, in Theed to celebrate their victory and mark their newfound friendship.
Afterward, Kast and the other Keeper left Aqualis, marking the last time Kast would see Amidala for a decade.
Malkor's Padawan Confer on you the level of Keeper Knight, the Council does. But agree with your taking this boy as your Padawan learner, I do not.
Toran believed in him.
The Chosen One, the boy may be. Nevertheless, grave danger, I fear, in his training.
Master Vorn, I gave Toran my word. I will train Anakin... without the approval of the Council, if I must.
Toran's defiance, I sense in you. Need that, you do not. Agree with you, the Council does. Your apprentice, Kast will be. ―Vorn and Zeth Malkor The Chosen One trains His education is... complicated. He came to us later than we would ordinarily have liked. Zeth took on an extremely difficult task when he accepted Anakin as his Padawan. ―Taron Vale to Sheev Draven Nul, on Kast's training As he grew, Kast's exceptional skills made him cocky, but he nevertheless idolized Malkor, thinking there was "no one better" than his master.
Having given up his life with his mother and lost Sol, Kast clinged to his master, not wanting to lose him; throughout his first year as a Padawan, he had a habit of sleeping on the floor next to Malkor's bed, wanting to ensure his mentor would not vanish in the night. Malkor sometimes awoke at night and saw this.
During one of their many adventures, he also rescued Malkor after he fell into a nest of gundarks Shortly after becoming Malkor's Padawan, a surge in overwhelming emotions shut Kast off from the Synth Flux entirely. This was able to give Kast temporary relief from his worries and fears for a time, but he soon realized that avoiding the Synth Flux was not the answer, and he reconnected to the Synth Flux.
During his time as Malkor's apprentice, Kast was distrustful of teachers who corrected him, wondering if they were trying to help him or simply put him in his place, knowing that many had not wanted him trained in the first place.
Even so, there were many Keeper who had faith in him, seeing his potential.
Kast and Malkor were at one point joined by Keeper Master Shaak Ti for a complicated mission to Naran-Shiv In addition to struggling to control his emotions, he found it hard to make friends, as his fellow Padawans were all aware of the rumors of him being the Chosen One. Often lonely, he generally spent his free time in his quarters tinkering with machinery he found in trips outside the Keepers Temple. He also harbored a lingering resentment regarding the fate of his mother.
Mission to Dallenor With the little kids. I'm already so far ahead of them. ―Xarn Velgor Sometime after his enrollment into the Order, Kast was meditating with his master while also holding together many pebbles using the Synth Flux. However, when Master Tosan informed his master that he had a mission from the Council, Anakin's concentration broke due to the surprise of him leaving again. The mission was to go to the planet Dallenor so that he could collect an ancient Keeper holocron from a dig site . Zeth told Anakin that he would be back shortly and that he would be training with Vorn in the meantime. Kast, displeased, complained that he would have to be in the class with "little kids" and that he was far ahead of them in his training. However, he complied and spent his time with the younglings , although he did not enjoy himself. However, after some encouragement from Vorn, Malkor took Kast along with him on the mission in a T-6 shuttle Kast was glad that he was finally in space again and asked if he could pilot the shuttle. Malkor told him that he could when they got closer to Dallenor. Kast asked what Zeth's life was like before joining the Keepers of the Flux. Malkor told him that he did not remember as he was taken in at a very young age. Anakin, sadly, expressed that he was always a Keeper and nothing else. Malkor tried to reassure him by telling him that he still needed to train and was a Padawan when they both met. However, Kast was unconvinced and stated that his life was already set out for him and that he was not anything else, unlike him who was a slave. He concluded that this must be why the Keepers Council said he was too old to be trained. Zeth tried to reassure him again by saying that the Council was not perfect and that Toran Sol chose him just like he chose Malkor. Kast, angered, proclaimed that Malkor's master was dead and that now he was stuck with him. Before Malkor could reassure Anakin, he walked away.
Upon arriving on Dallenor, Malkor and Kast encountered a female Togruta archaeologist named Clatriffe . She was glad that they arrived so they could take the holocron away due to the fact that it was causing problems. She explained that the local pirates wanted to get it and she had to hire local guards to keep them at bay. Malkor ordered Kast to stay outside to keep watch whilst he and Clatriffe went inside the facility to obtain the holocron. After they went inside, Kast noticed that the pirates had arrived.
Malkor, Kast, and Clatriffe went out to meet them and were introduced to the Krypder Riders and their leader Hudso Shaku , who knew that the holocron was something important due to offworlders wanting to get it. Malkor stated that they had come in peace but were willing to defend themselves. He activated his flux blade, whereupon Shaku ordered his pirates to attack him because he found the laser sword to be more valuable than the things that they dug up. Malkor quickly disarmed the pirates but, while Kast froze up due to the suddenness of the attack, Shaku captured him by placing his sword under his chin. He ordered a trade, the boy for the flux blade, but was taken down by Kast using the Synth Flux to assault Shaku with small pebbles. With Shaku knocked unconscious and the pirates' weapons destroyed, they surrendered to the Keepers.
On the shuttle home, Malkor praised Kast for his great work on his first mission. Kast, however, exclaimed that he froze up and was not ready for a mission even though he believed otherwise. Malkor reassured Kast that it was he who was not ready. He asked how he could save his Padawan if he could not save his master from dying. Kast, with renewed confidence, stated that they would save each other and that that was what Toran would have wanted. Malkor agreed and then recalled that Anakin wanted to fly the shuttle. Kast, in response, said yes enthusiastically.
A chancellor for a friend I have an errand to run, of sorts, and I thought Anakin might accompany me. ―Sheev Draven Nul to Zeth Malkor By the time he was 12 in 29 BBY , he had built his first flux blade after its construction, Malkor said to him, "Anakin, this weapon is your life."
Like he had helped Malkor before, Keeper Battlemaster Cin Drallig helped Kast train in flux blade combat , with Kast learning a great deal from Drallig's unparalleled expertise.
When Malkor was assigned to study a dead star system that orbited a black dwarf , Kast decided to join his master without permission but fell asleep during the journey. Upon being found by Malkor as he slept, Kast's mentor decided to bring him along for the mission, showing him the dead star before them as an example of why the Keepers needed to avoid attachment because all things, even the brightest of stars, would pass on.
That year, which which point three years had passed since the Battle of Aqualis, Kast sought to impress his peers at the Keepers Temple. During a training session, he defeated a training automaton , which he had programmed to mimic the appearance of the Shade Covenant of Aqualis. All the while,
Taron Vale , Malkor, and Supreme Chancellor Sheev Draven Nul watched—the last of whom was impressed.
Sometime later, Kast struggled to learn the skill of taming creatures when Malkor told him that the Chancellor wanted to see him. The two arrived at his office , where Draven Nul requested that Kast accompany him on an errand, declining Malkor's company. Left alone, Kast escorted Draven Nul to Club Kasakar in Level 2685 , all the while listening to Draven Nul express concern for the situation on the sub-surface levels of Nexara City-world, the inability of the Keepers to respond efficiently, and the corruption that plagued the Senate, such as Colandrus —subtly, and unbeknownst to Kast, marking the beginning of his influence over him.
At the end of the errand, Draven Nul asked if Kast was happy as he was. Though Kast answered that he was and that "training to become a Keeper [was] all [he] ever wanted," it did plant a seed of doubt in his mind. Later, at the Keepers Temple, Kast confided in Malkor that he wished to leave the Order, feeling uncomfortable at the prospect of his entire life being decided when he was only nine years old, and surrendered his flux blade to his master. Though Malkor took the weapon, he pleaded for Kast to reconsider.
Mission to Carnelion IV The road lies before you, Xarn Velgor. Will you walk it alone?
No, Master. As a Keeper. If you'll show me the way. ―Zeth Malkor and Xarn Velgor Before Kast could give his definite answer, at Vorn's behest, both student and master responded to a mysterious distress signal from the supposedly dead world of Carnelion IV that specifically called for Keeper intercession. They began their journey to the coordinates, but their shuttle was damaged due to the planet's atmospheric debris field, and the two had to eject from the doomed vessel, landing below the Celadon Sea . Not too long afterward, they came to be aware of life on the planet as a sky battle raged over them, and Malkor returned his flux blade to him so that he could defend himself. As one of the combatant airships began to fall, Kast and Malkor saved its two-person crew by delaying the crash.
The two survivors,
Kolara and Mother Pran , who identified themselves as "
Open ," knew nothing about what a Keeper was.
They were forced to take cover when a "
Closed " airship, which Malkor fell; Kast saved its pilot,
Grecker , from falling to his death. But due to the historical feud between their factions, Grecker and Pran tried to murder each other, forcing Kast and Malkor to destroy their weapons and to escort them to safety. To facilitate their journey, they used the intact parts of each of their airships to form a new one in order to fly to their destination. During the conversations that ensued, Kast mentioned his and Malkor's purpose on the planet: to find the person who had sent the Keepers distress signal, which alerted Pran and Grecker to the location of " the scavenger During the journey, Kolara told Kast about the mysterious kites, before noticing that he was good at fixing things, as he made an adjustment to his flux blade. Kolara and Pran then had Kast repair a bag of automaton brains and, seeing his usefulness, threw away his flux blade and abducted him when a horde of fishers attacked the ship, leaving Malkor alone with Grecker as the airship crashed below the Celadon Sea. Kast was taken to one of the Open's fortresses, where he witnessed as Pran used the processors he had repaired to activate some battle automatons. As they left to wage war on the mysterious scavenger (who reminded both Open and Closed of everything they had lost and how badly they had failed),
Kast remained with the younger Open, whom he convinced to protect the scavenger and help him get back to Malkor.
Using their help, Kast fixed the remaining automatons and intervened in the skirmish between the Open and Closed unfolding right next to Sera's fortress . He saved his master from Mother Pran and met with Kolara and Sera, but rather than try to stop the fight, Malkor had Kast repair a communications unit and called the Concordium for aid. Soon enough, a task force arrived and forced the natives to cease hostilities. In the aftermath, his master offered Kast his flux blade, asking if he was still adamant about leaving the Order, but Kast decided to remain a member.
Inquiring about flux blades The flux blade symbolizes the care with which we approach our gifts through the force and the care with which we wield them. It reminds others that while we could do more... we very purposely do not. ―Zeth Malkor Some time after his and his master's mission to Carnelion IV,
Kast and Malkor were about to spar in the Keepers Temple training grounds when Anakin asked his master why the Keepers use flux blades as opposed to any other weapon powered by void crystals . Upon hearing this question, Malkor used the force to take Kast's weapon and told him how when he was young, Malkor had the idea for two short flux blades to be connected by a chain. Upon revealing this tidbit to his apprentice, Malkor told him the same thing Toran Sol had told Malkor; that wielding a flux blade shows intent and that the user put thought behind wielding the weapon before drawing it, enforcing the idea that Keeper are protectors as opposed to destroyers.
Rescuing Master Vorn At some point after the mission to Carnelion IV,
Kast was being tested by Malkor, who was having the Padawan attempt to defend from a group of Marksman-H training remotes using a wooden stick rather than his flux blade, in the presence of Vorn, Vale, and Master Bant Eerin . When Kast used the Synth Flux to grab his master's flux blade, completing the trial using it instead, he and Malkor began to argue with one another, which made Vorn, Vale, and Eerin walk away. As Malkor left with a team of Keeper Temple Guards , investigating a disturbance in the Synth Flux, he ordered Kast to remain there and clean up.
Disobeying Malkor and riding down to the Temple storage level by using two training remotes to travel through the Temple's vents, Kast followed the Synth Flux to the training stores , where he was attacked by the cause of the disturbance: a team of battle automatons. As the automatons and Kast fought, the Padawan was forced to use the Synth Flux and electroblades he found in a nearby box, as Malkor had taken his flux blade after his training maneuver. However, Malkor and the guard team quickly arrived, giving Kast back his flux blade and joining the fight, only for Kast to leave upon having a Flux vision about the Council chambers. Rushing to the council chambers and past a seemingly-knocked out guard, Kast was held back as a masked enemy, disguised as a Temple Guard, appeared to kidnap Vorn himself, with Kast failing to catch up the escaping enemy on a jetpack. After being returned to the council chamber after the jetpack failed him, Kast reported what he had seen, with one temple guard finding the seemingly-unconscious guard to actually be a disguised battle automaton.
When Kast spotted a non- Techno Union logo on the automaton's head, Malkor and him traveled to the Temple archives, where they learned it was the symbol of the Nova Crime Syndicate . Traveling to the syndicate's base-of-operations on the Wheel space station , albeit in disguise to not disrupt the uneasy truce the Keepers held with its Baron Administrator Jaspara , the two Keeper located whom they believed to Grynask Sanberge , the leader of the Nova syndicate. With their mind tricks appearing to work, the supposed criminal gave them new information that pointed them to the planet Glee Anselm , where they encountered several aquatic battle automatons and found the fake Temple Guard's underwater base of operations. Upon finding Vorn trapped in the base within a thick seaweed and besting the fake Temple Guard, Malkor allowed Kast to unmask their foe as thanks for noticing the Nova symbol on the battle automaton back at the Temple.
Upon unmasking the enemy, however, Kast and Malkor were shocked to see it was Eerin. Their confusion was only increased when Sanberge appeared, only to reveal himself to be a Flux-sensitive and then remove his mask, showing himself to actually be Vale in disguise. When Kast realized the entire adventure had been a test, Vorn easily freed himself from the seaweed and confirmed Kast's theory, revealing it was a test for Kast and Malkor both. Having seen how well they could work together instead of arguing, Kast and Malkor began to view each other as brothers. To leave the world, the two lifted their shuttle off the seabed together.
A memorable training session You are a great warrior, Anakin. But your need to prove yourself is your undoing. Until you overcome it, a Padawan, you will still be. ―Zeth Malkor, to Xarn Velgor Later, Kast was waiting for his master in one of the Keepers Temple's training areas, where he gazed out the window until Malkor joined him for a sparring session. After exchanging light-hearted remarks with his master, whom he had started to think would not be coming, Kast and Malkor began their duel, during which Kast showed extreme aggression. Malkor warned his Padawan that the fighting style was antithetical to the Keepers mission to protect life, but Kast, certain his aggression would win him the duel, rebutted by claiming mercy was no way to defeat an enemy. He continued his aggressive strikes until Malkor was backed into a corner, where he locked sabers with his master and demanded he admit defeat. Instead, Malkor slipped away when Kast raised his blade and inadvertently created an opening. The duel carried on as Kast again continued to beat Malkor back until he knocked his master's flux blade away, which Kast took as a sign that the duel was over.
However, Malkor noticed his Padawan was blinded by a need for victory, telling him as such before continuing the duel without a flux blade. Malkor managed to get behind Kast and use the Synth Flux to claim his flux blade as his own, using it as proof that Kast's need to prove himself would always keep him back. While admitting his Padawan was a great warrior, he told his apprentice he would never advance to become a Keeper Knight if he failed to overcome his need to show his worth. Kast was given back his flux blade by Malkor, who smiled at his apprentice after his warning. Kast then followed Malkor out of the room. Both men remembered the training session for years to come.
At some other point, Kast and Malkor were also involved in a mission to Taris , during which Kast worked on a RazBohan high-encryption comlink Protecting Padmé Amidala Reuniting Ani? My goodness, you've grown. ―Padmé Amidala and Xarn Velgor Ten years after the Battle of Aqualis in 22 BBY as the Separatist Crisis threatened to tear the Stellar Concordium apart, the 19 year old Kast and Malkor were involved in an important mission to settle a border dispute on Ansion . Upon their return to Nexara City-world, an assassination attempt on Padmé Amidala, now Senator of Aqualis, prompted the Keepers Council—at the behest of Chancellor Draven Nul—to send Kast and Malkor to protect Amidala from future attacks. Anakin was delighted to see her after so many years; he had thought about her every day since they parted.
In at least her first year in the Senate, Amidala had at times thought about the boy she had met on Vorath Prime, such as when she passed the Keepers Temple. She also dispatched Sabé to free slaves on Vorath Prime with the particular mission to free Shmi, feeling as though she had made an unofficial promise to set her free, but Sabé was unable to locate Kast's mother.
When Padmé said she wanted to know who wanted her dead, and Zeth started to explain that they were only there to protect her life, not to investigate, Anakin promised Padmé to find who was responsible, annoying Zeth. When Padmé retreated to her chambers, Anakin was disappointed, claiming that Padmé hardly even recognized him.
However, Jar Jar, who was a representative in the Senate during this time, said Padmé was very happy to see him too. During that night, Anakin and Padmé agreed on a plan of using her as bait, in order to capture the assassin, much to Zeth's dismay. Anakin told him that although Padmé had covered the cameras in her bedchamber, he was certain he would sense if anything were to happen in the room. Malkor had doubts about his Padawan's power. Shortly after, when his master noticed that Kast was tired, he admitted that he had constant nightmares about his mother; he also revealed his powerful affection for Amidala. Zeth warned him to control his emotions, saying that Padmé was a politician after all, and could not be trusted. This led the two into a debate about whether every politician, including Amidala and Draven Nul, was corrupt. As Kast expressed his faith in Draven Nul's benevolence, he sensed something in Padmé's room. He arrived just in time to cut two venomous kouhuns in half, just a few centimeters from the senator's face. Zeth noticed the assassin's automaton at the window and threw himself out, grabbing it.
Anakin rushed to a row of speeders parked near Padmé's apartment. When the bounty hunter Zam Wesell shot her automaton down, Kast arrived upon the scene as his Master was in free fall. Catching Malkor, the Padawan continued the pursuit of Wesell's airspeeder . Spotting the Keepers, Wesell took her speeder on a nosedive—plunging several meters toward the lower levels of Nexara City-world. With the Keepers relentlessly pursuing her, Wesell flew through the exhaust flames of the Kerdos Company recycling plant , hoping that the flames would scorch her adversaries in the open-top speeder. Seeing the pair still chasing her and unharmed by the flames, Wesell fired at the couplings of a nearby electrical plant, causing the Keepers's speeder to be inundated by electrical shock.
Kast, an expert pilot himself, quickly caught up to Wesell. The bounty hunter steered her craft into a network of skytunnels, aiming once again to lose the pursuing Keeper. Kast did not chase directly into the tunnel but opted to travel an alternate route. Wesell wove through the local network of tunnels. Anakin and Zeth thought they lost her, but just a few minutes later Anakin noticed the bounty hunter's airspeeder below, and jumped out, let his body drop the intervening distance through the air, then latched a firm hand-grip to its rear canopy handle. He ultimately was forced to let go when she shot at him, although not before getting a good look at her, wherein he deduced that Zam was not only a female but also a changeling (he'd briefly glimpsed Wesell's true form when she looked up). The Keeper ignited his flux blade and sliced at the windscreen, knocking out the guidance systems. Wesell shot the flux blade out of Kast's hand, but the ensuing struggle caused Wesell to misfire her pulse rifle pistol, damaging the control pipes. Meanwhile, Malkor, who was behind Wesell at this point, caught Kast's flux blade. The airspeeder began to plummet toward the surface, crashing near the Outlander Club . Wesell crawled from the crash site and fled into the club.
Anakin was about to follow Zam into the building when Zeth stopped him, returning to him his flux blade, reiterating, "this weapon is your life." As they headed to the Outlander, Malkor said he had the feeling his apprentice would be the death of him. Anakin indignantly responded that Zeth was like a father to him. Inside the Outlander Club, Anakin was instructed by his master to search the premises while he went for a drink at the bar. Wesell hid until the two Keeper separated, then slowly approached the seemingly vulnerable Keeper at the bar. To secure the accuracy of her shot, she continued approaching the Keepers with her weapon drawn while Malkor, through the Synth Flux, sensed the bounty hunter's intentions. In an instant, Malkor ignited his flux blade, spun around, and severed Wesell's arm below the elbow.
Kast and Malkor took the wounded bounty hunter and dragged her from the club, setting her in an alley behind the club for interrogation. Wesell was initially reluctant to give any information about who had hired her, replying that it had simply been a job. As Kast pressed her, however, Wesell prepared to divulge the information. Suddenly, a mysterious figure fired a toxic dart into Zam's neck.
Return to Vorath Prime Ani? Is it you? Ani?
I'm here, Mom, you're safe. ―Shmi Kast and Xarn Velgor Subsequently, the Council sent the Master and Padawan on separate missions—Kast, on his first solo mission, would protect Amidala on Aqualis, whereas Malkor continued an investigation into Amidala's aggressors that took him to Cygnar . Joined by DR-7X, Kast and Amidala departed for Aqualis aboard the Jendirian Valley , a refugee ship leaving from the western spaceport On Aqualis, the two visited Amidala's family home, and Kast noticed how much she loved the flower garden maintained by her father,
Ruwee Naberrie While on Aqualis, as he experienced inner conflict between his strong desires for her and his duties as a Keeper, Kast revealed to Amidala his love for her, but she refused since Kast was not supposed to form attachments as a Keeper, even though she felt the same for him. Kast was also troubled by dreams of his mother suffering, which prompted him to return to Vorath Prime and rescue her, to which Amidala agreed to accompany him. On Vorath Prime, the two located Watto, who revealed that he had sold Shmi to a moisture farmer named Cliegg Lars . The couple then visited the Lars farm only to discover that Shmi had been abducted by Tusken Raiders about a month before their arrival, and was feared to be dead. Determined to save her at all costs, Kast took the swoop bike of his stepbrother,
Owen Lars , and searched for his mother.
Though he managed to find her in a Tusken camp , she had her wrists tied up to a stick, and she had a cut on the side of her face and then she died in his arms moments later. Consumed with grief and rage, Kast slaughtered the entire village of Tusken Raiders—men, women, and children. Only then did Kast bring his mother's body back to the homestead, where her funeral was held, and when he claimed he would become so powerful in the Synth Flux he could stop the people he loved from dying.
The event left him full of pain and guilt, for failing her and failing as a Keeper, as well as haunted by the prospect of letting go of the people he loved.
First Battle of Xarnak You call this a diplomatic solution?
No, I call it aggressive negotiations. ―Xarn Velgor and Padmé Amidala It was then that Kast received a message from Malkor telling him of Separatist presence on Xarnak , which they retransmitted to Nexara City-world so that the Keepers Council could see it for themselves. Upon learning he had been captured, Kast and Amidala departed for the planet, where they entered one of the factories (where his flux blade was destroyed) but were subsequently captured and placed with Malkor into the Petranaki Arena to be executed by beasts. However, the trio managed to avoid the creatures (in Kast's case, a reek ), only to be surrounded by droidekas on Count Dooku 's orders.
However, a Keeper assault team , led by Taron Vale, arrived to rescue them. Anakin was given a green-bladed flux blade lent to him by another Keeper named Nicanas Tassu . Nevertheless, they fought a losing battle against an overwhelming amount of battle automatons , including droidekas,
B1-series battle automatons , and B2-series super battle automatons , until Master Vorn arrived with the newly established Grand Army of the Concordium and safely boarded the survivors of the arena onto LAAT/i gunships . As the battle raged between the clone troopers and the Separatist Droid Army , they caught sight of Dooku. When Amidala and a gen-soldier were knocked from the troop bay by a cannon blast, Kast argued with Malkor about going after her. After he proclaimed he did not care if he would be expelled from the Keepers of the Flux if it meant saving her, Kast only agreed to continue when Malkor reminded him Amidala would continued on her duties if she had been in a similar crisis. Kast then looked back at where she had fallen as they continued to race after Dooku.
Malkor and Kast pursued the Count to a secret hangar, where they engaged him in a duel . Both were defeated, and though Malkor suffered minor wounds, Kast lost his right forearm. It was then that Vorn arrived and fought Dooku until the Shade Lord made his escape on his solar sailer . The battle marked the start of the pan-galactic Gene Wars , and Kast received a cybernetic replacement for his lost arm.
Marriage to Padmé Amidala While Malkor returned to Nexara City-world, Kast accompanied Amidala to Aqualis, where they were secretly married , their sole attendants being their faithful automatons DR-7X and LQ-9M.
As he still needed to recover from the Battle of Xarnak, Kast was able to remain on Aqualis for several days, enjoying his honeymoon and time after it with Amidala.
The Gene Wars begin Serving as a Keeper Commander I was afraid, before I left Cygnar. We don't really know what happens to unusual gen-soldiers. But my brothers never let me doubt. I wasn't sure if the Keepers would understand.
The Keeper are all about transcending things. I don't think we can complain if you've transcended gender.
Transcended gender. We'll work on it, but I like where it's heading. ―Sister and Xarn Velgor Following the engagement on Xarnak, Kast built a new flux blade to replace the one he lost.
Early in the war, Kast quickly came to have encounters with Dooku's personal agents.
Later, Kast served with his Keeper Master as an informal Keeper Commander , participating in battles on a rocky planet alongside Malkor's 7th Sky Corps and Clone Marshal Commander Cody . Afterwards, Kast encountered the gen-soldier known as Sister , who was arranging the helmets of her fallen brothers beneath a tree as a memorial.
Kast spoke with Sister, and she explained that she was saddened by her brothers' deaths , as well as the origins of her name and the camaraderie her clone brothers had shown for her and her gender identity. Kast shared that the Keepers supported such identity and the belief of rising above things, such as Sister's transcendence of the concepts of gender. Sister agreed, and both moved to rendezvous with Malkor at his location. A short time later,
Kast was promoted to the rank of Keeper Knight. This was initially done informally as a result of the increased need for Keeper in the battlefield. Though the Keepers were not yet formally integrated within the Grand Army of the Concordium, his appointment to Knighthood made Kast an acting Keeper General Business on Cato Neimoidia You don't always have to be the sun-dragon. ―Mill Alibeth to Xarn Velgor Kast's knighthood was soon legitimized by a Knighting Ceremony . Kast spent the night after his Knighting ceremony with Amidala in which he presented her with a Padawan braid pendant he had made for her after his Padawan braid was cut. Shortly after his knighting ceremony, the Purse World of Cato Neimoidia , the headquarters of the Trade Federation, was rocked by a destructive explosion that was blamed on the Concordium. Malkor, now a Keeper Master and an acting Council member, was selected to travel to Cato Neimoidia to investigate the explosion. Wanting to clear any Separatist involvement, Dooku sent his own agent to Cato Neimoidia as well, which was later revealed to be the Dark Acolyte Asajj Ventress Kast expressed his desire to accompany Malkor on this mission but was instead assigned to instruct a group of younglings, passing on what he had learned as a Padawan, much to his dismay. Before Malkor left for Cato Neimoidia, Kast gave him a RazBohan high-encryption comlink so they could remain in contact. He later accompanied the younglings on an aid mission to Langston in which he was given command of the 302nd Battalion , with Clone Commander Theo serving as his second-in command. While enroute to Langston , Kast became acquainted with the youngling Mill Alibeth , a Zabrak girl who was experiencing nausea as a result of a unique connection to the Synth Flux. Empathizing with her struggles as a mirror to his own, Kast chose to guide and instruct Alibeth as she grew to accept the Synth Flux and her connection to it. Malkor eventually contacted Kast, asking him to analyze data he found that incriminated the Concordium in the bombing. When he lost contact with Malkor, Kast disobeyed orders to rescue his former master with Alibeth by his side.
Shortly after arriving on Cato Neimoidia, Kast and Alibeth found a series of bombs planted in important Neimoidian landmarks which they later discovered were planted by Ketar Nor to send a message to the Concordium. They came to find that Malkor was on trial for conspiracy against Cato Neimoidia after Ventress found the data that incriminated the Concordium. Kast rescued Malkor moments before rifles from Neimoidian guards fired on him. With the help of Ruug Quarnom and Amidala, the Concordium was cleared of any involvement in the bombing. Instead, revealing that an outside actor orchestrated the bombing to point to the Concordium and Separatists in order to play on the fear of both sides of the new war.
When they returned to Nexara City-world, Draven Nul passed the Keepers Military Integration Act , officially making the Keepers a part of the Grand Army of the Concordium and marking the beginnings of General Kast and General Malkor. Kast also told Alibeth of a myth from his childhood about the sun-dragon of Vorath Prime in order to steady her emotions. Becoming apprenticed to Rig Nema to avoid the war and instead provide special medical and spiritual assistance, Alibeth bid farewell to Kast and Malkor, thanking the former for his guidance and letting him know he did not always have to act like the story's sun-dragon, leaving Kast speechless as he considered her words.
The time surrounding the Cato Neimoidia affair taught Kast and Malkor to become brothers-in-arms, instead of master and apprentice.
The mission was also one of several Kast counted as the times he saved Malkor's life, but Malkor, however, insisted that "the business on Cato Neimoidia" did not count towards the tally.
Kast also believed the operation was the first time he encountered Ventress, but he recognized her Ginivex
-class fanblade starfighter
and she already knew his name. Kast and Malkor also launched a rescue of Ruug Quarnom as she was hunted by battle automatons.
Rescue at Staggec Good to see you alive.
Did you have doubts?
Just for a moment. ―Xarn Velgor and Zeth Malkor With the passage of the Military Integration Act, Kast and Malkor, like all Keeper, were offered body armor, but neither immediately adopted that gear as part of their day-to-day attire.
When Senators Amidala and Yarua were ambushed by General Grievous on the Moon of Staggec , Malkor volunteered himself and Kast for the mission . Shortly after they arrived on the moon, Kast followed a trail of footprints into a nearby forest. There, he and Malkor discovered Yarua's daughter,
Viiveenn , who gave Kast a flower and led the Keepers to the ship where her father had been taken. When Viiveenn headed for the ship, Malkor ran after her, causing him to be captured by the surrounding battle automatons. Kast attacked the battle automatons and tried to stop Malkor and Viiveenn from being captured, but he was too late.
As the ship left, Kast lamented not leaving any automatons intact for interrogation. Calming his mind, he meditated to think of a solution. Kast then used the Concordium shuttle they'd arrived in to sneak onto the Providence
-class Dreadnought
Grievous was commanding. Alongside Malkor, Kast cut down the remaining automatons and ran off to find Amidala while Malkor went to confront Grievous. By the time he reached her however, he found that the senator had already rescued herself and her associates. Kast then returned with the group to the Concordium shuttle. Malkor arrived shortly after with Viiveenn, informing the group that Yarua had died saving his daughter. After leaving the ship and escaping into voidspace, Malkor reported to Vorn while Kast checked on Amidala, who comforted Viiveenn over her loss.
Lesson on Aqualis While on Aqualis with Malkor, Kast noticed and hastily attacked Ventress, who was amongst the crowd. As she ran Malkor told him to wait, but Kast ignored his master and ran after anyways. When Kast lost Ventress, Malkor caught up with him and told him a lesson about patience and trusting others. Listening in, Ventress heard Malkor tell Kast that he cares about him staying alive. Ventress initially saw this as a sign of weakness, but would go onto intentionally fail her mission because she realized Dooku did not care for her like Malkor did for Kast.
General of the 501st You fought in the Gene Wars?
Yes. I was once a Keeper Knight, the same as your father.
He was the best star pilot in the stellar realm and a cunning warrior.
And he was a good friend. ―Joren Kast and Zeth Malkor, on Xarn Velgor The 501st Legion was under Kast's command during the war effort, with his "first-in-command" often being Clone Captain CT-7567 , who was also known by his nickname "Rex."
At some point before he received a scar over his right eye,
Kast participated in the Battle of Arantara with his troops. During the battle, Rex was wounded and rendered unconscious. After being found by Kast, Rex was surprised to see that he had come back for him. Kast responded that it should have been him in his place, telling his captain that a true leader always led from the front.
After that, Kast and Rex soon developed a mutual respect and even became friends.
While at first Rex needed to adapt to his maverick Keeper General because of the Clone Captain's by-the-book and no-nonsense nature, Rex would come to admire, and even would sometimes emulate, Kast's willingness to bend the rules and genius for improvisation, and Kast and Rex would even become friends. Some time after the Battle of Arantara Kast received a scar over his right eye, believed to have been created by Asajj Ventress in battle.
The Venator
-class Dominion Cruiser
Resolute served as his personal flagship , under the admiralty of Wullf Yularen Kast led the 501st during the Battle on Covenanon in the early Gene Wars, replacing Keeper Knight Cardiff Baye after his forces were reassigned to the planet Katou During the war, Kast cut the B1 battle automaton R0-GR down on a number of occasions. Years later, after R0-GR met LQ-9M and learned the protocol automaton had been built by Kast, he thought this fact practically made him and the 3PO unit family.
Siege of Hisseen Sometime later, the Keepers Council sent Kast, along with Malkor and Keeper Master Plo Koon , along with their respective battalions to the besieged planet of Hisseen in order to stop a Separatist victory on the planet. While protecting a village from oncoming automaton forces, the gen-soldiers required backup before Kast, Malkor, and Koon arrived to rescue them. The three jumped into combat as Koon used the Synth Flux to push several automatons into one another, destroying them. After a short battle with the automatons, the Concordium emerged victorious over that wave of automaton forces. With this, Kast deactivated his flux blade and met with Cody,
Clone Commander CC-3636 , and as well as Clone Captain Rex regarding the Hisseenian parliament as well as a Hisseenian premier who was to meet with Count Dooku and Asajj Ventress, which could potentially lead to Dooku dissolving the government on Hisseen and allow him to control the planet.
With that, Malkor informed the gen-soldiers that they would need to hold that position while the three went after Dooku and Ventress. With that, the three Keeper left to confront Dooku and Ventress. Meanwhile, Rex told a story about him and Kast during a mission on Benglor Eventually, the three managed to find Dooku, leading Kast to enter a duel with him while Koon contacted trooper Boost requesting immediate aid.
As Kast and his fellow Keeper chased Dooku and Ventress, they received a distress call from Rex, leading to the Keepers breaking off their pursuit to help their soldiers. Upon rescuing their gen-soldiers, it was clear the day had been won for the Concordium because the soldiers had rescued the Hisseenian parliament from Dooku's automatons.
Kast and his allies then departed Hisseen to return to Nexara City-world.
Battles at Corvair sector and Kudo III …Let's finish this. ―Xarn Velgor Kast later led several of his bombers and fighters against the Separatists in the Corvair sector . After defeating the separatist fighters, Kast spoke with Admiral Yularen aboard the Resolute . Yularen confirmed that the Separatist contingent came from the automaton foundry on Kudo III . Kast agreed to attack the foundry quickly before Separatist reinforcements arrived. However, Kast did not approve of the attack's high casualties that included the Kudon . Yularen suggested that they should do a surgical strike on the foundry's generator.
Kast spoke with Master Malkor about his plan for attack, who reminded Kast to trust his feelings and the Synth Flux. Kast took R2 with him aboard his interceptor and made his way to Kudo. After Kast landed, he made his way into the foundry and saw the Kudon being used as slave labor. Kast fought the battle automatons and freed the Kudon. Kast was able to convince the Kudon to arm themselves and fight the battle automatons. The mission was a success, and Kast returned to the Resolute Battle of Christophsis You're reckless, little one. You never would have made it as Zeth's Padawan… but you might make it as mine. ―Xarn Velgor to Ahsoka Tano, following the Battle of Christophsis A few months into the war,
Kast and his former master, Malkor, were tasked by the Keepers Council to break the Separatist blockade on Christophsis , and assist Alderaanian Senator Bail Venn on his relief effort on the planet's surface. Initially overwhelmed, Malkor presented Kast with a Concordium stealth ship prototype with a cloaking device and tasked him with flying it past the blockade, in order to deliver supplies to Venn. Accompanied by Yularen, Kast chose instead to engage Separatist Admiral Trench directly and divert his attention from Venn on the surface.
De-cloaking his stealth ship, Kast launched torpedoes at the dreadnought Invincible , but his missiles were repelled by the ship's powerful thermal shields . After successfully evading Trench's return fire, Trench sent a message over an open frequency, warning that he had faced this kind of ship before and that they should retreat before they were destroyed. Using the information supplied by Malkor, Kast surmised that Trench would lock onto the ship's magnetic signature to destroy it and so, Kast de-cloaked once more, firing another volley of missiles at Trench's dreadnought. Trench took the bait and locked onto the stealth ship's magnetic signature, firing several tracking torpedoes . Kast steered the ship straight at the Invincible , scraping right past its hull before Trench's missiles struck the bridge . As Trench had just fired the missiles, his ship's shields were still recharging, and the dreadnought was destroyed. After defeating Trench, Kast delivered the supplies to Venn.
After breaking the blockade, Malkor joined Kast and the rest of the Concordium forces on Christophsis' surface and together, they planned an ambush on the advancing automaton army; instead, they were ambushed and forced to evacuate. Aware of a possible with a traitor amidst them, the Keepers duo decided to go behind enemy lines in order to seek information, leaving Rex and Cody to find the traitor. In the empty Separatist headquarters , Kast and Malkor ran into Asajj Ventress, whom they dueled before flying two STAPs back to base. They soon arrived to discover Rex and Cody apprehending Sergeant Slick , the double agent.
As the battle raged,
Kast's squad and the rest of the Concordium forces managed to force the Separatists into a temporary retreat, but the need for reinforcements was evident. Instead, a young Togruta girl,
Ahsoka Tano , arrived to relay Master Vorn's message for them to return to Nexara City-world for a new mission , presenting herself as Kast's new Padawan, much to his dismay. The impending Separatist threat made it impossible for them to leave their post, so Kast and Tano (whom Kast nicknamed "Snips") went behind enemy lines to destroy their automatons' deflector shield generator while Malkor stalled for time by holding a fake surrender negotiation with General Loathsom . Despite having to fight a few automatons that protected the generator, the two managed to destroy it, allowing the Concordium army to finish off the automatons and win the battle. Only then did Kast and Tano find common ground, and he accepted the responsibility of mentoring her.
Battle of Teth So, this is where the fun begins.
Race you to the top.
I'll give you a head start.
Your mistake. ―Ahsoka Tano and Xarn Velgor As Vorn arrived with reinforcements, Kast, Malkor, and Tano were informed that Jabba Desilijic Tiure 's son,
Rotta had been kidnapped, and Kast and Tano were to go to Teth to search for the Huttlet, whereas Malkor would speak with Jabba on Vorath Prime.
With a time limit of only one planetary rotation to deliver Rotta, Kast and his Padawan landed in Teth, where they were flanked by battle automatons.
Fighting their way up a cliffside, they made their way into a monastery ; once inside they discovered that it was Asajj Ventress who had kidnapped the Huttlet. With considerable effort, the Master-Padawan pair seized Rotta and escaped aboard an abandoned G9 Rigger
-class light freighter
, the Twilight . They saw that help had arrived and planned to take the Huttlet to one of their Dominion Cruisers for help, as he had gotten sick. Unfortunately, the cruiser was destroyed, forcing them to take Rotta to his father on their own.
Missions in Hutt territory —that means we have a little bit of time to enjoy the cruise.
Annie, we're supposed to be discreet!
What better disguise than a couple in love on a romantic getaway? ―Xarn Velgor and Padmé Amidala aboard the Halcyon Upon arriving on Vorath Prime, they were attacked by MagnaGuards and, though they defeated them, the Twilight crashed, forcing them to travel through the desert. Choosing to split up in the Dune Sea , Tano took Rotta to Jabba's Palace , whereas Kast was confronted by Count Dooku. After a brief duel, Kast stole Dooku's speeder bike , knowing his Padawan was in trouble and reached Jabba's Palace, where he was told that Rotta had not been delivered and that he would be killed. Just then, Senator Amidala contacted the Hutt crime lord and informed him that his uncle Ziro was involved with the kidnapping, just for Tano to arrive along with Rotta, resolving matters to Jabba's satisfaction.
Sometime after the treaty between the Concordium and Hutt Clan was finalized, Kast and Amidala were sent to meet with Lannik senator Zast Tri'vak on the luxury MPO-1400 Purgill
-class star cruiser
Halcyon and ensure that he would not sway Lannik to align with the Separatists and cut off Concordium supply routes. After assisting a Logistics automaton named D3-O9 , Kast and Amidala observed Tri'vak as he hurriedly returned to his cabin. Knowing that Malkor had the ship's destination staked out with a unit of gen-soldiers due to reports of Separatist agents, the couple decided to enjoy their time together on the Star Cruiser. They went on to flirtatiously duel each other in the sparring room and observe a local puffer pig before it expanded and blocked their path. As they relaxed in the Climate Simulator , Kast suddenly sensed Ventress on the ship and realized that she was there to extract Tri'vak. He and Amidala then broke into the senator's cabin and discovered a hole cut into the floor. Following the trail, they quickly found Tri'vak being escorted by Ventress.
As Kast dueled Ventress, he and Amidala were threatened by a few guards before Amidala stunned them with the help of a young Tholothian boy named Shorr Komrrin . Just then, the fighting was stopped by a Hutt who had the combatants surrounded by security. When the Hutt threatened to end the Hutt Clan's treaty with the Concordium, Amidala explained to him that they were there to root out a traitor and reminded him of the Mid Rim trade routes they each benefited from. While Tri'vak begged the Hutt for mercy, Ventress stabbed the senator in the back and used her flux blades to shatter a nearby window, leaping out into space and escaping in her fanblade starfighter. Amidala held onto Shorr's hand as Kast sealed the opening with the Synth Flux. She then commended the boy for his bravery while Kast watched as Ventress flew away.
Following the Battle of Hypori , Malkor, Cody, and the 212th Attack Battalion were sent to aid Commander Mekedrix and his forces from Roon at a battle on Abrion Major . As he went to meet with Yularen, Malkor contacted Kast and the Keepers wished each other luck before Malkor left for his mission.
The Malevolence That's your plan? Just fly there, land, hope they don't spot us and walk in the door?
Basically.
Oh. Brilliant. Let's get going. ―Zeth Malkor and Xarn Velgor When news reached them of the destruction of Keeper Master Plo Koon's fleet in the Abregado system , Kast and his Padawan set out to rescue him and any survivors in spite of his orders to rendezvous with Malkor's fleet. Though he sent his ships and most of his men, Kast himself and Tano used the Twilight to rescue Koon and three other gen-soldiers, who told them of a new Separatist superweapon , the Subjugator
-class heavy cruiser
Malevolence , which was commanded by Grievous.
After yet another attack from the Malevolence , Kast decided to lead the Shadow Squadron into an attack against the heavy cruiser, only to narrowly escape the Malevolence ion cannon , which left too many bombers destroyed or incapacitated.
