Forced to change the plan, Kast and the bombers dropped their payload on the ion cannon, forcing it to overload, damaging the ship and causing the Malevolence to retreat from its attack on a Concordium medical station While making his escape, Grievous captured Senator Amidala, who entered the in the belief she was sent to negotiate with the InterGalactic Banking Clan Supreme Executive, rather than a trap. Ordering their ships to halt their fire, Kast and Malkor boarded the Malevolence to rescue her. While on the ship, Kast sabotaged the ship's navigation system to cause the ship to crash into a moon should the hyperdrive be engaged, before escaping the ship alongside Amidala and Malkor.
Following the battle wherein a group of gen-soldiers from Rishi Station prevented a surprise attack on Cygnar, Kast and Malkor awarded medals to two of its survivors, newcomers Echo and Fives , with Kast welcoming them onto the 501st Legion.
At some point, Kast and Malkor crashed their Y-wing into a shield protecting a city near the Senatorial Palace as they investigated the activities of some rogue Nightsisters on Rodia . When the shield was abruptly activated, the starfighter was entirely destroyed, while the dome remained unaffected.
DR-7X lost So let me get this straight, Anakin. You risked the mission, all your men, even your Padawan , to save a automaton?
Drex found the listening post, and he saved our lives. We couldn't just leave him there, Zeth.
Oh, Anakin, one day ―Zeth Malkor and Xarn Velgor Following numerous Separatist victories from General Grievous, and the defeat of the Falleen battle group , Kast attempted to stop the cyborg from taking his and planned an ambush for the Separatist forces as they were passing through Bothawui 's asteroid field . Placing AT-TEs on the nearby asteroids , he managed to destroy the Separatist frigates and forced Grievous to retreat in the Soulless One , resulting in a pursuit by Kast. However, debris from the destroyed Separatist frigates damaged his ship, leaving him to be rescued by Rex at the cost of losing DR-7X. Though he was supplied with a new astromech automaton,
R3-S6 , Kast was saddened by the loss of his friend and did not take to the new automaton, and felt something suspicious on its true allegiance. Instead, he launched a search, scouring the battlefield; unable to find DR-7X, they boarded a nearby scavenger ship, the Vulture's Claw , only to leave empty-handed as Gha Nachkt delivered DR-7X to Grievous.
With all hope lost, Kast attempted to continue life without the automaton, scouting for a possible Separatist listening outpost, until DR-7X sent a message revealing his location at Skytop Station . Upon arrival, Kast ordered Tano and Rex to fulfill the mission objective—to destroy the station—while he went to find his lost automaton. Though Kast engaged several MagnaGuards, he found DR-7X and met up with Rex and Denal , who told him that Tano was gone to distract Grievous alone while they completed the mission. It was then, as Anakin already suspected about R3-S6's true allegiance, that R3 revealed himself to be Grievous' spy and set several vulture automatons and super battle automatons against them. As they resisted, Tano rejoined the group, only to be scolded by her master for engaging the automaton general alone; DR-7X opened the hangar doors, defeating R3-S6 in the process, to be retrieved by a happy Kast, delighted to have his longtime friend back safe.
Following Viceroy Nute Gunray 's capture in Rodia by Amidala, Kast separated from Tano, who went onto serve as Gunray's escort alongside Luminara Unduli . Although the two failed in their task, Kast reunited with Tano at the rendezvous point.
Captured on Florrum You should be more patient, Master. After all, the Count is an elderly gentlemen, and doesn't move like he used to.
I suppose you're right."
I would kill you both right now if I did not have to drag your bodies. ―Kast and Malkor mock Dooku as the three attempt to escape Together with Malkor, Kast spearheaded an attempt near the planet Vanqor to capture Count Dooku, where Kast let himself be captured by Dooku's Munificent
-class star frigate
and taken to a prison cell, only for Malkor to come and rescue him. The two Keeper then gave chase to Dooku, who escaped in his solar sailer , with a Sheathipede
-class transport shuttle
. After taking direct hits to their shuttle, they were forced to crash-land on the planet. In a nearby cave, the two Keeper began to hunt for Dooku, but the Shade Lord ambushed them by causing a sudden collapse in the cave ceiling. While Dooku escaped the cave, they had to render a gundark unconscious, only to nearly succumb to poison gas released from a trapped pocket in the cave. They were saved just in time, as Tano and a detachment of clone troopers rescued them.
Later, as Supreme Chancellor Draven Nul and his representatives were contacted by Hondo Ohnaka to receive a reward for capturing Count Dooku, Kast and Malkor were assigned to verify the pirate gang's custody of Dooku, agreeing to the pirates' condition to arrive unarmed. The Keeper were met at gunpoint in the Twilight and escorted to Ohnaka, who arranged a meeting with their prisoner. The Keeper mocked Dooku's imprisonment, and the Shade Lord warned them not to underestimate the Weequay . Kast and Malkor were invited to a "party" by the pirates, only to wind up passed out in a cell, chained together with Dooku.
Despite several attempts by the Keepers and Shade Covenant to escape on their own,
Representative Binks was the one to rescue them, though the mission came at the cost of Senator Kharrus ' life and Dooku's escape. Obi Wan told Anakin not to harm Hondo and left peacefully.
Later, Kast heard about the situation on Ridlay , where the entire planet stopped communicating for an unusual amount of time. He contacted Vale, who was already there to investigate the situation . He offered his and Tano's assistance, but Vale denied their involvement.
Quell and Maridun This battle is inevitable. You can stand by your beliefs, but let us stand by ours. ―Xarn Velgor, to Tee Watt Kaa Sent in as reinforcements for Keeper Master Aayla Secura 's and Commander Bly 's forces , which were heavily outnumbered and overpowered during the Battle of Quell , Kast, Tano, Rex, and their forces assisted in an evacuation of Secura's cruiser as it fell under heavy Separatist fire. Meeting with Secura, a raging inferno ran through the cruiser, and Kast sacrificed himself to save the others, severely injuring himself in the process. Tano and Secura dragged Kast onto the evacuation frigate, where he was given medical treatment for his injuries. As they prepared to dock with the Resolute , an ambush by several vulture automatons caused the ship's hyperdrive to activate, sending them into deep space. After narrowly avoiding an incoming star , they crash-landed on Maridun , where Kast was left in the care of Rex, while Secura, Tano, and Bly set out to ask the natives for medical support. Rex defended Kast from several vicious mastiff phalones until Tano arrived back with Bly and Lurmen healer Wag Too , before a final confrontation against the vicious attackers.
Soon after their arrival, Separatist forces landed on the planet, and the Lurmen leader Tee Watt Kaa accused the Keepers of bringing the war to their peaceful planet, ordering them to leave immediately. Though they did, the Keepers returned to the Lurmen village after learning about the Separatists' sinister objective—to test their new superweapon, the defoliator , on the Lurmen. The group set up a shield generator to protect the village from the blast of the weapon, as well as from the incoming waves of battle automatons. As their shield was destroyed and the defoliator was being reloaded for another shot, Kast disabled the weapon before capturing Separatist General Lok Durd . The Keeper then left the planet, now peaceful again, on several Concordium cruisers sent by Yularen.
Orto Plutonia …and this is the planet's tropical zone.
It's not Vorath Prime, that's for sure. ―Zeth Malkor and Xarn Velgor, on Orto Plutonia After losing contact with a Concordium outpost on Orto Plutonia , Kast, his automatons LQ-9M and DR-7X, and Malkor were sent to investigate with a squad of cold assault troopers under the command of Captain Rex, in the company of Pantora Senator Riyo Chuchi and Chairman Chi Cho . Kast and Malkor investigated the outpost following the mysterious death of all its clone troopers , and the two Keeper deduced that all the gen-soldiers had been killed, but not by battle automatons since another nearby Separatist base had also been attacked. Kast and Malkor followed their clues to a nearby canyon, where they found a village inhabited by Talz , who explained that they were only trying to protect their home as translated by LQ-9M. Acting as a mediator, Malkor proposed a peace meeting between the Talz and Chairman Cho, but the latter refused to share the moon with the Talz, and declared war, despite the Keepers's efforts to bring forth peace. As a battle broke out amongst the clone troopers and the Talz, Senator Chuchi convened with the Pantoran Assembly and called Cho out of order. The battle came to an end when Cho was killed, and Chuchi gave a proposal to the Talz to share the moon with them.
Blue Shadow Virus You must move cautiously if you— There's no time for caution. My Padawan is trapped on there, and so is Padmé—I mean, Senator Amidala. ―Gregar Typho and Xarn Velgor, much to Malkor's disapproving look Upon Amidala's petition to send Kast and Malkor to assist them in locating suspected Separatist presence on Aqualis, Kast, Malkor, and Tano arrived to find that Amidala and Binks had gone to investigate the eastern swamps . Sending the young Padawan with Peppi Bow to find them, the two Keeper were informed by Captain Gregar Typho that before losing contact with Amidala, she had informed them of the laboratory's location and of Dr.
Nuvo Vindi 's intentions to release the dangerous Blue Shadow Virus back into the stellar realm. While Tano led an attack on the southern entrance, providing a distraction for Malkor and Kast to enter through the hatches undetected, Kast confronted Vindi, who electrified Amidala and Binks to make his escape. After saving the Senator, Kast pursued the scientist , capturing him with the help of Peppi Bow and Malkor on the swamp above.
Prepared to transport Vindi to Theed for his trial, they heard an alarm go off below them and Kast contacted Tano; she told him that Vindi's servant automaton had set off one of the viral bombs , liberating the virus, and that she along with the gen-soldiers were trapped in the laboratory, just like Amidala was. Once in Theed, Captain Typho told the two Keeper that they had found a possible antidote for the virus, a reeksa root, but that it could only be found on the planet of Iego . Despite Typho's warnings that it was suicide , Kast set off alongside Malkor aboard the Twilight Upon arrival, Kast and Malkor were greeted by a group of inoffensive B1-series battle automatons that had been reprogrammed to do the bidding of a local boy,
Jaybo Hood ; he told the Keepers that the planet was protected by a deity known as Drol , who would not let any who arrived on the planet leave again, but also instructed them on how to retrieve the reeksa roots. Though the plant attacked them, they obtained the root and bid farewell to Hood before taking off, only to be forced to turn back after encountering a laser web , the work of the Separatists. On their second attempt, however, they used Hood's reprogrammed vulture automatons, and Malkor managed to shoot the laser generator, liberating the inhabitants of Iego. Kast and Malkor then arrived in time to rescue their friends on Aqualis.
Battle of Ryloth It was a trap, Snips. It was not your fault.
I lost so many of my pilots.
Take heart, little one. That's the reality of command. ―Xarn Velgor and Ahsoka Tano During the Battle of Ryloth , Kast gave Tano command of a squadron to fend off Mar Tuuk 's blockade to make way for Malkor's ground assault, encouraging Tano to overcome her nervousness about leading her first squad. However, during the battle, she disobeyed her master's orders and lost many men, for which Kast reprimanded her when she arrived back at the Resolute , yet understood her good intentions. Kast then reported his losses to Vale and Malkor. He gave her some time to recover but informed her that they needed to proceed with the attack on the blockade, which upset Tano because he was rushing the plan despite their recent losses. Though he told her to go cool off somewhere, Kast had an idea—he evacuated the damaged Defender , which he would pilot by himself into the Ryloth system to distract the Separatists, while he gave Tano full command of the attack operation. By the time Turk realized the ruse, it was too late, for Kast had already escaped in an escape pod and let the Defender collide with Tuuk's control ship; though Tuuk escaped, Tano destroyed the Confederate fleet, and Kast was rescued from his escape pod.
After Malkor and his men were able to take out the proton cannons and allow the transports to land, whereas Vale headed for Lessu with his forces, Kast and his forces secured the space around Ryloth , forcing the remaining Separatist cruisers to flee. He reported this in a holographic conference with Chancellor Draven Nul, Vorn, Admiral Yularen, Senator Orn Free Taa , and Vale. However, Separatist General Wat Tambor soon deployed Hyena
-class Droid Bombers
to destroy Ryloth's villages and Kast engaged them in his interceptor , accompanied by Tano and gen-soldiers. Before Lessu was destroyed on Count Dooku's orders, Kast and Tano shot them down. Subsequently, Tambor was captured, and the Concordium was victorious: Ryloth was finally free once more.
Holocron heist This could be a trap, Master. Are you sure you don't need us to go?
Of course it's a trap, Kast. ―Xarn Velgor and Taron Vale Kast and Clone Commander "
Wolffe " fought alongside each other during the Battle of Khorm , where the Clone Officer lost his right eye to Ventress.
Sometime later, during the First Battle of Felucia , Kast, Tano, and Malkor fought against the Droid Army, but, while Tano was away on a jungle patrol, Kast, Malkor, and their gen-soldiers were surrounded. However, a Concordium fleet commanded by Wolffe was able to break past the Separatist blockade , so Kast and the others could be evacuated by Master Plo Koon. However, Tano refused to retreat, and Malkor and Kast went to retrieve her before her forces were overrun by automatons. While Kast and Tano argued about the retreat, as Tano believed the automatons were retreating, she boarded the gunship in time, and the three Keeper watched as her abandoned vehicles were destroyed. Back at the Keepers Temple, Kast attempted to take the blame for her disobedience, citing that he had given her more freedom due to her skills. Still, the Council relegated her to guard duty in the Keepers Archives to reflect on what she had done.
When Vorn sensed that thieves would arrive at the Temple, Kast, and Malkor inaccurately deduced that their enemies were after their transmission codes and went to secure them, only to be proven wrong when they located the intruders' position at the ventilation shaft. They were misdirected then to the communication center, where they narrowly escaped an exploding Todo , and realized only too late that Cad Bane had stolen a holocron from the Vault . Furthermore, the bounty hunter that was assisting Bane,
Cato Parasitti revealed that Bane's next target was Bolla Ropal , keeper of the Kyber memory crystal , which contained a list of all known Flux-sensitive children in the Concordium.
Kast then volunteered himself and Tano to meet with Ropal, arriving in the Devaron system to cut off the Separatist fleet that was assisting Bane, who already had captured Ropal and tortured him to death in a containment field after he defiantly refused to open the holocron.
Kast, Tano, and Rex boarded Bane's Munificent
-class frigate
and stormed its bridge, where DR-7X accessed the ship's computer to locate Ropal, or rather, Ropal's dead body. After an explosion rocked the ship, the Keepers spotted Bane and chased him into a trap. After a duel with Tano, the bounty hunter took her as a hostage, and Kast was forced to bow to Bane's demands and open the holocron. Though he did so, Kast also pulled his and Tano's flux blades to himself and attacked Bane. However, Bane distracted him by opening the airlock on Tano's cell and escaped, while Kast saved his Padawan and made their way to the hangar, where troopers had stolen a shuttle the escape the exploding Separatist ship back to the Resolute , not before letting Bane enter, disguised in trooper armor After landing on the Resolute , Rex later noticed Duros blood in the shuttle, and pursued Bane, who nevertheless managed to escape in a V-19 starfighter . Kast and Tano returned to the Keepers Council to report their failure.
Kast, along with Malkor, Vale, and Vorn, meditated in hope of finding the children that Bane was after before he kidnapped them. Sensing the future, Kast and Tano were assigned to Aqualis to set a trap for Bane in Jan-gwa city , capturing him as he attempted to kidnap Roo-Roo Page . With the real child safe, the two Keeper took Bane to the Resolute , where Kast, Vale, and Malkor interrogated the bounty hunter with a combined mind trick ; exhausted, Bane gave them coordinates that Malkor and Vale were to follow , while Kast reported the details to Draven Nul on Nexara City-world. Soon, however, he reunited with his Padawan and DR-7X and began to examine Bane's Starfighter, from which Kast and Tano deduced from the ashes on it and its refueling record that Bane had been to Mustafar . With nothing else to do, the Keepers pair left on the Twilight for the planet, where they found the children. Though one of the automatons set the building to be destroyed in the lava on Sidious's orders, Kast and Tano saved the children and returned to Nexara City-world. Kast admitted to the Council that they were unable to find out who was behind the kidnappings, to which Vorn advised caution on the path ahead.
Battle of Felucia That's it. We'll just train the villagers to defend themselves.
But we are just simple farmers. Why are we paying bounty hunters to defend us if we're just going to end up doing it ourselves? Hush.
Trust me. We'll have you ready. ―Xarn Velgor and Dilanni On a mission to investigate the disappearance of Felucia Medical Station HCTFF2 that was orbiting over Felucia , their shuttle was attacked by a group of six vulture automatons deployed from an automated deployment station , forcing them to crash-land on Felucia. Trapped on the planet, they came across a nysillin farm village after searching around for a while. They soon realized that the Felucian villagers were being protected by a group of bounty hunters led by Sugi Embo Rumi Paramita , and Seripas . The village elder,
Casiss Midagatis explained to the Keepers trio that they had hired the bounty hunters because pirates had been taking their crops. After some debate amongst them, Kast, Malkor, and Tano decided to help the farmers to defend themselves.
That night they were visited by the pirates, Hondo Onaka's gang, familiar to Kast and Malkor. Ohnaka then told the group that he would be back, but by then Kast had taught the Felucians how to fight with staffs—though slow at first, they eventually picked up the craft. After a few days, the Ohnaka Gang returned as promised, and a skirmish ensued between the factions. During the confrontation, Kast dueled Ohnaka and his electrostaff , leaving him hanging off a cliff and begging for mercy, to which the Keepers Knight pulled him up and Ohnaka realized that their efforts were no longer profitable, leaving on his ship . Alongside the bounty hunters, the Keepers themselves left soon after as well, to reach the closest Concordium outpost.
The Zillo Beast Well, you made the right choice. We got the fuel and you saved your creature. And now, he will be safe.
Let's hope we could say the same about Nexara City-world. ―Xarn Velgor and Taron Vale During the lengthy campaign on Malastare , Kast discussed with Draven Nul, who contacted them via hologram, and Vale the effects of the new electro-proton bomb , just as the Separatist Droid Army prepared a new attack—with the Concordium expecting to successfully defend the Dugs and secure a treaty for their fuel . Though exceedingly effective, the bomb created a crater, which Vale went to investigate whereas Kast was to ensure the treaty with Doge Urus , only to be called by Vale to assist him with the scouting. The two soon discovered a gigantic creature whose armored skin was impervious even to flux blades, the Zillo Beast , from which they fled, only to be told by Urus that they wanted it killed, even though it was previously thought to be extinct. With the use of RX-200 Falchion
-class assault tanks
, the Concordium forces put the creature to sleep , convincing the Dugs that it was dead. With the treaty signed and Malastare now a member of the Concordium, Draven Nul ordered that the beast be shipped to Nexara City-world for study, despite Vale's objections.
After the Zillo Beast was brought to the Concordium capital, Malkor and Amidala asked Kast to speak with Draven Nul about the fate of the creature; reluctantly, he agreed. When both he and Amidala met with the Chancellor, Draven Nul and the Vice Chair Mas Amedda tried to justify that killing the beast was for the greater good—Amidala objected to their claims, but Kast chose to stay neutral, instead of opposing either his wife, Amidala, or his friend, Draven Nul. Although Draven Nul claimed that he wished to kill the beast, he in fact wanted to research it as he was intrigued by the durability of the Zillo beast's armor. However, the beast escaped from captivity in Dr.
Boll's laboratory and directly targeted the Chancellor himself for his attempts to kill it. Cornered by the beast, Kast and the others evacuated his office on his executive shuttle , only for the beast to grab it upon take-off. They were freed when Kast sliced the shuttle in half, causing everyone to fall out of the shuttle, with Kast saving Amidala from falling from the Senate Building's roof. The Beast was ultimately killed when gunships fired gas at its mouth on Draven Nul's orders.
No end in sight Another mission to Cato Neimoidia Wait. Now give me back my disk.
Don't worry, Clovis. It's good to know you have a heart. I wish I could give you the disk, but I have a lot of faith in your survival skills. ―Rush Clovis and Xarn Velgor Even as the Gene Wars continued with no end in sight,
Kast was allowed a leave after a long tour of duty. After returning home to his wife's apartment for a romantic evening, Kast was recalled to the Temple by the Keepers Council. Believing Senator Rush Clovis to be a Separatist supporter, they decided they needed a spy—in this case, Senator Amidala, of whom Clovis was an old friend. The next day, Kast met Amidala in the Senate and asked her about Clovis, who initially refused to do it until she heard that Clovis could be a Separatist. Kast accompanied her to the Keepers Council, where the Masters encouraged her to find out about his possible treachery. After learning that he was planning to go to Cato Neimoidia, Kast dressed as the pilot of Padmé's starship and took them there, all the while frustrating Clovis's attempts to get closer to his wife.
During the mission , Kast and DR-7X waited for Amidala to give them a signal; when she did, Kast sneaked into her quarters, where he saw Amidala and Clovis embracing. Though it prompted a surge of anger and jealousy in Kast, she discretely showed him the disk she had stolen from Clovis and gave it to Kast, after which the Keepers retreated. It was soon after that the poison that Amidala had been given by Lott Dod began to set in, and she passed out. After Dod revealed to Clovis she was a spy, he helped Kast take her back to their ship, back to Nexara City-world, and forced Dod to give him the antidote, only for Kast to leave Clovis behind, stranded on the planet.
Second Battle of Xarnak I knew they were still alive. I told you not to give up on them.
It's not that I gave up, Kast, but unlike you, when the time comes, I am prepared to let my student go. Can you say the same? ―Xarn Velgor and Luminara Unduli Immediately after routing Separatists near Dorin , Kast and Tano were sent to participate in the Second Battle of Xarnak , alongside Malkor and Ki-Adi-Mundi, as the Concordium returned to destroy the Xarnak primary automaton factory . Intending to make a three-pronged attack against the shield generator, Kast, Tano, and the 501st Legion set out to attack the Separatist defense lines from the south to reach the rendezvous point—with only Malkor's forces arriving at the landing zone, as both Mundi's and Kast's transports were shot down and forced to make their way to Malkor's position by foot. Though they encountered resistance, Kast's team met up with Mundi's forces. After contacting Yularen to deploy a squadron of BTL-B Y-wings to help them, they reunited with Malkor and his company, where they planned the assault on the shield generator.
They then improvised on their plan; leading the charge, Kast and Tano disabled the enemy cannons with EMP grenades , enabling the AT-TEs to destroy the shield generator; as the gunships arrived, the Xarnak Hive in the area surrendered. Though Mundi and Malkor left for medical treatment, the Concordium forces continued pushing towards the massive automaton foundry, and Kast and Tano were joined by Luminara Unduli and her Padawan,
Barriss Offee , who met as Kast and Tano angrily discussed Kast's lack of trust in Tano's dependability. As Offee and Tano were sent into the Xarnak catacombs under the factory to plant explosives, Unduli realized that Kast was unable to let go of Tano and put her at risk, rather than distrusting her. Nevertheless, the two Keeper generals went onto distracting the automaton army by marching straight to the factory and engage the enemy.
As Poggle the Lesser had his super tanks deployed, Kast and Luminara left their men behind and destroyed the bridge, only to learn that their Padawans had not returned yet. Cornered by automatons coming from the factory, Kast was contacted by Tano and she apologized, uncertain that she and Offee would survive their ordeal, before using a super tank to destroy the factory. Refusing to let his Padawan die, Kast ordered to have them found; Unduli, however, told him that he needed to let go of his attachment to her if she were to be dead. Fortunately for them, Kast was contacted by Tano; after locating them, they rescued the two Padawans.
Sending Tano and Offee to recover out of the battlefield, Malkor and Mundi returned to join in the clean-up of Separatist forces. For her part, Unduli left in search of Poggle before he could escape, when a sandstorm broke out. Forced to wait until the storm lifted after her last contact, Kast and Malkor went to Unduli's last known location, the Progate Temple . Entering the catacombs below, not only they discovered undead Xarnak warriors , they also encountered the captive Unduli, Poggle, and the Xarnak Queen Karina the Great . Setting up their men to surround the Queen and her brethren, Kast and Malkor tried to negotiate with Karina, for Malkor wanted to know how the undead warriors were created. After discovering her use of parasitic brain worms ), they freed Unduli, captured Poggle and fled from the Queen, with Rex and Cody burying the Queen in her lair.
With Poggle in Concordium custody, the four Keeper generals prepared to deliver him to Nexara City-world, while they sent Tano and Offee on a mission to transport medical supplies from a medical station near Ord Cestus to Taron Vale on Dantooine . Unbeknownst to them, one of the troopers,
Scythe , was infected with one of the Queen's brain worms and boarded their Pelta
-class frigate
, the TB-73 , forcing the two Padawans to fend off the infected gen-soldiers. When the ship failed to report in as scheduled, Kast believed there to have been a complication, and was proven right when Tano contacted him to tell him about the situation.
Angered, Kast went to Poggle's cell on his own and told the guard troopers to leave them alone. He demanded that Poggle answer his questions with a mind trick, but the archduke informed him that it would have no effect on a Xarnak like him. It was then that Kast resorted to physical violence, attacking the captive Xarnak; with Poggle still unwilling to talk, Kast gave in to his anger and began to Flux choke him. Only then did Poggle tell the Keepers Knight, through a translator automaton , that the worms were affected by the cold. Kast relayed this information to his Padawan, telling her through her comlink to rupture the cooling system. Thanks to his advice, Tano was able to defeat the worms as they reached the medical station. Kast rushed to her encounter, where he consoled Tano on her doubts about sparing her friend's life.
Saleucami Where there is Malkor, you will always find Kast not far behind. ―Grievous Following the capture of Keeper Master Eeth Koth in the Outer Rim, General Grievous sent a holo-transmission to the Keepers Council, in which Grievous stated that he did not care about the Keepers's politics , nor their Concordium, concluding that he lived only to see them all die. As Malkor decoded Koth's hand signals that told them of their location at the Saleucami system , Kast, Malkor, and Adi Gallia were dispatched on a mission to rescue him. As a distraction, Admiral Yularen and Malkor engaged Grievous's forces over Saleucami , with Malkor allowing the Surrogator to be boarded by Grievous. Discreetly, Kast, Gallia, Rex, and a group of clone troopers would execute a voidspace jump into the system in an ambassador shuttle that would bring them right alongside Grievous's cruiser.
Once they boarded it, Kast and Gallia made their way to the stern of the cruiser, where they encountered a T-series tactical automaton with a group of BX-series automaton commandos . The two Keeper destroyed all the automatons and rescued Koth, with Kast taking the injured Koth back to the shuttle. When Malkor reported that Grievous had defeated him and escaped, Gallia left them to help Malkor flank Grievous and capture him once and for all. Unsuccessful in their endeavor, Kast picked the two up at the hangar with their shuttle, only to leave for the Resolute where he waited while Malkor conducted a search mission for Grievous on the planet below alongside Cody, Rex, and other troopers.
Sometime later, Kast and his Padawan spearheaded a mission to Nexara's underworld to apprehend Car Affa , an arms dealer selling Concordium weapons to the Separatists. When they arrived at a cantina where Affa was hiding, Kast instructed Tano to wait outside while he apprehended the fugitive. The confrontation, however, caused a crowd to leave the bar at once—during which Tano lost her flux blade, which prompted her to seek the help of Keeper Master Tera Sinube when they went back to the Temple, as she did not want Kast to find out she had lost her weapon.
Mandalore What will you do? If you shoot me, you prove yourself a hypocrite to every pacifist ideal you hold dear. And you, Malkor, you are no stranger to violence. You'd be hailed as a hero by everyone on this ship— Almost everyone. Come on, then. Who will strike first and brand themselves a cold-blooded killer? ―Tal Merrik, to Zeth Malkor and Satine Kryze, before being killed by Kast Following allegations that Mandalore had allied with the Separatists, and Malkor was sent to the planet to speak to his old friend,
Duchess Satine Kryze , Kast joined them to escort the Duchess on her journey to Nexara City-world aboard the Coronet On the ship, Kast and Malkor told their men to be on alert, when Kryze requested their presence before her, and Kast correctly surmised that Malkor had strong feelings for her just as his former master told him of their past together. When DR-7X picked up readings in the cargo hold, the two generals were informed by Rex, and Kast went to investigate—only to be attacked by assassin probes . After destroying two of them, Kast informed Malkor that there might be a traitor on board the ship, and Kast returned to sweep the hold in search of more.
After Malkor lured the traitor out,
Tal Merrik , and Kast destroyed another probe, he helped search the Coronet for Merrik and Kryze, now his hostage, until several B2 super battle automatons sent by the Death Watch boarded the ship. Leaving Malkor to save his "girlfriend," he destroyed the automatons and then went to find Malkor, who like Kryze, hesitated to kill Merrik; Kast, however, had no such compunctions and impaled Merrik, branding himself a "cold-blooded killer," to retrieve the Senator's detonator before he could destroy the ship. With no further complications, they arrived at Nexara City-world; as Kryze complimented Malkor, Kast noted that she was quite the remarkable woman.
Following the Mandalore Defense Resolution , Kast and Malkor escorted Amidala to a meeting with the Chancellor in which he thanked her, but also apologized to Kryze, before leaving Malkor and Kryze alone.
Trapped on Vanqor That kid destroyed an entire cruiser trying to get to you, and you're just gonna let it go?
Is there something else I should be doing, Kast?
How about tracking him down?
So I should behave as this child does. I should seek revenge.
No. How is it revenge if you stop this kid and bring him to justice? ―Xarn Velgor and Taron Vale, on Kael Ryn Preparing to rendezvous with a Concordium frigate, Kast and Taron Vale traveled aboard the Endurance , on which they greeted a squad of clone cadets , with an infiltrated Kael Ryn among them. After Ryn sabotaged the ship and everyone had to evacuate in escape pods, including himself before being rescued by Bossk and Aurra Sing on the Slave I , Kast and Vale rescued the missing cadets' damaged pod.
As the survivors were being transferred to medical frigate, the two went to the Endurance s crash site, where they searched for Admiral Kilian and any other survivors. As the bridge appeared relatively intact, they resolved to enter the wreckage, even with DR-7X's misgivings, which prompted Vale to chastise Kast for encouraging individuality in his astromech.
Leaving DR-7X and R8-B7 to scan for life forms, Kast and Vale entered the bridge to find the survivors executed, with Kast noticing a Vraxian helmet . With Vale correctly surmising that it was bait set by Kael Ryn, he saved Kast from taking the full brunt of the explosion set off by touching the helmet. Now trapped under the rubble, the wounded Kast and the unconscious Vale were soon found by DR-7X, whom Kast told to call the Temple for help. As they waited for the astromech to come back, Vale told Kast why Ryn wanted him dead: he sought revenge for killing his father, Jango, during the first battle of Xarnak.
As the Endurance began to collapse, they were rescued by Tano, Plo Koon, and a clone squad, thanks to DR-7X delivering the message at the Temple itself.
After spending time in a bacta tank at the Temple, Kast urged Vale to track Ryn down and bring him to justice. The Keeper Master did not agree, until Koon and Tano showed them a message from Ryn and Sing, with hostages—one of them,
Ponds , killed in cold blood by Sing. Though Vale decided to go, Koon said that he would take the mission , alongside Tano, since both Vale and Kast were still recovering from their injuries. As they arrived on Nexara City-world with Bossk and Ryn in custody, Kast was present as Ryn apologized for causing so much destruction, but claimed he would never forgive Vale for killing his father, before they were taken away to the Concordium Judiciary Central Detention Center Balith I hope this assignment was not too boring for you.
Ah, it had its moments.
Huh, like the one where you convinced untrained children to help you overthrow a corrupt government?
That was a highlight.
It sounds pretty risky. You're lucky you didn't get hurt.
Nothing you wouldn't have done. ―Xarn Velgor and Ahsoka Tano Following his wife's mission to discover a conspiracy on Mandalore,
Kast and Captain Rex escorted Tano to her solo mission on Mandalore to instruct cadets at the Royal Academy of Government at the behest of Duchess Kryze and the Keepers Council. Upon their arrival at Sundari , Kast presented Tano to the Duchess and Prime Minister Almec . Before leaving to join with Kit Fisto , Kast took his Padawan's flux blade to respect Mandalore's laws. After Tano exposed Almec's black market operation, Kast reunited with his Padawan, to whom he returned her flux blade.
Back in the Keepers Temple, the Keepers Council congratulated Tano on her courage and strength, but also ordered Kast to command the Third Legion on Balith to give assistance in their civil war . Told by the Council to stay behind so she could give them her full report, Tano pleaded to Kast to let her come along, but he told her that she should use this time in the library to prove him wrong about his assertion that she learned more on the battlefield than in the Temple, before leaving for Balith with Rex.
Kast returned to Nexara City-world to greet, along with Vorn, his Padawan, Amidala, and Bail Venn after the Elara Refugee Conference , during which Tano captured Aurra Sing. Wondering who would pay for Amidala's assassination, Vorn had Tano use her improved precognitive abilities to find out who was behind the assassination attempt. With her description, Amidala realized who it was—Ziro the Hutt. Kast and his Padawan then went to the Judiciary Detention Center to question Ziro, who was tricked by Tano into confessing that he had hired Sing for the job. Satisfied, Kast and his Padawan then left the Hutt to his misery.
Battle of Cygnar I suppose you expect me to surrender.
Actually, I plan to let the gen-soldiers execute you. Right now.
Not this time. ―Asajj Ventress and Xarn Velgor Forewarned by the destruction of Rishi Station and an intercepted transmission between Asajj Ventress and General Grievous, Kast and Malkor traveled to Cygnar to warn Shaak Ti and Prime Minister Lama Su of an upcoming Separatist attack , with Rex, Cody, Echo, Fives, and the rest of the 501st Legion in their company. Shortly, Grievous's fleet arrived and Kast led the Concordium space forces, scoring several serious hits against the enemy. On the planet below,
Tipoca City was attacked by Trident
-class assault ship
and AQ-series battle automatons , prompting Malkor to recall Kast to the city. Soon after returning, Kast engaged Ventress, who had stolen the prime DNA sample of the gen-soldiers' template, Ryn Kar. With the aid of gen-soldier platoon , Kast recovered the sample from Ventress, but she, like Grievous, managed to escape yet again.
The blockade of Pantora Should you really proceed without the Council's approval?
We do it all the time. Don't we, Snips?
Yup. ―Amidala, Kast and Tano discuss the kidnapping of Papanoida's daughters Kast was later informed by his apprentice that Pantora Chairman Papanoida's daughters had been kidnapped . Knowing that the Keepers could not get officially involved, he slyly gave her permission to rescue them due to "personal concerns"; this turned out to be successful, with both daughters being safely returned to their father.
Senate crisis Young Kast… Not so impressive without your flux blade, are you, Keeper? ―Cad Bane to an unconscious Kast Supposed to be on a meditative retreat,
Kast spent some time with his wife on her apartment, observing as Amidala anxiously arranged a party for Senator Aang , who held the deciding vote on the Military Oversight Committee . Kast tried to ease her mind, telling her that the party would work out and that she should relax, when they saw LQ-9M being fuzzy about the arrangements. At Amidala's request, Kast sent the protocol automaton and DR-7X to buy the jogan fruit that the latter had forgotten to buy for Aang's favorite dessert, jogan fruitcake . Although they were delayed, Amidala nevertheless hosted the party all the same, with her and Kast waiting for the two automatons to come back—who did so just in time to present the cake to Senator Aang.
Not too long after, Kast went to the Senate Building to convince Amidala to go away together for two weeks , to a faraway place where no one would recognize them, so that they could actually be husband and wife, instead of Senator and Keeper. Amidala, however, rejected the idea, for she was preparing an important bill for the Senate. Kast retorted that the bill seemed more important than their love, but Amidala countered that it was not, just that they both lived to serve the Concordium. However, Kast stated that there was nothing more important than the way he felt about her, and proved it by giving Amidala his flux blade, which, Malkor had told him once, was his "life." Though she believed he was teasing her, Kast was sincere about his feelings, and the two shared a kiss.
They were soon interrupted by LQ-9M and Senator Venn's voices, prompting Kast to hide under her desk and Amidala to hide his flux blade in her sleeve. As she went to the lobby to attend a meeting about the Enhanced Privacy Invasion Bill , only to be taken hostage along with several other Senators by Cad Bane and his team. Following her, Kast saw the situation, when Bane spotted him and sent his henchmen to capture him, but Kast avoided them long enough to contact Draven Nul and defeat an IG-86 sentinel automaton empty-handed, before being shocked into unconsciousness by Robonino and Aurra Sing and brought before the Senators. After the bounty hunters planted bombs that would go off if their laser detectors were triggered and left the building, Kast woke up and Amidala returned his flux blade. He then used it to cut a hole in the floor, dropping all the senators one floor down into safety, just before Bane triggered the bombs and escaped alongside the now-freed Ziro.
Meeting Madam Synata I'm still not convinced this trip is a good idea, Anakin... ―Padmé Amidala Following the crisis, Kast went with Amidala to Caamas to board legendary actress Madam Risha Synata 's private ship . However, they were late to see Madam Synata's play about Darth Krist-Ov . After they arrived, Kast and Amidala met Synata. Kast agreed to see her museum , despite Amidala's reluctance. Kast took Amidala to her room. The next day, Kast and Amidala went to the museum. There, Kast and Amidala saw a display of Varykino where they exchanged their vows.
Kast and Amidala came under attack by several robots from the displays. Kast fought them off and helped Amidala escape them. Kast and Amidala then discovered a Separatist banner and a hologram of Count Dooku.
Kast and Amidala discovered that Synata was collaborating with Dooku and the Separatists. Kast then took one of the Separatist agents while Amidala discovered a list of anti-Separatist senators. Kast and Amidala discovered that along with Amidala, Synata planned to kill them with Dioxis gas. Kast and Amidala fought several LEP servant automatons to stop Synata from killing the senators. With assistance from DR-7X, Kast and Amidala reached the theater.
As Amidala told the audience to run, Kast borrowed a mask and helmet from an actor before confronting Synata, who had her own flux blade. Kast fought Synata until he held her at the end of his flux blade. Kast and Amidala then reported their trip to the Keepers Council.
Intense training Look, I know this is tough, but…[sigh]…I want it to be difficult. This is about life and death. And as your master, I'm responsible for you. The best way I can protect you is to teach you how to protect yourself. ―Xarn Velgor, to Ahsoka Tano After arriving late to one of Tano's training sessions, Kast became frustrated with the simplicity of the exercises. In turn, he told her to meet with him later for an actual training session. Later, Kast took Tano to an isolated area with Rex and the 501st. He then had the gen-soldiers surround Tano and fire stun bolts at her. The sessions continued for hours until Tano told Kast that the battle automatons weren't nearly as good as the gen-soldiers. Kast explained that this was the point and that he wanted her training to be difficult so that she would know how to protect herself. Tano would continue the sessions with Rex and the 501st throughout the war.
Sometime later, Kast was sparring with Tano, teaching her how to fight with two flux blades . She struggled to consciously utilize the other flux blade, but Kast reassured her. When Master Vorn entered to spar with her, Kast watched as she struggled against Vorn, but ultimately learned how to use Jar'Kai more effectively.
Unexpected Company Sometime later, Kast was assigned to a mission with Amidala to Elara . It took Kast a lot of work to get the mission with her, but they were both happy to spend some time alone together. That is until, Tano ran up to them, saying that she was encouraged help with the mission by Malkor. They all get on a T-6 shuttle, where Kast charted a shorter course in order to speed up the mission. Tano warned him that the Separatist fleet was spotted on that flight path, and that they shouldn't take it. It was too late, however, and Kast's ship exited voidspace in the midst of the fleet. Kast took control and tried to maneuver around oncoming fire, while Amidala and Tano took control of the laser turrets. Despite their best efforts, stray laser fire hit Amidala's side of the ship. Kast jumped to protect Amidala, which resulted in them embracing in a hug. Tano was able to take control of the ship and jumped into voidspace. Upon their arrival on Elara, Kast told her that he was glad she came. Amidala mentioned that her and Kast made a good team, with Tano returning the compliment.
Trouble with Tomarian The showman Tomarian later requested Kast as a Keeper escort for his Annual Festival of Light Gala . Kast thought that there would be an actual threat at the event, but in actuality Tomarian had requested him to impress his guests. Kast was then surprised to see Senator Amidala dressed up for the occasion. They both talked, glad to see each other. Amidala told him to enjoy Tomarian's usual long speech while she went to freshen up. Kast, along with Tomarian, walked into the theater where the gala was being held. Suddenly, the room went dark and Kast ignited his saber. He asked Tomarian if this was apart of the show, to which he answered no and put on a gas mask. Kast, along with everyone else, fell unconscious as the room filled with a noxious gas.
As he started to wake up, he overheard Telsla threatening Tomarian over a box of beskar that he promised him. Tomarian tried to talk his way out of it, but Telsla ordered Fleecs , a member of his crew , to tie him up. Telsla then set off a bomb in the venue, the commotion allowing Kast to escape unnoticed.
He managed to catch up with Amidala, who had escaped the gas attack and stolen a pulse rifle from one of Telsla's goons . He snuck behind her, scaring her, before he offered to get her out of there. She denied his request as she didn't want to leave anyone in the building behind. After they argued they agreed to hide in the scaffolding to come up with a plan. Amidala tried to contact "Checker" , who was unresponsive. As more explosives went off, she tried to contact him again, but there was massive feedback that revealed their position. They fought off Telsla, causing him to flee. They jumped down and killed Fleecs before chasing after Telsla. Just as Telsla was about to blow up the music hall where most of the guests were, Kast stopped him. He bargained with Telsla, saying that he would make Tomarian talk in exchange for one bar of beskar. Meanwhile, Amidala snuck up behind Telsla and pressed her pulse rifle against his back. He swung at her, dropping the explosive detonator. Checker was able to catch it, and Amidala confidently placed Telsla under arrest, making Kast fall in love with her a little more. After everyone had woken up, Kast complimented the senator on her performance. Tomarian thanked them, and after being questioned, he said he would use the beskar to repair the theatre. After Kast glared at him, he backtracked and also said he would donate a lot of it to charity. Amidala wished him luck, and Kast asked her out for dinner, to which the senator agreed.
Keeper Ceremony One day, Kast was waiting with Master Vorn in the Keepers temple grounds for Tano. Once she arrived, Kast reprimanded her for being late, but Vorn praised her for helping out and proceeded to extend her Padawan braid.
Following a debate in the senate about a financial reform bill , Kast was asked by Amidala to persuade the Keepers Council to speak with Draven Nul about denying the continuation of the war. Kast allowed Tano to go with Amidala in order for her to learn more about politics. After an apparent Separatist attack , Kast confronted his apprentice about her and Amidala's meeting with Onderon 's senator Mina Bonteri on Raxus Secundus and how foolish it was. Despite that, Kast's apprentice learned that politics were not as black and white as she had previously thought.
Dathomirian threat Warrior-like men, witches—this place has all kinds of fun. ―Xarn Velgor to Zeth Malkor, on Dathomir Kast and Malkor commanded Concordium forces in a battle against Asajj Ventress's fleet in the Sullust system . Directly participating in the battle with their starfighters, Malkor was singled out and attacked by Ventress, whose Ginivex
-class fanblade starfighter
was, in turn, disabled by Kast. As Malkor and Ventress crashed in the hangar of the Separatist command ship, Kast soon landed to assist his former master; together, the two Keeper engage Ventress in a duel. They were interrupted, however, by the destruction of the ship, secretly ordered by Count Dooku. The two Keeper fled in their interceptors, presuming Ventress to be doomed.
After the massacre on Devaron Kast and Malkor were sent to Dathomir , as footage had shown the Council of a Dathomirian's role during the attack on Devaron. Once on Dathomir, they made their way to a village , where they were ambushed by the Nightbrothers until Kast took their leader hostage; the Zabrak then told the Keepers that a Nightsister had taken one of them to their fortress , prompting the Keepers to go there. Kast and Malkor questioned Mother Talzin , who told them his name—Savage Opress—and whereabouts. The two Keeper then headed to Toydaria where they confronted Opress, but failed to stop him from killing King Katuunko . Using the king's ship as theirs was destroyed, Kast and Malkor caught up with their target on Dooku's Separatist flagship; however, Opress managed to overpower them, forcing the two to retreat and leave the ship.
Killing a defector Master, I didn't mean to-- It's all right, Anakin. You did nothing wrong.
Don't…hold it against me, boy. It merely seemed…the quickest way back…to the sunrise… ―Xarn Velgor, Zeth Malkor and Mekedrix Following their encounter with Opress, Kast and Malkor were called away from a blockade at Herdessa to meet with Yularen. The admiral informed them that Mekedrix had gone missing. In addition, an execution squad known as the Death Wind had been striking at Concordium and Separatist targets. The group's namesake, the Death Wind Corridor also passed by Mekedrix's home planet of Roon, creating a possible link. Yularen went on to say that the Death Wind may have taken up a covert position on Ando . As such, Kast and Malkor were to locate the Death Wind and kill Mekedrix if he was involved with them. Kast initially refused, believing that killing a potential ally would make the Keepers like the Separatists. Malkor assured Kast that they would attempt to find a peaceful solution before they departed for Ando. After arriving on the planet and pushing through the local swamplands, the Keepers came upon empty sets of clone armor being used as effigies. Upon reaching the terminus of a river, they discovered an outpost built from the ruins of a Shade Covenant temple. They were then met by the Death Wind, who allowed the Keepers to see their leader in exchange for their flux blades. Entering the outpost, Kast and Malkor came upon Mekedrix, seated on a throne and wielding a sword. Mekedrix claimed he had saved his victims by showing them the truth; that the purpose of existence was to kill and die. When Mekedrix revealed that his sword was made from the bones of a gen-soldier, Malkor asked him what the clone's name was. Malkor went on to claim that life gave the stellar realm meaning and that death had no use for a sunrise, a name, a home or a friend. Mekedrix then lunged at Malkor, but Kast retrieved his flux blade and stabbed Mekedrix through the chest. Kast expressed regret over killing him, but Mekedrix assured that this was the quickest way back to the sunrise before he passed away.
Personal and wartime conflicts The Destiny of the Chosen One You are the Chosen One. You have brought balance to this world. Stay on this path and you will do it again… for the stellar realm. But beware… your heart… ―The Father's last words, to Xarn Velgor When the Keepers Council received a 2,000-year-old Keeper distress signal , Kast, along with Malkor and Tano, were sent by the High Council to the Chrelythiumn system , where they were supposed to meet up with Captain Rex and an armed cruiser. Though they both made it to the rendezvous point, neither Rex's nor the Keepers's ship could see each other. In fact, the Keepers's ship was guided to a mysterious planet,
Mortis , where they met by a mysterious figure called the Daughter , who asked Kast if he was "the one" and who wanted to lead them to the Father , when Kast was separated from Malkor and Tano, and the Daughter.
Kast tried to follow her, only to reach the monastery , where he met the Father and was given shelter for the night. As he tried to sleep, the figure of his mother, Shmi, appeared before him and encouraged him to tell her of his pain and guilt—that he was too late to save her, and that he had failed as a Keeper and that he had failed her, having tasted only vengeance when he slaughtered so many to avenge her death. But, as he mentioned his wife, who he claimed was "everything" to him, the figure finally revealed itself to be the Son , briefly assuming a monstrous appearance before disappearing.
Shaken by the encounter, he confronted the Father, who told of his family's history, and wondered if he was truly the Chosen One. Though Kast dismissed the prophecy as a myth, the Father arranged a test to see whether the Keepers Knight was fit to fulfill his destiny . Ordering his children to kill Tano and Malkor, the Father goaded Kast into choosing between his master and his apprentice, but Kast instead controlled the two beings, bringing them to their knees. Having proved himself to be the Chosen One, the Father pleaded with him to stay, but Kast refused, leaving with Tano and Malkor on their shuttle They were interrupted when the Son kidnapped Tano, prompting Kast to pursue him, at the cost of damaging their ship near Son's cathedral. Despite Malkor's reservations, Kast went after his Padawan and climbed the tower to find Tano, corrupted by the deep flux. Kast fought her, soon to be joined by Malkor, only to be interrupted by the Father's arrival. Malkor tried to hand Kast the Dagger of Mortis , but Tano grabbed it and gave it to the Son, who killed her, for she was no longer useful; the deep flux user then mortally wounded his sister as she stepped in to save her Father. Kast begged the Father to save Tano, and the dying Daughter used Kast as a conduit to channel the last of her life force into Tano, resurrecting her. The Father told them to leave the planet before the Son used their ship to do so.
The ship's repairs forced them to stay a bit longer, though, and Kast went to the Father for guidance. On their meeting, the Father was determined to kill Son, but refused Kast's offer of aid, telling the Keepers Knight to look inside himself for the answer for what was to be done. Leaving him, Kast encountered the Synth Flux spirit of his first mentor, Toran Sol. Though Kast sought his advice, Sol advised him to remember his training and trust his instincts, and to go to the Well of the Dark Side to face his most personal challenge. There, the Son forced Kast to confront his future, one consumed by the deep flux. Horrified, Kast submitted to the Son as he promised him the power to avert this destiny . Soon, Malkor arrived, and Kast left him trapped at the well, claiming to have seen the truth—that it was the Keepers who would stand in the way of peace.
Kast arrived as his Padawan disabled the ship and went to rescue Malkor, while the Son went to steal the Dagger. As Kast waited, the Father appeared before him and recognized that his son had broken the rules of time ; he wiped Kast's memory of these future visions, leaving him unconscious, and brought him to his monastery. Kast awoke to find the Father, Malkor, and Tano, moments before the Son arrived with the Dagger. To end the conflict, the Father impaled himself to distract his Son, while Kast killed the Son with his flux blade. As the Father also died, he warned Kast to be wary of his feelings as they could lead to his downfall. As was noted by the Father,
Kast had brought balance to the Synth Flux on Mortis through the deaths of the Synth Flux wielders, and he still had a chance to do so for the full stellar realm.
After the Father's death, the three Keeper were transplanted back to the stellar realm to finally rendezvous with Rex. Surprised to learn that, to Rex and the others, they had only been gone for a moment, they returned to the Dominion Cruiser.
They would later report this encounter to the Council, with Kast even mentioning that he had spoken with Sol to Vorn. Even though Kast was not entirely convinced, they came to believe it had merely been an illusion of the old mentor.
In reality, it had truly been the spirit of Sol.
The Citadel I'm not sure what to think of your new ally.
Well, I think we need people like him. This is a war. If we aren't willing to do what it takes to win, we risk losing everything we try to protect. ―Zeth Malkor and Xarn Velgor, on Wilhuff Varnus More than one year and nine months into the war, following the capture of Keeper Council member Even Piell by the Separatists, Kast and Malkor were briefed by Master Plo Koon and sent along with their team—which included Rex, Cody, Echo, and Fives—to infiltrate the Citadel on Lola Sayu to retrieve Master Piell, who held the information on the Nexus Route . Refusing to let Tano risk her life on such a dangerous mission, Kast devised a strategy to bypass the lifeform scanners of the prison; the whole team underwent carbon-freezing and were transported to the Citadel by Commander DR-7X and his reprogrammed battle automaton squadron.
After landing, Kast was thawed out and he found, to his surprise, that Tano had disobeyed orders and followed him. Forced to take her along, the team made its way to the facilities, leaving the astromech to guard their shuttle. After free-climbing to the entry point through a cliff wall blasted by a strong gale and littered with electro-mines , and after Tano deactivated the ray-shield on the entrance, the team was able to enter, except for Charger , who fell to his death and alerted the prison staff, led by Osi Sobeck , of their presence. Inside, they lost Longshot , but finally made it to Master Piell, whom they freed and who told them they needed to rescue his officers.
Despite the many traps laid through the fortress , the team rescued the officers, when they met the other carrier of the Nexus Route coordinates, Captain Wilhuff Varnus . The team was split into two—while Malkor's team created a diversion, Kast would lead the others away. In Varnus's company, Kast and his team escaped through the cavern system below the prison, when Varnus expressed some skepticism about the Keepers's plan, to which Kast told Varnus that he reserved his trust to those who understood gratitude.
However, they soon found common ground, for they both believed the Keepers Code prevented the Order from going far enough to achieve victory in the ongoing war, and Varnus developed a grudging appreciation for Kast.
As they made their way out of the fuel line to rendezvous with DR-7X and the shuttle, they were attacked by automatons and forced to join Malkor at their pick-up point, with the shuttle heavily guarded. During the skirmish, Echo tried to defend the shuttle, but its destruction and subsequent explosion seemingly killed him. As they ran into the cave system, Kast and Malkor contacted the Council, telling them they needed to escape, and Koon immediately departed to pick them up.
They contacted the Council again to be informed of the extraction point; as they proceeded to the location, Sobeck sent anoobas after them. The creatures claimed Master Piell's life, who passed his intel on to Tano, and the group took a moment to honor his death. Ultimately, they got to the extraction point on time, and fled on Plo Koon's rescue shuttle, with Saesee Tiin 's fleet covering them to allow their escape back to Nexara City-world.
Padawan lost When I was out there, alone, all I had was your training and the lessons you taught me. And because of you, I did survive. And not only that, I was able to lead others to survive as well.
I don't know what to say.
I do. Thank you, Master.
You're welcome, my Padawan. ―Ahsoka Tano and Xarn Velgor Kast, Tano, and 501st forces fought on Felucia alongside Plo Koon and his gen-soldiers in taking out a reinforced Separatist outpost . Breaking their forces into three groups, Kast and Rex's team were responsible for attacking the front gate and destroying the tactical automaton TZ-33 , securing the outpost together with Koon and Tano's teams.
However, Kast then noticed that Tano had disappeared during the battle, and ordered a perimeter sweep. Kast had his men search around the outpost, to no avail several times over. Master Koon then informed that he had alerted all forces throughout the Outer Rim and assured Kast that she would be found eventually, but also firmly told him that their mission on Felucia was finished and that they had to return to Nexara City-world. However, Kast refused to abandon her, to which Koon observed his emotions were clouding his judgment. Kast countered "I will not leave her fate up to others." Koon insisted, "It's time to go." Only then did Kast comply and call everybody in to leave the planet.
Back at the Temple, Kast relentlessly searched for possibilities in the star charts, which prompted Koon to suggest that he should trust in Tano's abilities.
Following the events on Wasskah , Kast's search for his Padawan came to an end when she arrived to the Temple on the Halo , in company of her Thoran allies and the younglings O-Mer and Jinx . Upon seeing her, Kast profusely apologized for all that happened, heavily blaming himself for his apparent inability to protect her. Tano, however, assured him that his instruction gave her the skills necessary to survive and thanked him for his guidance. The two bowed to each other in newfound mutual respect under the delighted eye of Master Vorn.
Battle of Horain Stand down! It's General Malkor. And it looks like he's bringing a new friend. ―Xarn Velgor Sometime later Kast participated in the Battle of Horain , alongside Malkor and Rex. Kast was contacted by Rex, who was requesting assistance, though he was already preoccupied with a group of Vulture Droids. Kast later met Malkor and Rex in disbelief, noticing they had hijacked an AAT alongside faulty battle automaton Bats Skirmish on Retta On Retta , Kast ambushed a group of B1 battle automatons alongside Grand Master Vorn. Kast was soon attacked by Kreeda , a Segredo who was attempting to scare the Keepers away. However, Kreeda soon became friendly to the Keepers, as she was an old friend of Vorn. When Kast attempted to recruit Kreeda as a spy, the Segredo left, and Kast and Vorn were attacked by battle automatons soon after. After being separated with Vorn, he met the Keepers Master and Kreeda again, the latter of whom explained she was protecting refugees of the Gene Wars. Using a hat from one of the individuals she was protecting, Kast pretended to be using the " cloaking cap " to distract General Flebek , allowing Vorn to take out a B1. Believing there was no secret to invisibility and that it had been a trick, Flebek fled the area and boarded her ship. Upon asking what his report would entail, Vorn told Kast that he would tell Taron Vale there was nothing of interest on Retta.
Battle of Mon Cala Alright. I think I'm getting the hang of this. [Kast gets electrocuted]
Less talking helps. ―Xarn Velgor and Kit Fisto, restrained by Mon Calamari eels After the assassination of King Kolina of Mon Cala and new discord in the fragile peace between the Mon Calamari and the Quarren , Kast and Amidala, sent as representatives of the Concordium, arrived to the planet at the behest of Captain Gial Ackbar of the Mon Calamari Guard and protector of young Prince Lee-Char , but so did Riff Tamson , Separatist ambassador . Though Kast and Amidala tried to mediate between the parties, the Quarren left the council hall, dissatisfied with the Mon Calamari rule. Along with Ackbar, they contacted Vorn and Vale, who dispatched a company of clone troopers equipped for underwater combat under the command of Kit Fisto and Tano.
In the meantime, during the Quarren attack assisted by Separatist aqua automatons, Kast and Amidala accompanied Lee-Char, who in turn was bodyguarded by Ackbar. He engaged the Quarren soldiers until he was recalled by his wife to help Senator Meena Tills , while leaving Tano to cover the prince and rescue him from Tamson. As the first Separatist/Quarren assault came to an end, Kast reunited with Amidala, Tano, Fisto, Ackbar, Lee-Char, and Tills to await the second assault, this time assisted by the Hydroid Medusas , which proved incredibly effective against the Concordium-Mon Calamari army. Forced to retreat into the caves below, they regrouped to decide their next course of action—to retreat to the surface, only return to the sea floor as their frigate was destroyed and they were attacked by more enemies.
They split into two groups: Kast went with his wife, Ackbar, and Tills, whereas Fisto, Tano, and two troopers accompanied the Prince. Expecting Concordium reinforcements to come soon, Kast and his group proceeded to the Mon Calamari central planetary scanner facility, where the Keepers Knight collapsed the entire structure with the Synth Flux, rendering planetary defenses blind to upcoming Concordium reinforcements—the Gungan Grand Army, and Representative Binks, sent at the behest of the High Council. However, Tamson used Trident
-class assault ship to create whirlpools, disorienting the Concordium-Gungan forces, and though Kast managed to destroy one of them, all but Tano and Lee-Char were captured.
Brought before Riff Tamson, Kast and Fisto were restrained by electric eels , whereas Amidala and Jar Jar Binks were imprisoned within containment devices , interrogating the two Keeper about the whereabouts of Prince Lee-Char. During the interrogation, Tamson attempted to force them to talk by biting a tiny hole into Amidala's helmet, allowing her suit to fill up with water . However, Tamson left to see the located Prince in person, Kast and Fisto used the Synth Flux to remove the water from her helmet, while Binks used his saliva to seal the puncture, saving her from drowning. The four were soon taken to witness Prince Lee-Char's execution, in which Nossor Ri , the Quarren leader, saved the prince at the critical moment and turned his people against the Separatists, resulting in the victory of the Mon Cala people. With the peace restored, Kast and his friends were present for the coronation of Lee-Char , now recognized by both Mon Calamari and Quarren as their new monarch.
Aqualis Padmé, I'm sorry. ―Xarn Velgor, after being traded for Grievous Following rumors that the Gungans planned to aid the Separatists in an attack on Theed, Kast accompanied Amidala to Theed and later, to the Gungan territory near Lake Paonga where they met with Binks, who confirmed the troubling rumors. He took the couple to Otoh Gunga, where they spoke to Boss Lyonie , and realized he was under the influence of a mind-controlling necklace , which Kast telekinetically snatched, making him recover; Lyonie told them that new minister Rish Loo , had given it to him.
Kast, Amidala, and Binks assisted Lyonie as he confronted Loo, only to face automaton commandos, and for Lyonie to be stabbed by Loo before the minister's escape. Taking the Boss to an infirmary, the couple noticed the uncanny resemblance between the unconscious Lyonie and Binks, who they convince to impersonate Lyonie and cancel the Gungan assault on Theed. As he did so, uncovering Loo's deception to the army, Kast pursued the fugitive minister to a trap set by Count Dooku in Loo's laboratory . As Dooku killed Loo and revealed his secret input in the Battle of Aqualis, Kast dueled the Count, only to be overwhelmed by Dooku's powers and his four MagnaGuards.
Dooku then contacted Amidala and offered an exchange—the Keepers Knight for General Grievous, who had been captured by the Gungan Grand Army, thanks to Gungan General Tarpals's sacrifice. An hour later, Kast was brought and handed over to Amidala, Queen Neeyutnee and the Gungans, while Grievous was allowed to remain free.
Battle of Umbara Does my battalion have to do everything?
You seem to always volunteer. ―Xarn Velgor and Zeth Malkor During the campaign on Umbara , Kast and Malkor discussed their attack strategy against the Umbaran capital city , which was being defended by Confederate and local militia forces. Together with Rex and Fives, Kast prepared to repel enemy reinforcements striking against Malkor's joint attack with Keeper Masters Saesee Tiin and Pong Krell , who would be supporting Malkor from the south whereas Kast and 501st Legion would advance from the north.
Amidst heavy fire, Kast and his battalion made it to the landing site, and then to a ridge which they used as a staging area. As he and Rex awaited the rest of their men, hoping to move out soon and reinforce General Malkor's battalion,
Dogma reported that all platoons had reported in, and Kast told him to get some rest. Dogma replied that he was fine, but Rex told him it was a direct order, prompting Dogma to do as he was told. As Rex observed Dogma was "wound tight but loyal," Kast joked that he reminded him of Rex, who disagreed, saying that was him in the old days.
Shortly after this, the Umbarans ambushed Kast and his troops, but an airstrike by Master Krell and Odd Ball saved them. After the quick save, Krell approached Kast with a disconcerting message: the Council had ordered Kast back to Nexara City-world, effective immediately, as requested by Supreme Chancellor Draven Nul, for reasons unknown. Though reluctant to leave his troops in the midst of battle, Kast was forced to relinquish command over his troops to Master Krell and make the journey back to Nexara City-world.
Despite Krell's efforts to sabotage the Concordium effort by tricking the 501st and the 212th battalions into attacking each other, Malkor was successful in taking the capital and routing the remaining Umbaran forces, securing all sectors of the planet for the Concordium.
Kiros Zygerrian scum!
I'll handle that slaver… ―Xarn Velgor, on Darts D'Nar After ten rotations of Vorn's last contact with the Togruta colonists on Kiros , Kast, Tano, Rex, Cody, Malkor, and their forces arrived at the world to investigate its silence, with Kast particularly worried they would be too late. After landing, suspicious of the lack of colonists in the town , they used BARC speeders to break through the city and surround the governor's tower , used as the Separatist Headquarters. The enemy commander,
Darts D'Nar , then sent a message to Malkor to discuss terms of surrender. Kast then took the holoprojector out of Cody's hand and called D'Nar "
Zygerrian scum." Though Kast wanted to deal with the slaver himself, Malkor told him to locate the missing colonists while he negotiated. As Kast contacted Admiral Yularen to conduct a planetary bioscan to find them, Malkor explained Kast's behavior to Tano.
Overhearing from Malkor's negotiations that D'Nar had planted bombs throughout the city, Kast and Tano set out to disarm them. Despite sniper automatons , they destroyed the bombs in the nick of time, just for Malkor to tell them D'Nar was escaping on the Tecora . With some effort, they managed to board the ship, inside which D'Nar pitted a blixus against them, but Kast managed to throw it off the ship, while Tano bested D'Nar in the cockpit. Soon, Kast joined her and, with frightening intensity, threatened the Zygerrian into revealing the colonists' location, much to Tano's surprise. However, it proved effective as D'Nar revealed that his Queen would hold the Royal Slave Auction.
Zygerria A most curious Keeper who cares more about his friends than his own mission. Hero. General. And now, my servant. ―Miraj Scintel When Kast, Malkor, Yularen, and Tano told the Keepers Council of their findings, they were tasked with locating the colonists.
On the Tecora , and disguised as Zygerrian, they land on the capital city of Zygerria ; splitting into two teams (Malkor and Rex went to located the colonists), Kast and Tano went to meet Queen Miraj Scintel , with Kast posing as "Lars Quell" and bringing news of Bruno Denturri 's supposed death, and presenting Tano as a present. He quickly won Scintel's favor, with the Queen inviting him to accompany her during the slave auction, where she intended to have Kast prove himself a slaver by torturing Malkor.
Instead, he freed Malkor and tried to escape with DR-7X returning their flux blades, Rex providing cover fire and Tano confronting the Queen. Despite their efforts, however, they were subdued, but Scintel saw Kast's determination, for five electro-whips were needed to reduce him. Despite Dooku and half her kingdom demanding his death, Scintel had Kast brought to her quarters. There he woke up and interrogated Scintel about his friends' whereabouts, while he throttled the Queen with the Synth Flux. However, Scintel gasped that his friends would die unless he obeyed her, and Kast was forced to comply and become her bodyguard and escort, just as she became infatuated by him.
After a few days, Dooku would arrive, and Kast observed that she too was a slave to the Count, before she left to receive the Shade Lord. With the help of DR-7X, he escaped his guards and freed Tano from her cage and sent her to prep the ship, while he interrogated Scintel to tell him Malkor and Rex's location. However, he found himself before Atai Molec , who had betrayed Scintel, and Dooku, with whom Kast futilely dueled before Dooku blamed him of killing Scintel before her guards. Kast then took Scintel and escaped to the Tecora , aboard which she told him Malkor was in the Kadavo system and that she, just like Kast, was a slave, before dying.
After contacting Plo Koon to send reinforcements, Kast and Tano soon reached Kadavo and entered the slave-processing facility . During the battle that ensued, Kast disabled the turrets that were attacking Koon and his forces, while Tano saved the Togruta colonists from falling to their death (as caused by Keeper Agruss ) by placing Admiral Barton Coburn 's Arquitens
-class light cruiser
Hand of Justice underneath the facility. Along with Rex and Malkor, Kast evacuated the facility before it was destroyed on Koon's orders, before returning to the rest of Koon's fleet.
Draven Nul kidnapped Kast, a powerful Keeper you are, yet unpredictable and dangerous you can be, to both your friends and enemies. For Zeth, on your patience, everything depends. ―Vorn, to Xarn Velgor After the capture of Moralo Eval , rumored to be the mastermind of a plot to kidnap Supreme Chancellor Draven Nul, Kast, Tano, and Malkor were recalled to an emergency meeting by the High Council while they walked through the nightly streets of Nexara City-world. On their way to the Temple, however, a sniper opened fire at them, which prompted the three Keeper to chase their aggressor. However, the sniper killed Malkor before escaping on a speeder, prompting Kast to return to his master, to find Tano crying and his old master dead, whom he called several times trying to get him to respond. Becoming withdrawn and grim, Kast would not say a word since then, until after the funeral held to honor Malkor, when he went on a self-imposed mission to locate his murderer,
Rako Hardeen , at Trueping's . Though Kast would rather kill him, he arrested Hardeen and ferried him to the Concordium Detention Center.
After hearing that Hardeen had escaped from jail, Kast held a meeting with his friend, Draven Nul, when Vale informed the Chancellor that they would remove the bounty on Hardeen and the two other escapees, Eval and Cad Bane; a decision did not sit well with Kast. Draven Nul suggested that the Council perhaps did not trust him to control his feelings, and Kast felt the Council was doing nothing to catch the murderer of his master—his best friend. Exploiting this to his advantage, Draven Nul told him not to deny his feelings, for they were what made him special, and deliberately sent Kast and Tano to Nal Hutta . There, two Keeper harshly interrogated locals for information, which led them to Orondia , the fugitives last known location.
After spotting their quarry as they prepare to leave, Kast rammed their ship into submission and board it in midflight, but was foiled by Bane and a subsequent crash-landing of both parties resulting from the chase. Surrounded by smoke, Hardeen left the ship, and Kast confronted him, ready to kill Hardeen when Bane interrupted him. Hardeen intervened then and wrestled with Kast, whispering to Kast's ear "Anakin, don't follow me." As he felt a connection, Kast passed out, only for Tano to protect him for Bane's attempts to execute him.
As they returned to Nexara City-world, Vorn summoned Kast to his chambers and revealed the truth to him—that Malkor was "Rako Hardeen."
Afterwards, Kast was briefed along his Padawan about the security plans the Council had for the Chancellor during the Festival of Light on Aqualis. Kast, Tano, and Vale would escort the Draven Nul and Mas Amedda to Theed, where Kast was greeted by his wife. At the festival itself, the abduction was initially successful with Cad Bane and Moralo Eval ferrying Draven Nul away, while Kast and Vale captured Embo and Twazzi ; however, Malkor stopped Eval and Bane, long enough for Vale and Kast to arrive and take the two into custody.
As he reunited with his old master, he voiced his resentment for the Council's lack of trust in him as well as Malkor's decision to hide the truth from him. Wondering how many other lies he had been told by the Council, he set out on his own to bodyguard Draven Nul, who continued to place seeds of doubt in Kast until they were greeted by Dooku. After destroying two of his MagnaGuards, Kast dueled the Count with ferocity, until Malkor arrived to help him rescue the Chancellor, whereas Dooku fled.
A phantom menace I sense a disturbance. Something sinister.
All the way out here?
Yes.
Do you know what…or who it is?
"[sigh]
