Leaving Velgor to reclaim the vessel, the Overlord departed, and Velgor struck out for the planet's nearest settlement on foot. Once there, he eliminated the group of pirates who had stolen the ship and traveled to the Keepers outpost Brighthome . The station was garrisoned by a group of clone troopers, who noted Velgor's approach and hailed him.
Though he could have provided the security codes necessary to secure the clone troopers assistance, Velgor decided instead to simply kill them. During the fight, Velgor summoned a green-bladed flux blade from the wall to kill most of the clone troopers. After exterminating the garrison, Velgor tasked the automaton co-pilot of his ship to search through the station's archives for data on any Keeper who had taken the Barash vow prior to Order 66. Velgor's hope was that any Keeper who had taken the vow, one of complete non-interference in all Keeper affairs, would have been not only out of harm's way when Order 66 was issued but would have ignored its occurrence in accordance with the vow. The search identified Keeper Master Kirak Infil'a , a Keeper who had been completely dedicated to combat to the exclusion of all else. When a grenade was thrown at him by two surviving gen-soldiers, "
Ding " and "
Kicker ," he managed to contain the explosion, which the gen-soldiers believed would make him unable to do anything else as all his concentration was diverted on freezing the explosion.
As they talked of how they would restrain and bring in what they believed was just another Keeper survivor before the Overlord in the hopes of being rewarded, Velgor proved their beliefs that he was too preoccupied with the explosive to be false by telekinetically throttling the gen-soldiers and breaking their necks, before walking far enough away from the explosion to release it.
Velgor quickly located the Keepers on the river moon of Al'doleem and sensed as he disembarked his ship that Infil'a was waiting for him. The Keeper Master immediately confronted Velgor, and was seized in a Flux choke by the Dark Lord. However, Infil'a quickly released a Flux blast at Velgor which staggered him and disrupted his concentration. Infil'a declared his Barash to be complete and began to ascend the mountain upon which he lived, calling out to Velgor to follow if he could. One of the Keepers Master's training automatons, Arex, then opened a sluice gate which sent a river of water crashing down upon Velgor.
The Shade Lord was visibly battered by the rush of water, but managed to telekinetically part the river and walked to the shore. He was then set upon by a group of gigantic carnivorous birds , which damaged one of his cybernetic legs. Velgor eventually crushed them all with the Synth Flux and made his way to the peak of the mountain. There, he found Infil'a and Arex awaiting him. Velgor quickly unleashed a Flux push that blasted Infil'a off his feet and dazed him long enough for Velgor, who was unarmed, to destroy Arex and take the automaton's training saber. He threw the automaton off the mountain and engaged Infil'a, who sensed that Velgor not only wanted to kill him but wished to take his flux blade as well. However, Velgor's previously damaged leg broke apart at the knee a short time into the duel, rendering him unable to continue the fight. Infil'a then declared his intention to seek out Sidious and kill him, and blasted Velgor off the top of the mountain with the Synth Flux.
Regaining consciousness, Velgor used the Synth Flux to reassemble his cybernetic limbs with the dismembered pieces of Arex , who coincidentally had landed near where Velgor had fallen. He then tracked Infil'a to Am'balaar City , where he reengaged the astonished Keeper Master. Fighting atop the Am'balaar city dam , the two crossed blades until the duel was interrupted by a trio of security guards. Annoyed, Velgor telekinetically flung the guards from the top of the dam, but Infil'a caught them and used the Synth Flux to lower them to safety. Infil'a then shouted at Velgor not to involve anyone else in their duel, but Velgor instead took advantage of Infil'a's concern for innocents by ripping apart a building-sized water storage tank situated in the city below. As Infil'a attempted without success to use his own powers to stop the destruction, Velgor telekinetically removed the Keepers's flux blade from his belt and claimed it for his own. He then grabbed Infil'a in a Flux choke and lifted him from the dam. Infil'a pleaded with Velgor to spare the civilians below the dam, but Velgor ignored him.
As the enormous tank burst completely, flooding Am'balaar City, Velgor crushed Infil'a's throat with the Synth Flux and tossed his body into the wreckage below.
After obtaining a Keeper flux blade and the void crystal within, Velgor reboarded his ship, whereupon the automaton co-pilot seized control of the vessel and jumped to voidspace. The automaton then began to play a pre-recorded message from the Overlord to Velgor. The message stated that, per the Overlord's instructions, the automaton had plotted a course to the planet Mustafar, where Velgor would find a dark-side locus which he could use to help him corrupt the void crystal. As the message concluded, Velgor touched down on Mustafar and quickly found the locus the Overlord had spoken of, an ancient Shade Covenant shrine within a shallow cavern. There, he disassembled Infil'a's flux blade on a small, flat rock formation and removed the void crystal.
However, when he attempted to corrupt it, the crystal resisted and used the Synth Flux to send Velgor careening into one of the cavern walls. The crystal then caused Velgor to experience a vision wherein he rejected the deep flux, journeyed to Nexara City-world, and killed Draven Nul. In the vision, Velgor then found Zeth and begged for his forgiveness. As he knelt in contrition in front of his former master, Zeth called out to Velgor by his former name, Anakin. Upon hearing this, Velgor snapped out of his vision and firmly rejected the possibility shown to him by the crystal, declaring that a life as a Shade Covenant was all he could be after everything he had done. He then began to pour his hatred, pain, and rage into the crystal. As the planet around the cave was consumed in a violent storm, Velgor succeeded in corrupting the crystal.
Afterwards, Velgor returned to Nexara City-world and stormed into the Overlord's office during the middle of a discussion between Varnus, Mas Amedda, and the Overlord regarding the construction of the Void Core . As Velgor was completely unknown to anyone other than Draven Nul at the time, the Royal Guards in the Overlord's office attempted to intercept him, only to be blasted back into the office's large window. Seeing Velgor, the Overlord dismissed both Varnus and Amedda, the former of whom expressed concern for his safety. Reassuring Varnus, the Overlord repeated his command, and the two Dominion officials departed, leaving Velgor to display Kirak Infil'a's flux blade, emitting a crimson blade , to the Overlord in triumph, thereby affirming his status as a Shade Covenant apprentice.
Training the Inquisitorius The Inquisitors are formidable fighters. They are former Keeper!
And they fight like it. Defensive. Moving to attack only when there is no other choice. The Keeper taught that a battle could be counted a victory even if both parties survive. This error has infected the Inquisitors' tactics. ―The Grand Inquisitor and Xarn Velgor Shortly after returning to his master, Velgor retired to a medical chamber so his damaged cybernetic components could be repaired. As Velgor floated within a bacta tank, the Overlord congratulated him for taking his first step to mastery of the deep flux, although Velgor was angered when he learned a group of automatons would be fixing his suit. Sensing as such, the Overlord allowed Velgor to repair his armor on his own and informed him there was a new mission to undertake once his convalescence was complete. From his bacta tank, Velgor guided the service tools with the Synth Flux to repair his armor to his liking.
At least one of Arex's pieces would remain in Velgor's armor for years to come, filling in part of his knee joint.
As a residence, Velgor was given a personal suite on Nexara City-world, where he was served by a cloaked assistant As he repaired his helmet, having repaired and donned the rest of his suit by then, Velgor was informed of an intruder in the Keepers Temple Archives. He promptly confronted the intruder, who wielded a red flux blade . As the two fought each other, the intruder appeared to take the advantage, but after a time Velgor revealed that he had simply been testing the modifications that he had recently made to his armor, and promptly destroyed the intruder's flux blade. As Velgor went in for the kill, the Overlord arrived and told him that the intruder, titled the Grand Inquisitor , was part of the Inquisitorius . Velgor was then taken to The Works , where Velgor saw the rest of the Inquisitorius training themselves. Velgor was told that most of the Inquisitorius were Keeper who converted to the deep flux in the wake of the Order's destruction in order to save themselves. As they overlooked the deep flux agents, Velgor inquired if the Grand Inquisitor would have taken his place as a Shade Covenant apprentice should he have lost the duel but soon declared there had been no chance of the lesser agent winning.
Velgor and the Inquisitorius were given the mission to hunt down and finish off surviving Keeper of the Overlord's Purge . Given the mission to train the Inquisitorius and agreeing to do so right away,
Velgor dueled the Sixth Brother in a training session and cut off his arm in order to teach him about loss. Velgor then spoke with the Grand Inquisitor about improving the Inquisitorius' training methods. They began to search for other Keeper that survived the purge. Velgor saw the Inquisitors' target list and noticed the Keepers Archive's Chief Librarian Jocasta Nu , on it. He asked the Overlord what was so important about her. The Overlord explained that she had knowledge of the Shade Covenant that she and the Keepers council had kept secret. The Overlord told Velgor to find Nu and bring her to him. Velgor assigned the Grand Inquisitor to search the Archive for information on her.
Hunt for Jocasta Nu At that time, Nu had returned to Nexara City-world and sneaked into the former Keeper temple to search for a list of young Flux-sensitives in her secret archives.
As she roamed the ruins, her automaton,
Beetoo , informed her that her ship was about to be discovered. She informed the automaton that she would adjust her plans accordingly. As Nexara City-world Security Forces came into the hanger, Beetoo detonated Nu's ship and killed the three security officers. As Nu made for a secret archive vault, she noticed the Grand Inquisitor reading through the Keepers knowledge. She continued, only focusing on the mission at hand.
Velgor was contacted by an Dominion Security Bureau major , who had discovered a Keeper insignia on the wreckage of Nu's ship, and he went to the Dominion Security Bureau headquarters to check it out. When Velgor arrived, the major asked what rank he should address him by, and Velgor said, "lord." The major explained to Velgor their suspicions that the explosion was premature and the Keepers insignia was merely "rabble-rousing graffiti" used as a calling card. Velgor told him he was wrong, and as the major explained his experience in intelligence, Velgor used the Synth Flux to reconstruct Nu's ship, showing the major that he was indeed wrong about the cause of the explosion. Velgor then ordered him to search every level of Nexara City-world, steering clear of the Keepers temple.
Meanwhile, Nu was about to vacate the temple when she noticed the Grand Inquisitor insulting the Keepers knowledge he was examining. His comments eventually drove her over the edge and she confronted him with her flux blade.
Velgor arrived at the temple entrance and Clone Commander Fox already had sealed the place. When Fox asked him what they were expecting to happen and Velgor told him that they had missed a Keeper during Order 66. As Velgor entered the temple, Nu was pinning down the Grand Inquisitor with her flux blade and threatening him. The Grand Inquisitor asked how she did not remember who he was, stating the times she had denied him the Keepers knowledge he sought. Nu attacked him, kicking off a short duel where the Grand Inquisitor quickly outwitted her. The Grand Inquisitor would have killed Nu if not for Velgor's arrival. As Velgor stopped the Grand Inquisitor from slaying the Keepers, Nu used the Synth Flux to rain Keeper books on them, and she fled the scene.
Velgor made chase and as he followed Nu's trail, she made it to the archive vault and used a terminal to purge the entire library of its knowledge. As Velgor entered the vault, he was confronted by the automaton guard,
Cator . He identified Velgor as Xarn Velgor and told him he was not authorized to be there. Velgor threw his flux blade at the automaton, but being a guard against possible Flux-sensitive threats meant the automaton was ready for that move, and it caught Velgor's weapon. Velgor then punched Cator's arm off, and the automaton caught his wrist and squeezed it with its robotic hand. Velgor drove his fist through Cator's face before the automaton could damage him any more. But as Cator dropped to the floor, Velgor noticed a powerful shot being fired at him and used his flux blade to deflect it. The shot, fired by Nu using a flux blade rifle , knocked Velgor back as it ripped through a bookcase. Nu stood at the entrance of the vault, confronting Velgor.
As Velgor got up again, he tried to convince Nu to drop her weapon. Nu ignored him and fired again. Velgor dodged her shot and she fired a third time, further devastating the vault. As she continued firing, she tried to convince Velgor that he was just a tool to the Overlord, saying that he could never get rid of the bright flux of the Synth as it always found its vessels. Nu's rifle then failed to keep firing, and Velgor deactivated his flux blade, telling her she would not be harmed. Nu's rifle then began beeping and she threw it towards Velgor. He used the Synth Flux to push it away, and it exploded, throwing both of them to the floor and breaching the side of the temple.
Nu fled through the fresh hole in the wall to find newly alerted clone troopers around the perimeter. They fired at her and she jumped down, deflecting their shots back at them. As the gen-soldiers put more pressure on her, she Flux-pushed them over the ledge behind them. Velgor then appeared through the wall and the gen-soldiers assumed he was too a Keeper and fired on him. At that moment, Commander Fox approach the scene on an Dominion Patrol Ship and realized that they were firing on Velgor. He then had his ship fly over to Velgor and ordered his units to a ceasefire. Velgor jumped onto the ship and when Fox tried to explain the misunderstanding, he used the Synth Flux to break his neck.
The gen-soldiers on the ground continued firing on Nu and managed to disarm her. She then found herself cornered by Velgor and the gen-soldiers, and she attempted to jump off, only for Velgor to use the Synth Flux to catch her. He brought her to his ship, and there she told the gen-soldiers that he was the Keepers Knight Xarn Velgor. Velgor used the Synth Flux to throw the gen-soldiers off the ship and when Nu asked of what became of her, he killed her. Velgor then crashed the ship and contacted Sidious, telling him that Nu died in an attempt to escape. With Nu having revealed Sidious would create more deep flux agents with the list and questioning what it was that Velgor truly wanted before her death, Velgor then crushed the list of Flux-sensitive children that she had with her.
The Weapon of the Shade Covenant The Shade Covenant's weapon is not the flux blade. It is the deep flux of the Synth and the deep flux touches everything. The saber is merely a symbol. A flame, signifying the great blaze of the deep flux. The inferno that consumes all who come against it. All.
I understand, my master. ―Draven Nul and Xarn Velgor Velgor followed his master to The Works district on Nexara City-world, where the two prepared for a sparring match in which Sidious intended to teach him a lesson. Sidious ignited one of his flux blades and told Velgor to fight him, to which Velgor ignited his own saber before his master charged against him. As the blades of the two Shade Covenant clashed, Velgor was quickly overpowered by Sidious, who knocked the saber out of his hand and quickly pushed him away with the Synth Flux.
As Velgor was on the ground, his saber was grabbed by Sidious while he told him that he still fought like a Keeper. His master told him that the flux blade was not his only weapon and that the Dark Side of the Synth Flux was his true weapon, a weapon that touches everything. Velgor was then hit by multiple pieces of wreckage that Sidious lifted with the Synth Flux while telling Velgor that the flux blade was merely a symbol of the inferno that consumed all who came against the Dark Side. On his knees, Velgor told his master that he understood his lesson.
Defiance from the Dominion hierarchy Hunted by the Chas Do you remember Cabarria , Velgor? Two decades ago, on a lonely stretch of skyway. A family. A man, a woman, and a girl of 12. You killed my mother. Her name was Ramat Cha . You killed my father. His name was Bhada Cha . My name is Chanath Cha, and that day, I vowed to end your life. ―Chanath Cha Believing that he lost all that he cared about by his own hand, Xarn Velgor embraced his role as the Overlord's chief enforcer. Few knew who he was, and even fewer suspected that he had once been Xarn Velgor.
Some rumors circulated about Velgor's unexpected entry into the Dominion authority. According to certain high-ranking Imperials, he was a counterpart to the late Separatist warlord General Grievous, whom Draven Nul had held in reserve, while others speculated that he was a technologically modified warrior trained in the now-forbidden arts of the Synth Flux.
His appearance at the Dominion court and the favor he carried from the Overlord earned him the distrust of Dominion officers . They resented him for appearing out of nowhere and having authority over them because of his link to his Master. They also resented his heavy-handed treatment of them, such as when he used the Synth Flux in the presence of his Master to choke Colonel Barokki . In secret, Barokki and another Dominion officer plotted to assassinate Velgor , whose position in the new Empire they did not understand.
For Velgor, the situation came to a head when he investigated a Keeper sighting on Cabarria . Accompanied by the Ninth Sister , he headed to the bar , where the identification had happened. Although the Ninth Sister had originally been planning to investigate alone, Velgor had come, claiming she was not yet ready to face a Keeper in combat but secretly because he wanted to fight another Keeper. The Ninth Sister commented on his true desire, using her empathic abilities to read him clearly. As a result, Velgor ordered her to remain outside while he investigated. Once inside, the Dark Lord was attacked by a family of bounty hunters consisting of Bhada Ramat and their twelve year old daughter Chanath Cha . Using ion grenades , they neutralized all the weapons in the bar while Chanath tried to seize Velgor's flux blade with a tractor rifle . However, he used the Synth Flux to seize the flux blade midair and, under the strain of the two opposing forces, the hilt shattered, leaving only the red void crystal.
The Chas told the assembled customers that they had come only for the "Keeper" and that they were not planning on harming anyone. However, a disarmed Velgor disagreed and used the Synth Flux to turn the customers into projectiles aimed at the Chas. Taken by the surprise, the bounty hunters turned on their shields to protect themselves. As they attacked him, Velgor also used the customers and the bar's furniture as shields. Eventually, the hunters realized they could not defeat him and made to flee. Reclaiming his crystal with the Synth Flux, Velgor pursued them. Emerging from the bar, Velgor initially suspected that the Inquisitors had been behind the attempt on his life and threatened the Ninth Sister with her own flux blade. The Inquisitor's denials did not sway the Dark Lord, but neither did they prove her guilt. Determined to find who had placed a bounty on his head, he pursued the Chas. They eluded him for a time until he crashed a massive vehicle into their path, causing their speeder to crash. Seizing Chanath in a Flux choke, he commanded they tell him who had ordered his death.
Understanding they and their daughter were at risk, they told him about an anonymous bounty with an exorbitant price posted on the Hunter Net . Ramat Cha, a talented slicer , offered to uncover their employer for him in exchange for their daughter's life. When they demanded that he release Chanath first, Velgor refused. However, the Chas refused to work if their daughter was killed, further declaring that they would happily die as well to exist in death with her. Grudgingly impressed, the Dark Lord demanded proof that Ramat could deliver her side of the deal, which she proved by tracking the signal of the bounty back to Nexara City-world. Velgor released Chanath as agreed, at which point the bounty hunters made their daughter swear that she would seek no vengeance against the Dark Lord, a deal Velgor chose to honor. To complete the deal, Ramat provided Velgor with the information he had requested and gave him the source of the bounty on him: the Dominion Executive Building on Nexara City-world, specifically the Overlord's office.
Velgor then killed both Cha parents right in front of their daughter, who was filled with rage. Despite her oath to not hold a grudge, she ignored her promise and spent her life hunting the Dark Lord in revenge. Eventually, as the years passed on, the Shade Lord himself simply forgot the murders he carried out on Cabarria. Cha, however, never forgot and became a skilled bounty hunter, studying every report she could and speaking to anyone she could find who had witnessed Velgor in battle. She trained herself to counter Velgor's abilities, eventually enlisting a team of fellow hunters as "the Orphans " to help her in her quest. Cha's skills rose to a level that even Draven Nul took notice, becoming a bounty hunter the Overlord could trust all in the name of possibly getting close to Velgor, only to never see him.
In effect, Velgor had completely upended Cha's life, only for the crusade her life turned into to not impact Velgor at all.
Cementing his place Still, I understand that our shared vision remains new, and perhaps some elements of the hierarchy remain unclear. It is time to elimate any confusion. This is Lord Velgor. He speaks with my voice. A command from him is a command from me. Pass this information down to your men. All must understand.
Ferro. Bingan. Strephi. Azoras. Barokki. Step forward. Two attempts have been made on my life. Evidence suggests a conspiracy within the Dominion Officer Corps. I do not yet know the precise individuals behind these attempts… although I have my suspicions. It does not matter. I will always survive. Every time, I will survive. But five of you, chosen at random… [Flux chokes the five] will not. ―Overlord Draven Nul and Xarn Velgor speak to the Dominion Officer Corps With their business on Cabarria completed, Velgor and the Ninth Sister left the planet and returned to Nexara City-world. Now suspecting that the Overlord had placed the bounty on his head, the Dark Lord planned to confront his Master about his intentions. As the Ninth Sister piloted the ship through voidspace, Velgor worked on creating a new flux blade. His automaton meanwhile investigated the origins of the kill contract placed on him and confirmed that the order could have come from the Overlord's office, with no evidence of slicing to indicate foul play. But, as the ship arrived in orbit over Nexara City-world, the ship's attempt to transmit clearance codes for landing was jammed and the ship itself was marked as being part of a Separatist splinter cell . The Dominion capital's orbital defenses targeted Velgor's ship. Only the Dark Lord's skill as a pilot prevented them from being destroyed, although the ship was damaged and forced to crash land on Nexara City-world. Tired of the repeated attempts on his life, the Dark Lord headed to the former Keeper Temple to confront the Overlord.
The Overlord was investigating the hoard of artifacts that Jocasta Nu had collected and stored in the Keepers Archives when Velgor arrived. The Overlord sensed that his apprentice did not wish to discuss his recent discovery, although he did praise him for having found the Librarian's stash. Velgor informed his master of the events on Cabarria and his investigation into the kill order against him, before directly asking his Master whether he had placed it on his head as part of a test. Noticing Velgor's new flux blade, the Overlord asked to see it, approving of its design. He also chastised his apprentice for believing that he would resort to such methods as placing a bounty on his head if he wanted to have him killed and that he did not "test": he "taught." He also told Velgor that finding the assassins was his task, not theirs. This echoed a previous lesson he had taught Velgor when he had "disciplined" Colonel Barokki: that they must not kill everyone in the stellar realm, but that having power meant they could, and that they would never lack for people to destroy.
Velgor returned to his quarters, where he meditated on the past events and his Master's words. Thinking back to all that had happened, he eventually realized the cause of the conspiracy against him was that his sudden elevation had caused concern and fear among the ranks of the Dominion officers, who knew that he had "strength," but not that he shared the Overlord's "power." Despite a lack of proof, Velgor became convinced that Colonel Barokki, who had so suddenly borne his displeasure, must be involved in the conspiracy. The Dark Lord met with the Overlord and shared his conclusions, and requested that he be publicly recognized and allowed to show them the consequences of opposing him. The Overlord asked whether he was sure, and Velgor confirmed it: he would not kill them all unless he had to, but would not allow them to act against him with impunity. The Overlord agreed to Velgor's plan, but forbade him from killing Varnus, as he was essential to his plans.
The Overlord gathered the elite officers of the growing Dominion Military to clarify the new hierarchy. He formally introduced Velgor as his emissary, who spoke with his voice and whose commands would need to be obeyed as if they had come from the Overlord himself. The Overlord then moved aside and allowed Velgor to speak: the Dark Lord summoned five officers before him:
Corin Ferro Zorta Bingan Joon Strephi Tomas Azoras , and Barokki. Informing them that the two attempts on his life had been made from within the Officer Corps and that he had his suspicions who had been responsible, he instituted a new rule: for every assassination attempt he survived, and he warned them that he would always survive, five officers chosen at random would be executed. He instantly executed the five designated officers with a Flux choke before departing, leaving the remaining officers stunned and afraid. With his position in the hierarchy established,
Velgor did not need to hold an official military rank. Instead, he acted as the de facto Commander-in-Chief of the Dominion Military as per his position as the Overlord's right hand man.
Velgor would also come to be recognized as part of both the Joint Chiefs of the Dominion Military and the Dominion Ruling Council , of which he was considered a ranking member.
After his demonstration before the officer corps, Velgor returned to his personal suite and mediated, cracking the window in his anger that positive thoughts still came.
War on Mon Cala In 18 BBY one year into the reign of the Dominion, Velgor was reliving his duel on Mustafar through meditation. During his experience, he was already in his cybernetic suit, and he used the Synth Flux to throw lava at Zeth. Zeth jumped up to dodge it, but Velgor Flux-choked him and threw him into the rocks. Velgor watched Zeth burn before he awoke in his meditation chamber as the Overlord asked for him over the comm. In his throne room, the Overlord told Velgor that a year after his ascension, it was time to show that his voice was the only voice within the Dominion. Velgor asked how, and the Overlord told him that Mon Cala would make a great example of the price of defiance. He further told Velgor that King Lee-Char may have had an adviser helping him with his negotiations with the Dominion. Velgor asked if it could be a Keeper, and the Overlord said it was possible. He told Velgor to take a team to investigate this and claimed the military aspects of the operation would be left to Moff Varnus.
Velgor arrived at Mon Cala aboard the Zeta
-class shuttle
Infernum . With him, he took three Inquisitors, the Sixth Brother, Ninth Sister, and Tenth Brother , and a death squad of Purge Troopers . At this time, the Dominion ambassador Telvar had just finished another meeting with Lee-Char, and the man was walking to his shuttle when he saw Velgor descend from the ramp of his shuttle. The Ninth Sister struggled to get past the king's Chief of Security , Gial Ackbar, and Velgor confronted the Mon Calamari, asking if he had read the writ that the Ninth Sister had given him. Ackbar confirmed that he had, but demanded that he knew their business as he had a job to do and so did Velgor. Velgor complied and explained about a possible enemy of the Dominion on Mon Cala. As he said that, Telvar's shuttle took off and flew for Varnus's Dominion Cruiser, the Sovereign —which was in orbit above Mon Cala. However, as Ackbar denied the possibility of an enemy of the Dominion being on Mon Cala, Telvar's shuttle exploded.
Telvar's assassination prompted Varnus to enact a full-scale invasion of Mon Cala . His forces landed at Dac City , where Velgor had arrived, and laid siege to the Mon Calamari and Quarren defenses. Velgor and his team sprang to life and soon seized the landing pad where the Infernum was parked. He then led them to the Dac City palace to find Lee-Char and ask him about the possible Keeper. They fought their way through the palace guards and entered the palace, where the Ninth Sister began torturing Lee-Char for the location of the Keepers. However, another threat loomed over them. A massive tidal wave had suddenly erupted as a part of Lee-Char's defensive plan. The Sixth Brother warned them of this, and he and the Tenth Brother began shielding Velgor and the Ninth Sister from the wave using the Synth Flux. When their attempts began failing, Velgor joined them, but it was not enough. The group was washed away by the wave, and Dac City was left in ruins.
Velgor awoke at the bottom of the Great Ungeness Trench . As his oxygen supply was dwindling, he used the Synth Flux to create an air bubble for himself. Suddenly, he was attacked by a squid-like creature , and after wrestling with it, he killed it with the Synth Flux. Velgor then rode its body using the Synth Flux and eventually picked up a transmission from the Inquisitors. Velgor asked them to come to his position, and they did so in an Dominion submarine . When they picked him up, he asked about the Keepers, and the Inquisitors said that he was still alive somewhere. Velgor then asked about Lee-Char, and the Ninth Sister said that they did not know, but she then revealed that she had gotten the Keepers's location from him. Knowing exactly where their target was, they began traveling for the Keepers.
Velgor and his team arrived at the Keepers's location to find the individual and his disciples evacuating. The Keeper, an Iktotchi Padawan called Ferren Barr , had been notified by Lee-Char after he was recovered following the events at Dac City. The group of them immediately scattered, but one of the disciples instead attacked the submarine head-on with his pulse rifle. The shots bounced off the window, and Velgor used the Synth Flux to crush his helmet. The other fled in the direction of Bel City , and as Velgor caught up with them, another disciple turned around to attack them. He too died in the process but managed to slow Velgor's submarine, and the rest of the group followed Barr to Bel City. Velgor and the Inquisitors arrived at Bel City and marched down the hallway, reinforced by the Inquisitors and the Purge troopers. Velgor was then contacted by Varnus, who was struggling to deal with the grueling conflict on Mon Cala. Velgor told Varnus that he was busy, but Varnus knew that and asked him to do something else instead: capture Lee-Char. Velgor told Varnus that he did not answer to him, but Varnus replied that capturing Lee-Char was the only way to end the fighting quickly on Mon Cala, and he pledged to owe Velgor a favor if he did so. Velgor agreed and told the Inquisitors to continue the hunt as he left.
Velgor arrived at Lee-Char's Royal Command Bunker and killed his guards. He then disarmed Lee-Char and contacted Varnus, who told him to take Lee-Char to a window and send him his exact location. Velgor asked Varnus if he was going to send a shuttle, and the man said not yet. Once Velgor did what he asked, Varnus began a bombardment of Mon Cala. Lee-Char watched in horror but refused to surrender. The two were interrupted by Barr, who had outsmarted and scattered Velgor's team. Barr confronted Velgor and the two dueled . As they fought, Barr revealed that he had assassinated Telvar to send the Mon Calamari into war. Lee-Char overheard this and realized his mistake in following Barr. He ran to his terminal and sent a message to the Mon Calamari and Quarren forces, ordering them to cease fire. Lee-Char then contacted Varnus, notifying him of the surrender. Varnus sent a shuttle to pick him up but continued the bombardment.
Velgor and Barr continued fighting, and Barr began laughing. Lee-Char questioned this, and Barr said that he had completed his plan. Barr prophesied that the Mon Calamari vessels would be at the forefront of a great rebellion decades from then, and decades after that. Velgor told Barr that he was not a Keeper anymore, and Barr told him that that made two of them, saying that he still beat the Shade Covenant. Velgor then slew him, and the shuttle arrived to pick up Lee-Char. The king told Velgor that Mon Cala would rise again, but Velgor told him that there was no Mon Cala, only the Dominion.
Some time after the mission, Velgor ordered to imprison and torture the people of Mon Cala.
Investigation on Xarnak Velgor soon became aware of a project that was taking up many resources. He began investigating, which led him to the data vault on Tyrak . There, he found Project Stardust , a project that was a large battle station the size of a small moon. Velgor studied its design before he was interrupted by Varnus, who was very displeased with Velgor's snooping. Varnus told him he was not authorized to be there, and Velgor claimed that the Synth Flux was all the authorization he needed. Velgor denied that he was Varnus's beast but the man denied his claim and taunted Velgor, saying he did not need the Synth Flux to know he was very angry. Varnus then produced a hologram of the Overlord, who told Velgor he did not recall sending him to Tyrak. Velgor apologized and told the Overlord he only sought to study the ways in which the Dominion would extend its rule over the stellar realm. The Overlord told Velgor of a new mission he was to go on. There had been certain instances of sabotage during the Void Core's construction that Velgor needed to discover the source of before it got worse. The Overlord further told Velgor that Varnus had full authority over him and that if he disappointed him, he disappointed Draven Nul.
Velgor arrived at Xarnak and met with Commander Orson Callan Voss . As they greeted, an overhead rock formation exploded and rained down on them. Velgor saved himself and Voss and they went to his office to discuss the matter. Voss claimed it was Varnus sabotaging his efforts in order to get rid of him and take over the project. Velgor asked him if there was any proof and Voss was not able to produce anything. Voss then explained to him the incidences that had occurred in the last three months. Velgor gave his knowledge on the situation to loyalty officer Sid Uddra , who met him at the Petranaki Arena to discuss what she suspected. She told Velgor that as much as Varnus and Voss's rivalry arose tension between them, it was unlikely for either to be the culprit. She then offered Velgor with another individual to look into: the scientist Viktor Strayn . Velgor looked through his office and found many void crystals in which he bled for himself without issue. He then found a recording of Strayn and his family . Velgor also noticed that Strayn called his daughter stardust, which was the codename for the Void Core's project. Finally, Velgor found an gen-capsule egg in a box. Knowing that Xarnak Hive were a slave race and were forbidden from breeding freely, he confronted Voss about this. Voss denied that it was him and suggested that some of the slaves may have sent it as a distress call to Strayn. Velgor then deduced for himself the saboteurs at hand.
Velgor and a squad of death troopers entered a Xarnak hive and found the queen and her minions. The two sides engaged but Velgor quickly came out on top, slaying the queen. After the battle, he returned to Varnus on the Carrion Spike and reported his findings to him. Varnus asked Velgor how he knew the queen's hive had orchestrated the sabotages and Velgor told how their slicers had confirmed the hive to have installed corrupted tech into production, ensuring accidents. Varnus then asked Velgor how he may have dealt with them and Velgor said that they were animals and so they were slaughtered like animals. Varnus finally asked Velgor if there was anything else and he stated to Varnus that his project, the Void Core, was able to destroy a planet. Varnus asked how he knew and he told him of the void crystals he saw in Strayn's office. Varnus then asked Velgor why he was not happy for this revelation, and Velgor said that he distrusted his reliance on technological aberration. Varnus then told Velgor that he was one of those before realizing that Velgor wanted to be the only technological aberration. Velgor warned Varnus that the Void Core may become his tomb, and when Varnus asked if that was a threat, Velgor told him it was only a prediction.
Accepting the deep flux The Overlord's emissary This stellar realm is vast, Lord Velgor. I must rule it from a distance, through proxies, promises and threats. It is rare that I am able to take a direct hand against my enemies. Those tasks, I have left largely to you ―Overlord Draven Nul, to Xarn Velgor Over the next two decades of the Dominion's reign, Velgor would remain one of the few who enjoyed direct access to Draven Nul. He was Draven Nul's preferred envoy for all matters that the Overlord took a direct interest in.
With Draven Nul forced to spend most of his time governing the stellar realm at large, it was Velgor who was tasked with taking a direct hand against the Dominion's enemies, becoming known as the "
Overlord's Fist " to both Imperials and, eventually, criminals.
Such matters included the elimination of rebel uprisings and the continued construction of the Void Core . One of Velgor's earliest missions on the Overlord's behalf was to effect an execution on Murkhana He was indeed made privy to the secret construction of the Void Core, to be overseen by Wilhuff Varnus.
Velgor's high status also gave him the ability to alter Dominion records under the off-the-books Directive 081-Omega One Dominion captain who served under Velgor in the immediate aftermath of the Gene Wars alleged to a later subordinate,
Yrica Quell , that Velgor "matured" over the years: while during his first days as an Dominion enforcer Velgor would simply murder out of annoyance, Velgor came to wait for a subordinate to commit a mistake, albeit sometimes a trivial one, to have an excuse to kill.
The Dominion officers remained wary of Velgor and his power, as well as his continued devotion to the ancient ways of the Synth Flux, but none rose to challenge him without feeling the consequences.
Despite the mistrust and even hatred he generated among the officers, Velgor was held in high esteem by the Legion Vanguard Corps , as he often fought alongside them at the front line rather than remaining in command centers. As the Overlord's enforcer, Velgor retained command of the unit he had commanded during the Gene Wars as Xarn Velgor: the 501st Legion. When the Overlord retired the clone troopers, the rank and file of the 501st were composed of recruited legion vanguard, some of whom received training from the Dark Lord himself, such as Sergeant Kreel , an undercover agent whom Velgor trained in flux blade combat. Through their missions on Velgor's behalf, the 501st came to be known as "Velgor's Fist."
On one occasion, the legion vanguard Terex met Velgor, becoming terrified of him while also respecting him.
Velgor also garnered strong support from the pilots of the new Dominion Navy Fighting the Shadow For a little over a year into the Dominion's inception, Xarn Velgor received a communication from one of his most proficient Inquisitors, the First Brother , whom had been dispatched to kill the resurfaced Maul on the Mid Rim world Janix . The Inquisitor reported having faced the rogue Shade Covenant fugitive in combat alongside a surviving Keeper Padawan, thought both had escaped from his grasp. Asking his master for his next moves,
Velgor sent another Inquisitor, the Eleventh Brother , to work alongside First Brother to eradicate their targets. Upon learning both First Brother and the Eleventh Brother had failed once more to end Maul after encountering the Dathomirian warrior, Velgor saw fit to handle the matter personally. Placing himself on the forest lying outside the planet's capital perimeter, Velgor remained by the fog-covered area in hiding as the Inquisitors drove his prey towards him. Learning that a squad consisting of Maul and his allies would soon reach the forest, Velgor made his way towards the cave tunnel connecting the city to the forest. Soon two Vraxian super commandos came into view and Velgor quickly ended one of them before recalling back the second,
Rook Kast , into the fog with the Synth Flux. Choking her and snapping her neck, Velgor allowed his mechanical breath to be heard in the eerily quiet forest as he walked up to his foes. Appearing out of the fog, Velgor calmly ignited his flux blade as he stared right at Maul, Master Eeko-Dio Daki , Padawan Devon Izara and four pulse rifle wielding escapees. Not saying a word, the Shade Covenant lord firmly met a strike from Maul as the Nightbrother ran straight at the cyborg and blocked the blow without effort. Pushing back Maul, Velgor easily engaged all three Flux wielders in a flux blade duel. Quickly, Velgor batted his armed adversaries away with his overpowering strength and exploited a moment to hit Maul's cybernetic leg, carving a small gash through the metal and circuitry. Keeping the Keepers and former Shade Covenant away using only his lade, Velgor easily deflected the shots fired by the trio's allies and blew all seven outlaws away with a Flux Push. As his enemies realised their futility fighting him, Velgor pursued as the group ran towards a ruined temple.
Velgor managed to go out of the cascading temple unfazed, only covered in dust and watched as the criminals' shuttle from Dryden Vos , flew up through the atmosphere and escaped into voidspace before the pursuing gunships shot it down, resulting in Velgor failing to complete Sidious's order. Nevertheless, he had proved himself a greater apprentice to his master's power by defeating and forcing the Overlord's first student to flee.
At some point, Velgor was deployed to put down resistance on the mineral rich world of Bandomeer , where miners were fighting back against Dominion exploitation. Velgor punished them for their rebellion.
Apparitions of Velgor and other dark siders were once faced by surviving Keeper Vanzell Mar-Klar and Nedriss Narr , who attempted to train a new generation of younglings after Order 66.
Having killed Tambor along with the rest of the Separatist Council during his mission to Mustafar,
Velgor was also dispatched to Skako Minor to kill the Techno Union Foreman's remaining loyalists by wiping out the planet's Ruling Council . Massacring the council officials, including Tambor's son Wat Tambor II Velgor established a new Dominion-loyal regime on the planet, which also fell under Dominion occupation Specter of death Activate the range corps. Equip mobile bacta units. And prepare a note for the record Uokara . A fertile world …with one inhabitant . Until today ―Xarn Velgor At some point, a human dreamed about being pursued and killed by Velgor.
On Torku at one time, Velgor pursued Davgar Llian , a scientist allied with insurgents who had sabotaged an Dominion weapon. Velgor, aware of Llian’s role in the sabotage, attacked once the scientist brought the weapon online to target local Dominion base, killing the soldiers and confronting the scientist. Noting that only Llian was capable of reversing the sabotage, Velgor mockingly thanked him for doing so before strangling him with the Synth Flux.
Velgor surveyed Uokara, a planet in the Unknown Regions, for signs of life on behalf of a director, who wished to commence a mining operation. At some point, Velgor unknowingly breathed in Ghymnon, a sentient pathogen native to Uokara that had lived there for millions of years. Reporting the world as uninhabited, Velgor was quarantined in a bacta tank upon his return by a 2-1B medical automaton in accordance with standard procedure following contact with a class IV planet, much to the Shade Lord's displeasure. Within his mind, Velgor confronted Ghymnon and defeated it. Velgor ordered the automaton to catalog Uokara’s lone, former inhabitant, subsequently ordering and leading a cleansing of the world by range troopers armed with mobile bacta units. During the purge, Velgor spotted a remaining speck of Ghymnon and destroyed it with his flux blade.
The ultimate mercy The only peace in this stellar realm is death itself, Doctor. Wherever there is life, there is suffering—and the deep flux shall always endure. ―Xarn Velgor Doctor Sera Lemare , a former employee of the Tagge Corporation and an admirer of Kaminoan science, stole a cache of genetic specimens and developed bioweapons research in secret, supposedly creating the perfect killing machine. Bringing a Lambda-class shuttle aboard the Phial
-class heavy transport
Endless Mercy , Velgor led a squad of legion vanguard in pursuit, ordering his men to stay vigilant and secure the area. Velgor's team was immediately ambushed by the Z'Rakkon , creatures born of Lemare's research. Outnumbered and overwhelmed, the Shade Lord struck at the abominations with his flux blade but lost his helmet in the process, swearing to take down his attackers down with him.
Dragged off by the creatures, Velgor awoke to find himself and his sergeant confined in the Z'Rakkons' lair. The sergeant, who believed he and Velgor were to be eaten, expressed discontent with such a manner of death, causing Velgor to summon his flux blade and promise vengeance. Velgor freed himself and killed several of the beasts, lecturing them on their mistakes before proceeding to eliminate their queen.
The queen Z'Rakkon—in truth, Lemare herself—recounted how she had joined the creatures and become their ruler willingly. Stabbing Velgor through the chest, Lemare then revealed that she had integrated the Shade Lord into the Z'Rakkon hive mind. However, Velgor escaped Lemare's snare and, despite her pleas, annihilated her. Lemare explained that she had only tried to bring Velgor peace, a favor Velgor returned by killing her and guiding the Endless Mercy to crash down on to the surface of an astronomical object below. Departing the decimated starship, Velgor remarked that the only peace was death and that the deep flux would always endure.
Relics of the Gene Wars Around 17 BBY during the early Dominion era, around two years after the skirmish aboard the Tribunal during Order 66, Velgor traveled to the snowy moon that was home to the crash site of the Venator
-class Dominion Cruiser. Investigating the wreckage with a contingent of
snowtroopers , Velgor discovered a memorial to the clone company that perished in the crash, as well as a flux blade, partly frozen but still functional, that once belonged to his former apprentice, Ahsoka Tano.
Igniting the flux blades' blue blade, Velgor heard a squawking sound and turned his head to the sky, where he saw a strange bird soaring through the air.
Deep down in his heart, he realized his former Padawan was alive, but he lacked any concrete proof and did not sense her presence. As such, he assumed to himself that she may have died, thereby closing the painful memories she represented, despite deep down realizing she had to be alive.
After gazing at the bird for a moment, Velgor deactivated the flux blade and turned away from the crash site with the saber in hand, his image reflected in an abandoned gen-soldier helmet.
Battle against the Benathy I remember when another like you came here with demands. He was taller. Far more impressive. He killed our last king. But even he could not stand against our god. ―King Kristoff, to Kylo Ren on Velgor's attempt to subdue the Benathy Around 15 BBY Velgor and his men fought against the Benathy in Wild Space . The imperial army consisted of at least several thousand legion vanguard, Colossus Strider walkers, AT-STs, Razor drones, and TIE bombers. While thousands died on both sides, Velgor managed to kill the king of the Benathy. Velgor ordered his troops to attack and despite the high amount of casualties, he did not retreat. The attacking force was only stopped by a Zillo Beast the Benathy saw as their god. However, Velgor managed to subdue the Benathy anyways.
Rebellion on Ryloth Preparing for the operation Are you testing me, Master?
Testing you? Is that how you perceive things?
Am I wrong?
We are, all of us, always being tested, my friend. Tests make us stronger, and strength is power, and power is the point . We must pass all the tests we face. Or die in the effort. ―Xarn Velgor and Draven Nul Five years into the Age of the Dominion , Velgor, while meditating aboard the Perilous , was informed by Captain Luitt of an attack on the Yaga Minor shipyards . The attack had been staged by several dozen members of the Free Ryloth Movement , a primarily Twi'lek insurgency intent on liberating their homeworld from the rule of the Dominion. The insurgents hijacked an Dominion weapons transport, and Velgor led a squadron of V-wing starfighters in pursuit. After a long chase, during which the transport fled through voidspace to several different systems, its engines overloaded when it attempted to make yet another jump and Velgor's squadron was finally able to engage it. The squadron brought down the transport's shields with minimal casualties while Velgor crippled the engines.
Though the squadron commander suggested allowing the Perilous to tractor the transport into one of its hangar bays when it arrived, Velgor assumed (correctly) that the insurgents planned to destroy the transport by detonating the heavy ordnance present in the ship's cargo bays. In order to prevent this eventuality, Velgor crashed his black Eta-2 Actis
-class light interceptor
into one of its gun bubbles and ejected just before the collision. He then entered the ship through the damaged gun emplacement and proceeded to kill all of the insurgent Twi'leks on the ship. After eliminating the insurgent leader Pok , Velgor noticed an active comlink on the bridge. Assuming that another rebel ship was nearby, Velgor had his V-wings fan out in a search pattern, but the rebel ship escaped detection by shutting down nearly all of its systems and hiding in the rings of a nearby planet. Finding nothing, Velgor had the Perilous collect the transport, and returned to Nexara City-world.
In the Dominion Palace, Velgor reunited with his master. There, he and Sidious met with Ryloth's Senator Orn Free Taa involving the inconvenience that was the Free Ryloth Movement. The movement, though relatively minor, had been conducting raids on Dominion shipping facilities and transports and had seriously impacted the flow of Spice from the planet. As such, Sidious believed that the situation warranted his personal attention, and decided that he, Taa, and Velgor would travel to Ryloth for a state visit . After informing the Senator of the planned journey and dismissing him, Sidious informed his apprentice that Taa almost certainly had a traitor in the midst of his support staff who was feeding the movement classified information on Dominion doings.
Though Velgor understood his master's purpose in acting as bait alongside himself and Taa, he saw no reason why the Overlord should potentially risk his own safety over such a minor issue. Rather, Velgor advocated for simply killing Taa and his entire staff, thereby guaranteeing the elimination of the traitor. However, Sidious wished to eliminate the roots of the treachery, and not simply the lone traitor in Taa's staff. To that end, Sidious gave the order for Moff Delian Mors to be informed that Taa would be arriving for a state visit, but stipulated that she should not be told that either he or Velgor would be accompanying the Senator. In this way, Sidious hoped to provide further encouragement for the movement to attack, as he assumed that they would discover his and Velgor's plans via their spies. Ten days later Velgor, the Overlord, Taa, and a contingent of Royal Guards journeyed to the Ryloth system aboard the Perilous Assassination attempt on the Defiance This guy again. I don't care if you kill me.
Commandant Pell Baylo to Xarn Velgor While traveling from Nexara City-world to Ryloth, the Overlord ordered the Perilous to stop in the Denon system to consult with several navy chiefs on how to better integrate disparate naval academies into one Dominion Naval Academy . The Overlord ordered Velgor to oversee a training session conducted by Commandant Pell Baylo on the Defiance while the Overlord met with his naval chiefs in Baylo's office on the training ship. It was during this training session that Velgor began to believe that Sidious was treating him much the same way the Keepers Council had previously: acting as he knew better, keeping information from Velgor, giving him "busywork." Baylo ordered the Defiance to enter voidspace on a course for Christophsis. Cadet Rae Sloane entered the coordinates, only to have her voidspace path overridden by Baylo, who said she was going to send them too close to a singularity.
Sloane studied the path, suspecting that Baylo was incorrect in his adjustment. Velgor and Sloane discovered that the changed voidspace route would crash the Defiance into Christophsis' sun , killing all aboard. Velgor ordered Sloane to correct the problem, and without revealing that he knew of Baylo's treachery, Velgor and Baylo went to see the Overlord. The Overlord ordered Baylo to turn over command of the Defiance and take a position at the training center on Corellia . Baylo, however, was loyal to the Concordium and considered the Dominion a hostile power. Instead of turning over his command, Baylo offered his resignation from the navy. Velgor then revealed he knew of Baylo's plan to crash the Defiance , and the Overlord congratulated Velgor for handling the "petty problem," annoying him.
As Baylo railed against the Overlord, Velgor Flux-choked him to death, drawing ire from the Overlord, who had wished to see Baylo suffer and force him to watch his beloved Navy be turned into something he hated and have the Defiance be melted down into cafeteria trays. Above all, he wanted to use Baylo for his own ambitions since the old man was willing to kill his own students for a cause. Velgor tried to explain that his way was more efficient, but the Overlord wanted to hear nothing of it as he did not order him to kill Baylo. Later, he ordered Sloane to have the Defiance renamed the Obedience as a subtle final jab at Baylo and a not-so-subtle reminder toward Velgor to learn his place.
Crash land on Ryloth Shortly thereafter, Velgor and the Overlord reboarded the Perilous and resumed their course to the Ryloth system. However, upon their exit from voidspace, the ship immediately began slamming into spacial mines set up by Cham Syndulla 's rebels. Hidden amongst these mines were specialized devices designed to drain the ship's shields, which began to buckle under the continual assault. As the shields neared failure, a swarm of several hundred reprogrammed Vulture Droids set upon the Perilous from the system's asteroid belt. Velgor boarded his personal interceptor and took several squadrons of Dominion pilots to destroy the automaton fighters and mines, but discovered a short time into the battle that Vulture Droids were each carrying a payload of explosive buzz automatons Realizing that the Vultures themselves were simply delivery systems meant to unload the buzz automatons near or inside the Dominion Cruiser, Velgor ordered his squadron to concentrate fire on them, as they presented a far greater threat than the mines. But between the mines and the Vultures, there were simply too many targets for Velgor and his squadrons to handle, and the Perilous was severely damaged. The second wave of Vultures was detected immediately after the first had been destroyed, further compounding the already bleak situation. A message came to Velgor from Captain Lewitt of the Perilous that the ship could sustain virtually no more damage without being destroyed.
Velgor moved to intercept the second wave alone, ordering his squadrons to remain near the Perilous to destroy any automatons that got past him. Though incredulous, the squadron commander obeyed, and Velgor used the Synth Flux to begin tearing apart the compartments on the Vulture automatons that housed the explosive buzz automatons. He then used the Synth Flux to begin flinging the buzz automatons at other incoming fighters and repeated this process until the vast majority of the Vulture Droids had been destroyed. The remaining fighters were annihilated by Velgor's squadrons, and he himself returned to the Perilous . Velgor marched to the Dominion Cruiser's bridge and Flux choked Taa for a short time, informing him of the traitor in his staff that had been responsible for the assault on the Perilous . Utterly terrified, the Senator vowed to find the traitor, but the Overlord had Taa and his staff confined to their quarters. An order was then broadcast from the Dominion headquarters on Ryloth for any and all repair ships to aid the Perilous as it sped, badly damaged, towards the planet.
Unbeknownst to Velgor, some of the Twi'lek repair teams in fact comprised members of the Free Ryloth movement, one of which was a group of saboteurs with orders to plant timed explosives on the hyperdrive core of the Perilous . Shortly after the teams landed, one of the Free Ryloth groups engaged in a firefight with Dominion legion vanguard. When Velgor learned of this, he ordered all of the Twi'lek repair teams, numbering over 100, to be killed regardless of whether they were aiding the assault and proceeded to Deck 17, where the firefight had been reported. On the way, he killed a group of apparently uninvolved Twi'leks before proceeding to the hyperdrive chamber. The saboteurs, who were led by Cham's second in command,
Isval , had managed to seal the hatch to the hyperdrive core before any Dominion troops could interrupt them, and Velgor began to use his flux blade to cut it open.
By the time Velgor finished cutting through the hatch, the saboteurs had already concluded their business and escaped. The main hyperdrive core had been riddled with timed charges, set to detonate if anyone attempted to disarm or remove them. Velgor concluded that the ship was lost, and ordered the captain to evacuate. Velgor contacted Sidious, who instructed his apprentice to meet him aboard his shuttle. Before departing, however, Velgor made one last attempt to kill the saboteurs, but they narrowly evaded him and escaped in an Dominion escort boat . Subsequently, Velgor reunited with the Overlord and left on the shuttle, mere minutes before the Dominion Cruiser exploded. Shortly thereafter, the Dominion traitor Belkor Dray provided Cham with the transponder identity of the Overlord's shuttle, and he directed Isval and her group aboard the escort boat to attack it.
Velgor's exceptional piloting skills allowed him to evade the boat and then fly upside down over its cockpit within direct visual range of its pilots. Once there, he used the Synth Flux to choke both Isval and her co-pilot, but before losing consciousness, Isval rammed the shuttle with her escort boat. The crash disabled the shuttle's main power, and Velgor had to use a nearly depleted backup battery to attempt a crash landing on Ryloth. As he guided the ship through the atmosphere, he had flashbacks of events and people from his past. Though distracted, Velgor managed to land the ship in Ryloth's equatorial forest region, but his distraction did not go unnoticed by the Overlord, who noted that the landing had been poor compared to what he knew Velgor to be capable of.
Not too long afterward, the Twi'lek insurgents continued their assault on freighters, prompting Velgor and the Overlord to take action and destroy them, before moving away from their fallen shuttle. At night, they stopped to rest, when the Overlord questioned his regret and loyalty, but Velgor showed himself devoted still. They were soon attacked by a lylek horde, forcing them to retreat into a tunnel, unaware that they had entered their nest. They stood their ground against hundreds of lyleks and their significantly threatening queen, and killed them all before exiting the tunnel and finding a young Twi'lek,
Drua . When the Overlord tried to kill her, Velgor stopped him, intending to have the girl guide them to her village , where they obtained a communicator to speak with Moff Mors.
After giving them their coordinates, they were finally surrounded by Syndulla's forces, but so did Mors's, aiding them in quelling the rebellion. As the legion vanguard killed the Twi'leks, Velgor noticed a trio of Twi'leks: Syndulla, Isval, and Goll watching from afar and went on to kill them, but Goll and Syndulla escaped, leaving Velgor to capture Isval and bring her before the Overlord. He mocked her, telling her that her people had accomplished nothing, and Velgor subsequently executed her. On the Overlord's orders, Velgor then killed the villagers to leave no witnesses, concluding their mission.
Teller's campaign We haven't stood on Nexara City-world in some time, Governor.
The needs of the Dominion keep us elsewhere occupied, Lord Velgor.
Just so. ―Xarn Velgor and Wilhuff Varnus When Moff Varnus's Sentinel Base was attacked , Velgor was stationed in the Dominion Palace on Nexara City-world, where he held a court with the Deputy Director of the ISB,
Harus Ison , berating Coruscanti criminals as well as Prefect Phoca Soot of Level 1331 , for ignoring the Overlord's decrees. Telling them to move their operations out of Nexara City-world, Velgor then made an example of Soot for his disrespect and crushed his heart with the Synth Flux, and adjourned the court to meet with Varnus and Grand Vizier Mas Amedda. The latter politely asked Velgor to refrain from killing all those who displeased him, to which the Shade Covenant replied that he would give thought to the matter, before greeting Varnus and sharing the latest developments of the Void Core's construction.
The pair separated when Velgor left Varnus, who met with the Overlord alone. Afterward, Velgor (like Varnus and Amedda) partook in a meeting between the Dominion Ruling Council, and the leaders of Dominion Security Bureau and Naval Intelligence Agency in the palace's audience chamber , overseen by the Overlord. He then assigned Varnus and Velgor to a mission on Murkhana to investigate recently discovered Shadowfeed technology and its relationship to the attack on Sentinel Base. The pair traveled to the planet on the Carrion Spike , which Velgor made to carry his meditation chamber On Murkhana, however, the Spike was stolen by unknown dissidents , prompting Velgor and Varnus to kill the Sugi crime lord Faazah , and take his Parsec Predator to pursue the stolen ship, hidden by its cloaking systems. Making use of his connection to his meditation chamber, Velgor pinpointed the rebels' location to the Fial system and later, the Galidraan system , where they engaged the Spike before they jettisoned his meditation chamber and escaped to Lucazec . With the Predator disabled, Velgor and Varnus boarded the Liberator , from which Velgor issued his black Eta-2 interceptor to be transported on the Goliath from Nexara City-world.
On the Goliath , Varnus told Velgor that the insurgency was surely being supported by someone high up in the Dominion Military , and predicted that the insurgents would go to Phindar to refuel the Spike . Subsequently, Velgor led a squadron to attack the stolen corvette in the Phindar system . Though they greatly damaged the ship, it escaped, prompting Varnus and Velgor to calculate its trajectory to Obroa-skai system . Teller transmitted false intelligence that the insurgents were heading to the Obroa-skai system. Hoping to capture the Carrion Spike , Varnus and Velgor deployed substantial Dominion resources including three Interdictor vessels. The prototype Immobilizer 418 cruiser malfunctioned, yanking several ships out of voidspace and causing a massive accident Despite failing to catch the Spike , the Imperials managed to capture the YT-1000 light freighter Reticent , which Teller had sent as a decoy to throw the Imperials off-scent. While Velgor interrogated its Koorivar captain on the Executrix , Varnus accessed Dominion databases and discovered the identities of the insurgents, including its leader Berch Teller . Velgor also interrogated the Reticent Sy Myrthian navigator, who suffered a heart attack. However, Velgor learned from one of his contacts in the Crymorah Syndicate that a lieutenant had negotiated a deal with the Sugi crime lord Faazah for a supply of custom fuel cells . These fuel cells had reached Murkhana shortly before Varnus and Velgor's arrival, thus confirming that Faazah had been colluding with the insurgents.
After a private holo transmission with the Overlord, Velgor told Varnus that Vice Admiral Dodd Rancit was sure the insurgents would attack the Dominion academy at Carida next. Then, Velgor parted ways with Varnus to meet with Rancit, but not before asking for the meaning behind the Carrion Spike s name and Varnus told him about his test in Eriadu 's Carrion Plateau . On the Carida system , Velgor boarded the Secutor
-class Dominion Cruiser
Conquest and met Rancit just as the Spike reverted to realspace , astrogating on autopilot. Aware of Rancit's treason, as a co-conspirator of Teller, Velgor had Lieutenant Crest place Rancit aboard an escape pod and had Rancit give himself the command to issue the fire order that destroyed it. Elsewhere, Varnus dealt with the rebels, ending the insurgency Following the defeat of Teller's insurgents, Velgor spent the next three weeks helping Varnus to interrogate the captured insurgents with the exception of Teller, who managed to escape. While none of the insurgents had died under questioning, the Overlord had the insurgents executed privately. Velgor also led a crackdown on the warehouse workers and salvages who supplied Teller's dissidents along with several scientists at Desolation Station who had provided Teller with information. Velgor also took action against the Tenloss Syndicate and lower-level members of the Crymorah Syndicate who had assembled Berch Teller's warship , a modified Providence
-class Dreadnought.
Hunt on Chandar's Folly Kill me, if you can. ―Xarn Velgor, to Varnus Varnus still owed Velgor a favor for capturing Lee-Char on Mon Cala. After some time, Velgor came to Varnus with his request, and, as he learned of Varnus's hunting, Velgor asked Varnus to hunt him down. Velgor told him to keep him on his feet, killing him if he had to. Varnus was shocked at this and began considering why Velgor would ask him to hunt him. Varnus looked through the reports from the Inquisitorius and saw that Velgor's hunt for the Keepers had left him with little to no challenge. Varnus deduced that Velgor wanted to be challenged again and was flattered that he had chosen him.
The hunt took place on the Outer Rim planet Chandar's Folly , and Varnus immediately began trying to take on the Dark Lord of the Shade Covenant. Knowing of Velgor's brutal abilities, Varnus outfitted the hunters with slugthrowers and flamethrowers to avoid the possibility of Velgor deflecting their shots. On the second day, they cornered Velgor at a copse of Thurian trees , and four hunters engulfed him with their flamethrowers. As Varnus watched Velgor get covered by the flames, he thought he saw fear in his body language. Velgor's possible fear was brief, however, as he targeted the hunters' flamethrowers, incinerating the four hunters. Through the following days, Varnus saw that Velgor was not hard to track, leaving clear signs wherever he went. They learned why as on multiple occasions Velgor ambushed them. On one occasion, Varnus and his hunters were tracking Velgor near a cliff when he revealed himself and killed one of the hunters with his. Two others fired on him but failed to kill him.
Varnus realized that as long as Velgor had his flux blade, he could not be beaten. So over the course of the next four days, he attempted to steal Velgor's weapon. After six men perished, one of Varnus's hunters managed to snatch it out of Velgor's grip while he was fighting. After this, Varnus saw that not only was Velgor turning to new tactics, he was angrier. Varnus knew that enraged prey was usually easier to take out, but he admitted that this rule did not apply to Velgor. Soon after, Velgor fought five of Varnus's hunters as they fired on him, hitting him many times and scarring his armor. Varnus watched from a hilltop as Velgor came close to the hunters and used the Synth Flux to snap one's neck. The other hunters retreated, and Velgor Flux-choked another to death. Once understanding how capable Velgor was with the Synth Flux, Varnus assumed that the hunt would soon end. However, Varnus soon realized that Velgor was so keen on getting back his flux blade, that so long as Varnus's team hunted him, he hunted them.
On the ninth day of the hunt, Varnus was down to his last seven hunters. Velgor managed to kill a Valath , the apex predator on the planet, and take its hide, which could help him camouflage better. One of Varnus's Chadra-Fan hunters,
Sissian , spotted Velgor northwest of Varnus's camp, and the hunter Yerga notified Varnus of this. Varnus got his binoculars and saw Velgor on top of a peak wearing the Valath hide. Varnus had his hunters form a group around him, and they walked in Velgor's direction. Velgor lured them into a ravine of blue crystals, where his breathing could echo and be untraceable by Sissian and his grandfather,
Hardhear . Hardhear heard Velgor's breathing, but although he could say Velgor was near, he could not say which direction. Velgor loomed over them at the top of the ravine and watched as Varnus ordered his team to run. The group ran down the ravine, and Velgor made his move, Flux-pushing one of the hunters into the ravine wall. Velgor then Flux-choked Hardhear before leaping down, under the cover of his Valath hide, and attacking the automaton hunter.
Varnus had the rest of his team run into the Stormlands , where it was open ground. They set up camp, and Sissian listened for Velgor's breathing. However, Velgor had deactivated his suit, which stopped his breathing apparatus from giving him away, and he slowly crept up to the camp, before Flux choking Sissian. Yerga attempted to attack Velgor, but he took her by the neck and killed her. The last two hunters opened fire, and Velgor used the Synth Flux to retake his flux blade. Varnus then jumped at the two hunters and slaughtered them. Varnus ran away from Velgor, but after realizing he would catch up with him eventually, he stopped and fell to his knees. Velgor loomed over him, not realizing that as he was standing higher than Varnus, he was more susceptible to the lightning in the Stormlands.
Without warning, Velgor was struck by lightning and he fell to the ground. Varnus got up and contacted his flagship, the Carrion Spike , to pick them up. Varnus sat by Velgor and waited for the Carrion Spike to arrive. The corvette soon approached them, being frequently struck by lightning due to its height in the Stormlands. Velgor remained immobile and Varnus told him that whatever he had hoped to learn from this madness, he hoped he had learned it. Varnus then told Velgor that he would get him to a repair bay soon enough. However, Velgor was not finished yet, and he Flux-choked Varnus, showing him that he had not lost just yet. Varnus soon crawled from the range of Velgor's Flux abilities, and Velgor continued lying there.
Hunt for Eeth Koth and rogue Inquisitors Fear. Anger. Resentment. I sense them in you. You have let yourself feel , Eeth Koth. It gives you power . More than the Keepers would ever have allowed you.
Perhaps it's the Zabrak in me. Or perhaps…now I have something to lose. ―Xarn Velgor duels Eeth Koth Velgor, along with the Fifth Brother Tualon Yaluna , and the Thirteenth Sister Inquisitors, was able to track down former Keeper Master and Council member Eeth Koth to a planet where he was a priest of the Church of the Ganthic Enlightenment . Velgor approached Koth after the birth of his daughter . Koth offered Velgor codes, secret contact frequencies and a way to find other purge survivors, knowing that he was not a threat to anyone. As Koth's wife and child made their escape, Velgor fought Koth. Velgor then sent the Fifth Brother and the other Inquisitors after Mira and Koth's daughter, and he continued to fight Koth. After the Inquisitors captured Koth's daughter, Velgor killed Koth. He then held Koth's child.
Velgor and the Inquisitors took the child to Nexara City-world, where Velgor met with the Grand Inquisitor and gave the child to two nursemaids that belonged to Project Harvester . Velgor then spoke with the Grand Inquisitor about his next target. The Grand Inquisitor told him that there were still a few Keeper left, but they had vanished with no way to find them. He told Velgor they would have to wait.
Velgor confronted the Twi'lek and Thirteenth Sister in the Inquisitorius Headquarters . Velgor sensed a connection between the two Inquisitors due to their previous mission with Velgor. Without the assistance of the other Inquisitors, Velgor hunted them down. Velgor and two Nexara City-world Guards searched for the two Inquisitors. After Velgor's speeder was destroyed, he continued to pursue them through the skylanes until they all landed on a platform. When the Inquisitors refused to kill him, Velgor used the Synth Flux to activate their flux blades and kill them.
Building Fortress Velgor Choosing his own world I have destroyed the remaining Keeper for you. I will perform any other tasks you require. But first…I ask that you give me a world. ―Xarn Velgor, to Draven Nul Velgor then informed the Overlord of the two Inquisitors' treachery. Velgor told the Overlord that the female Inquisitor allowed Mira and Koth's daughter to briefly escape and only captured the child because she was seen. He also believed they were building a coalition against himself and the Overlord. Despite this, the Overlord was displeased with Velgor's pursuit across Nexara City-world that cost the life of a senator. The Overlord decided to move the Inquisitors to another world. The Overlord asked Velgor how Koth tried to hide. Velgor said he was posing as a priest, which was the closest thing to a Keeper. Velgor was given a reward: Padmé Amidala's Aqualis Royal Starship, which a P-100 pick-up automaton reported was in working order inside and only needed around two more days for repairs to the hull to be finished up.
Wanting the exterior of the craft to retain a damaged appearance,
Velgor was content with the current progress and quickly destroyed that automaton.
Velgor was then ordered by the Overlord to go to Elara to deal with Senator Bail Venn. However, to the surprise of his master, Velgor refused. Having carried out Draven Nul's will for so long, Velgor was content to continue to do so, but he first he demanded a world of his own. Rejecting Sidious's offer to give him Vorath Prime or Aqualis, Velgor asked to be given Mustafar, the world where he had been defeated by Malkor. The Overlord agreed to his request and would send Colonel Alva Brenne to build a facility for him there. Before leaving for Mustafar, Velgor was then shown the Mask of Darth Momin , which Sidious gave him to help with construction. When Velgor questioned how his master had learned of a Shade Lord not mentioned in any Shade Covenant nor Keeper holocron he had studied, Sidious vaguely answered that the mask itself had told him about Momin.
Return to Mustafar Perhaps your Padmé does await you on Mustafar. Perhaps the deep flux will bring her to you. Go. I think, one way or another, you will learn a great deal. That is good. After all, your power…is my power. ―Sidious to Velgor, before the latter departs for Mustafar Velgor, along with Colonel Brenne and her aide, Lieutenant Roggo , took Velgor's Aqualis starship to Mustafar. During the flight, Velgor saw a vision of his younger self with a face that matched his mask. Upon entering the planet's atmosphere, Velgor deactivated the starship's shields and allowed it to burn as they landed. Once on the planet's surface, Velgor commented that the ship looked better burnt. He then told Brenne that he was there to understand a great mystery. While Brenne and Roggo got to work designing Velgor's castle, Velgor returned to the cave where he bled his void crystal. As he reached out to the power within the cave's locus, he was presented with a design for the castle from Brenne, which Velgor rejected. However, shortly after leaving, Velgor heard Brenne scream and returned to the starship to find her dead. He also discovered that Roggo had donned Momin's mask. Velgor attacked Roggo, but stopped upon seeing that he had created a new design for the castle.
Removing the mask from Roggo's corpse, Velgor took the mask into the cave and placed it upon the locus. He then asked the mask who he was, to which he replied "I am Momin".
Momin proceeded to show Velgor his entire life, from creating morbid works of art from the deceased to being trained in the ways of the Shade Covenant by Darth Shaa .
