The following day, Velgor returned in his official capacity as the Overlord's envoy to negotiate as if nothing had happened the day before. Jabba welcomed Velgor and claimed that his resources belonged to the Dominion as long as he was paid. Velgor then turned to leave, leaving his legion vanguard to tell Jabba what was required, but the Hutt invited him to a feast and entertainment in his honor, for he liked to seal his business ventures by watching someone die. Aboard the sail barge Khetanna , Velgor and Jabba watched as the latter's guards killed wild banthas for the sport until Jabba's court jester Salacious B. Crumb , let out a loud cackle. Velgor warned the Hutt to not let the Kowakian monkey-lizard do that again in his presence if he valued Crumb's life; Jabba merely laughed and told Velgor he now regarded him as a "good friend." The Hutt then inquired whether his bounty hunters were adequately serving his needs; though Velgor berated him for bringing up the private matter, he also told the Hutt that their missions were simple enough: to capture an Dominion agent and a boy. Jabba argued that the "boy" had destroyed the Void Core, and had possibly been trained by Malkor. The name prompted Velgor to request that Jabba tell him all he knew of Malkor's exile on Vorath Prime.
Jabba first required someone screaming, though, so he ordered his guards to pilot the Khetanna to the Great Pit of Carkoon . He expected Velgor to be impressed by the sarlacc , which he believed was one of the few treasures that Vorath Prime had to offer, and said he found it hard to believe that anyone of note (like the Myrkon pilot) could have ever been born on Vorath Prime. In silence, Velgor watched his homeworld's sunset.
Servitude unto Tagge You should think of yourself as your flux blade. A singular weapon from an older time—and one that is dangerous to everyone around them without a skilled hand to wield it. In time you will understand that I am the skilled hand you've been waiting for. ―Cassio Tagge to Xarn Velgor After leaving Vorath Prime, Velgor met with Tagge aboard his Imperator
-class Star Dreadnought
Annihilator . During a time of many raids against ships carrying Jabba's weapons and equipment supplies, Velgor was sent to protect an Dominion shuttle,
CZ-246 , from a pirate attack . With his TIE Advanced, Velgor went to their aid, alongside Black Two and Black Three , and successfully disabled the automated CR90 corvette attacking the shuttle. Upon returning to the Annihilator , Velgor reported to Tagge that the pirate vessel had been captured and that he suspected a security breach.
As he said so, Tagge disagreed, believing the raids to be predictable piracy, and admitted to seeing the Void Core as "Varnus's folly." Velgor noted that Varnus at least had a vision, prompting Tagge to admit his plans were not as grand as Varnus's or Velgor's, but they still were effective; he believed that the Dominion could not be based around a single asset, like the Void Core or Velgor himself. Tagge then informed Velgor that he intended to repair the corvette and use it to attack its base of origin , sending Velgor to head the attack, although with an adjutant , Lieutenant Oon-ai Velgor left to prepare for the mission and oversee the repairs on the corvette. Oon-ai followed, asking permission to look into Velgor's personal communications to provide information to Tagge. Although it annoyed Velgor, as it was the Overlord's command that he serve under Tagge, he allowed it and left. He then planted treasonable evidence into a black astromech so as to incriminate Oon-ai during the mission. Soon thereafter, Velgor, Oon-ai, and six legion vanguard boarded the corvette and found the pirate base, docking with the station. Greeted by a Twi'lek pirate , whom he killed, Velgor ordered the troopers to secure the core, while his astromech made his way to the command center to plant the false evidence.
They found themselves under the attack of two customized droidekas who brandished missile launchers, prompting Velgor to use the Synth Flux to throw the incoming missiles back at the attackers, destroying the automatons and fatally injuring the pirates. A Mon Calamari pirate survived long enough to activate the self-destruct, and Velgor ordered the surviving troopers to return to the corvette and prepare to launch as soon as he returned. Followed by Oon-ai, Velgor went deeper into the station to retrieve the false evidence. They returned to the corvette just before the station exploded.
Back on the Annihilator , Velgor reported to Tagge, telling him that he was impressed with the results of his plan (the intelligence he had gathered confirmed the raiders were being backed by the Crymorah Syndicate), but also claimed Oon-ai was the leak, noting that Tagge should be more careful of those who were close to him. Shortly after, Velgor isolated the black astromech under the pretenses of a private discussion; the Dark Lord ensured the automaton had deleted all records of its data uploads on the station, before venting the automaton through an airlock and into space, where it exploded.
Following a Dominion-Coalition confrontation , Velgor went to the Palace of Grakkus the Hutt to meet with Sergeant Kreel. A covert operative of his battalion "Velgor's Fist," Kreel informed him that his mission to end Grakkus' operations was a success, but that the Myrkon Coalition pilot had escaped them yet again. Velgor inquired if he had mentioned his name (which he did not), and asked him to tell him everything he had learned of the boy.
A automaton army There was a time I had armies at my beck and call. That time has passed. I need resources of my own. Private resources. ―Xarn Velgor to Dr. Lyra Venn, on why he needed her services From the information he gathered from the pirate base, Velgor learned that a Doctor Chelli Lona Lyra Venn had supplied the pirates with the customized droidekas. As he wanted similarly effective units in the personal army he sought to build, Velgor set out to find Lyra Venn, conducting a manhunt for her in many locations, gathering information and killing the witnesses, until he finally learned of her presence on Quarantine World III in Kallidahin Space . He landed there just as Lyra Venn was arrested by Utani Xane and a squad of B2 super battle automatons; although Xane warned him that he was violating treaties, Velgor began destroying her automatons and saved Lyra Venn as she tumbled over a ledge. Velgor stabbed Xane through the back with his saber before telling the doctor he needed her services. He helped her up and she agreed.
Together, they boarded Lyra Venn's ship, the Void Wraith II , where she admitted to being a fan of his. The rogue archaeologist also introduced Velgor to the Triple-Zero protocol personality matrix and estimated it would take hours to unlock the matrix's codes that were restricting it. It took Velgor a few seconds to unlock it, which let Lyra Venn install the personality matrix into a protocol automaton's body right away. As the automaton introduced himself as , she imprinted herself and Velgor as his masters, and used him to wake up BT-1 , a blastomech prototype . But as it was hostile towards his new masters, 0-0-0 shut it down before he could do any harm.
Velgor then let her know the reason he had sought her out was to secure his own private resources, ones only she could help him procure. Lyra Venn (who had originally acquired the two automatons for the Gotra ) instead gave them to Velgor after realizing Velgor was probably going to be her employer for the foreseeable future. She asked what else he needed, and he answered that he wanted troops of unquestionable loyalty: battle automatons. Lyra Venn then suggested that they acquire an unusual automaton foundry on Xarnak, one in possession of Queen Karina , who had survived the Gene Wars and the planet's sterilization . As they landed the Void Wraith , and she sent 0-0-0 and BT-1 to map the cave route to the queen so that she and Velgor could follow, Lyra Venn asked Velgor if he had ever been to Xarnak before. Her questioning brought up the memory of his first kiss with Amidala, shortly before the First Battle of Xarnak.
Velgor ordered Lyra Venn to cease her probing, and entered the catacombs; she followed him. They reunited with BT-1 and 0-0-0 and encountered the queen. Velgor immediately amputated her from the womb automaton factory. The queen, injured but alive, ordered her "children" to stop the intruders; at Velgor's command, BT-1 opened fire on the automatons, and he and Velgor began to overwhelm her forces. Meanwhile, Lyra Venn set a beacon on the ceiling with Velgor's help and ordered her Void Wraith to barrage the locator to make a hole in the roof. Velgor protected himself and Lyra Venn by creating a Flux barrier . His T-327 Starcraft then lowered a crane-platform, which Lyra Venn attached to the factory while Velgor fought off the advancing automatons, and the ship carried them and the factory to the skies.
They would later board the Void Wraith , where Lyra Venn successfully activated the factory and told him he would have his private automaton army. She then asked Velgor if he would kill her then or later, and said that if he did, she wanted a flux blade right through the neck, with no warning. Velgor finally told her she had proved resourceful and would be safe as long as he had use for her, unless she tried to blackmail him. It was then that 0-0-0 interrupted them to inform Velgor that he had a signal from Black Krrsantan: he was bringing the Overlord's agent to him.
Cylo's enforcers The Synth Flux is obsolete. These are its successors.
This is... blasphemous!
This has nothing to do with the Synth Flux. Much like you, Lord Velgor. I look at you, more machine than man, and I see a bridge between the old world and mine. In many ways, these are your children.
Enough. ―Cylo introduces Xarn Velgor to his enforcers As Krrsantan arrived, Velgor confronted the Overlord's agent, Cylo-IV. Velgor demanded that he tell him his name, his commission from the Overlord and the location of his . As he refused, Velgor assigned 0-0-0 to retrieve all relevant information from him. After an interrogation that resulted in the agent's death, 0-0-0 reported to Velgor that he was Dr. Cylo-IV, that his research base was located in an Outer Rim nebula , and that his commission was to create replacements for Velgor. After learning this, they immediately departed for the base, aboard his J-type 327 Nubian . While Lyra Venn remained on the ship, Velgor and two platoons of automaton commandos infiltrated it. Velgor sent a platoon to "the dojo" and had the other one follow him to the barracks . As they killed the soldiers in there, Lyra Venn reported that they had lost the other platoon, prompting Velgor to go to the dojo, where he met the twins Morit and Aiolin Astarte They soon engaged in combat, but before any of the parties could harm each other, Vex-9 appeared and told the twins to stand down. He explained to Velgor why he was still alive after his last body was killed, and that he was not making apprentices for the Overlord but enforcers, as he believed the Synth Flux to be obsolete. Believing him to be blasphemous, Velgor attempted to strike Cylo down when the scientist claimed they were, in many ways, Velgor's children and successors. The Overlord then revealed his presence to Velgor and stopped him, claiming he wanted to see Cylo's demonstration without delay.
Velgor was immediately made to fight the Astarte twins,
Tulon Voidgazer , the Mon Calamari Karbin , and a Trandoshan. As neither party was injured, the Overlord ordered the fight to be one to the death. As such, Velgor fought the Trandoshan , but its ferocity threw him off balance, prompting Morit to intervene and kill his fellow enforcer. The Overlord then ordered the fighting to cease; Velgor warned Morit that his trespass would not be forgotten, as the Trandoshan's life was his to take. The Overlord then told the enforcers and Velgor that their mission was to strike down all who opposed the Dominion and that they were not to slay each other. As per his master's orders, Velgor followed him.
In private, the Overlord, discovering the remains of some of Velgor's commando automatons, admitted to being impressed that he had some independence left in him after so long and that perhaps he could triumph against the enforcers. Velgor criticized his dealings with Cylo, as he saw his enforcers as a heresy to the Synth Flux, but the Overlord berated him for his lack of respect—for it was Velgor who had disappointed him on Mustafar and had forced him to contact Cylo. As he left, he observed that perhaps, Velgor would prove himself worthy of the name he had given him back then.
Parenthood Did you bring me anything of value, bounty hunter?
Not much. Just his name. Kast. ―Xarn Velgor and Kael Ryn Troubled, Velgor returned to his ship. Aboard the Nubian, Lyra Venn told him that Ryn wanted to report in during Velgor's absence. Subsequently, Velgor traveled to a Dominion Cruiser to meet with the bounty hunter and hear his story. The Dark Lord was disappointed to hear that Ryn had lost the boy, but before leaving, Ryn told him his name: Joren Kast. In his solitude, Velgor remembered Amidala telling him about her pregnancy, and Draven Nul's half-truth, and cracked the viewport in his anger. Thereupon, he used his meditation chamber's holoprojector to contact his master, but chose to say nothing about his son; rather, Velgor assured him that he would not fail and ended the transmission. Nevertheless, he accepted Joren as his son and vowed that the boy would be his.
Determined to find more about Joren, Velgor returned to Vorath Prime on the Void Wraith , with Lyra Venn, 0-0-0 and BT-1 in his company. They landed at the Lars homestead, which they discovered to be deserted after an attack by Dominion troops, and Velgor tried to find traces of Joren's presence. Unable to find anything of importance, Velgor left the moisture farm and went to Malkor's home where he indeed felt the remnants of Joren's usage of the Synth Flux during his duel against Ryn, but nothing else. As they left, Lyra Venn activated a molecular purge bomb to remove all remnants of their presence, and Velgor dismissed Lyra Venn, for he was to return to his duties for the time being.
Anthan Prime I'm trying to ascertain if you are... dangerous.
Oh, I am, Lord Velgor... But only to the enemies of the Dominion. ―Xarn Velgor and Inspector Thanoth As part of Tagge's crackdown on Outer Rim criminal interests who had gone unchecked in the past, Velgor took part in a mission to terminate a criminal organization known as Son-tuul Pride . He and his troops destroyed the Pride's base and seized its stockpile of credits, to be transported on an Dominion light cruiser, but he also secretly sent Lyra Venn all the information needed to steal the credits cargo.
Following the theft , Velgor reunited with her on Anthan 13 , where he sent her to confirm some information about Commodex Tahn Velgor then attended a meeting between Tagge and Cylo's enforcers, wherein the Astarte twins were assigned to deal with the Plasma Devils , and wherein Velgor let Tagge know he wanted the mission of finding the Myrkon Coalition pilot (which was Karbin's mission). Tagge felt he was too personally invested, and sent Velgor to punish whoever was responsible for the theft of the Son-tuul Pride's fortune in the Anthan system ; he also informed Velgor that Inspector Thanoth was his replacement adjutant.
While Thanoth went to the Anthan Prime Orbital Dockyard to investigate the theft's circumstances, Velgor returned to Anthan 13 and killed the entirety of a small Coalition cell to cover his tracks. He later reunited with Thanoth on Anthan Prime , where they sought Doowan , an employee of " the Dragon "—an arms dealer who was likely to have sold the explosives needed for the theft—and interrogated him. The Nautolan promptly revealed the location of the Dragon in his mansion , before the twins killed him in a show of force.
Thanoth then noticed Velgor's passivity, but Velgor dismissed it, saying he was merely trying to ascertain if Thanoth was dangerous or a traitor.
The two then went to the Dragon's mansion, which Velgor entered on his own, killing the people inside until he reached the Dragon himself. Although he tried to tell Velgor everything to save himself, Velgor killed the Ortolan , and claimed he would have detonated the building once Thanoth reached the office. The inspector then opened the Dragon's vault to access his records and asked Velgor to ready a strike force for when he located the suspects.
Velgor then used the opportunity to meet with Lyra Venn, who had just come back from a raid on Commodex Tahn's villa on Aqualis. She confirmed his sources: Amidala had indeed died but had given birth to a healthy boy, who was taken away by the Keepers. Velgor then tasked Lyra Venn with finding Joren before Karbin could; subsequently, she resorted to The Ante Velgor then returned to the Dominion Cruiser, where Thanoth they would travel to the Spire to find The Ante. When they found him, he told them the location of the Plasma Devils to show cooperation and pointed at the one responsible for the theft, Lyra Venn. Before the Givin could identify her by name, Velgor telekinetically forced an unlucky stray shot and killed him. Velgor then went to find Lyra Venn before anyone else.
As they encountered each other, Velgor ordered her not to struggle and Flux choked her, but Lyra Venn claimed she knew of Joren's location. Velgor released her and ordered Lyra Venn to tell him, but she refused, claiming she would tell him later. He then let her go, using the Synth Flux to drop debris above him as she escaped on the Void Wraith . He got out of the rubble as Thanoth and the legion vanguard arrived and claimed he had fallen into her trap. To follow her,
Velgor and Thanoth boarded their Arquitens
-class light cruiser. As they waited for their
TIE/sa bombers to catch Lyra Venn, Velgor and Thanoth agreed to use their ready strike force to kill the Plasma Devils before they could escape.
The two had their light cruiser go to the Thanteen Substrata on Anthan 1 , where the Coalition cell was located. As per their plan, their light cruiser bombarded the westernmost tunnels and sealed them, whereas Velgor went to the east route and felled incoming Coalition ships by throwing his flux blade until the cell was annihilated. He then took three of the Plasma Devils' flight helmets and, alongside Thanoth, presented them to Tagge, who agreed with their course of action given the tight situation. As Karbin reported that the Myrkon Coalition pilot was free, Velgor and Thanoth left, and the Inspector let him know it had been an honor to serve alongside him.
Shu-Torun As a princess, you served your father. As a queen, you serve your people. This is a small reminder of what happens to worlds who resist the Overlord. This is what remains of Elara. ―Xarn Velgor to Queen Trios Velgor traveled to Shu-Torun with a message to reinforce their cooperation and force its government to fulfill the quotas, while 0-0-0 and BT-1 killed the king and his closest servants. Expecting to meet the King of Shu-Torun , he instead met Princess Trios upon landing, who was to show him to the ball, a little celebration to showcase their culture. Soon after their arrival, however, Shu-Torun assassins that wished death to the Dominion and the king, but Velgor successfully fended them off and demanded to see the king immediately. Trios complied and guided him through the tunnels as the main elevators were being watched by the traitorous dukes, according to her. Velgor recognized Trios as admirable for her willingness to sacrifice herself for the greater good, as lava poured into the tunnels.
However, he used his flux blade to make a round platform to float above the lava and reach one of the exits above. Taking an unconscious Trios with him, Velgor made it to one of the corridors where he killed the guards. Trios awoke, only to point a pulse rifle at Velgor. He, however, cut her hand off and entered the king's chambers to find the monarch, his other children and guards killed by Velgor's assassin automatons. With Trios as the new Queen of Shu-Torun, he delivered the Overlord's message; a piece of Elara, as a reminder of what happened to worlds that resisted his rule. Velgor then told her that he would pacify Shu-Torun, but that she was to not forget whom she served.
Vrogas Vas Xarn Velgor! Lay down your weapons! You are surrounded!"
"All I am surrounded by is fear. And dead men. ―Xarn Velgor responds to a Coalition commando company ordering his surrender Velgor met with Lyra Venn and the automatons, and demanded that he tell her Joren's location:
Vrogas Vas . She continued by saying she did want to work for him and show that she was trustworthy. After a moment, he conceded that his work alongside Thanoth had given him an appreciation of talent, and told her not to make him regret his decision. He left, saying it was a task for him alone. Unbeknownst to him, a probe automaton from Karbin followed him.
In his TIE Advanced, he went to the planet's system where, upon coming out of voidspace, he found three Alliance starfighter squadrons orbiting the planet. As he sensed Joren's presence, he remained and charged against the starfighters, who dismissed his TIE as a mere Dominion scout.
During the battle , Velgor engaged the Starhawk starfighters , and managed to take out both Blue Squadron and Yellow Squadron , as he lost contact with Lyra Venn. Velgor was then confronted by Joren, who was flying as Red Five , and flew his Starhawk into Velgor's starfighter, hoping to kill him. During the collision, their stabilizers were damaged prompting their craft to crash on the planet's surface. There, Velgor was hunted down by the Alliance forces, starting with Gray Squadron , but he promptly destroyed them by use of the Synth Flux.
Afterward, a company of Coalition commandos moved in to attack him, but he activated some of their thermal detonators before they could throw them, taking out a tank by deflecting its shot against it and continued to reduce the Coalition forces.
Velgor also took the pulse rifle array of his fallen TIE Advanced x1 and used it to take down further ships, this time from Cyan Squadron . Not too long afterward, another platoon was deployed to attack him, although this time Venn was leading them herself. Velgor, choking her two companions, confronted her but felt an incoming ambush. When he let her go so that she contacted Joren, Venn used the opportunity to arrange an airstrike on her and Velgor's location. Instead, Commander Karbin arrived with a task force, and tried to take his victory—both his son and the princess' execution—from Velgor. Subsequently, Velgor engaged the enforcer in a duel, during which Venn escaped, and during which they entered the Keepers Temple in ruins.
Inside the temple, he was momentarily distracted by ghosts of his past (Sol's, Malkor's, and Vorn's voices), but continued the duel. Lyra Venn then contacted him from aboard the Void Wraith and informed him that Joren had been captured by Karbin's guards. So Velgor had her crash her ship against Karbin and confronted the mortally wounded Karbin, who realized there was more to the Myrkon pilot and Velgor's relationship than met the eye. Velgor then finished him and contacted Lyra Venn to tell her that her "pardon" depended on Joren's capture. With the Synth Flux, he brought down the shuttle transporting Joren, and Lyra Venn tried to capture Joren only to be captured herself by the Coalition forces, leaving Velgor to watch as his son on the Shadow Hawk fled from Vrogas Vas.
Shu-Torun War The queen of Shu-Torun. Did you choose well?
There was no other choice. ―Queen Trios and Xarn Velgor Velgor then returned to the Dominion Palace and brought Karbin's body to the Overlord, demanding that his master give him a fight worthy of his time. The Overlord then told Velgor to return to Shu-Torun as the ore-barons were rebelling and he required a full military intervention, and that Cylo (per Tagge's request) was to join him in the task. As a result, Velgor returned to Shu-Torun, alongside Cylo, the Astarte twins and Voidgazer. Accompanied by Queen Trios, Velgor led an attack against a rebellious delving citadel , and destroyed it as a demonstration of force. He also accorded to treat Trios with the illusion of respect and tasked, in secret, a group of bounty hunters to find Lyra Venn and return her to him, either dead or alive.
After sending a broadcast to the barons, Velgor, Trios, and Cylo and his enforcers went, per the queen's suggestion, to the ancestral retreat of the Shu-Torun royalty. There, they were soon attacked by Baron Rubix 's Lava Leviathan , and Velgor used Trios' escape vessel to reach the Leviathan and dared the twins to follow him. With their flux blades, they boarded it, and from the inside, the three cut large holes that allowed the ship to flood with magma and be destroyed. Upon returning, Trios suggested that they attack Rubix next (as she had identified the ship's owner) and so, Velgor told Cylo to prepare his enforcers. In the meantime, he killed Beebox for trying to deceive him with a stranger's disintegrated body, and refused Aiolin's request to train her.
Soon enough, they carried out the siege to Rubix's delving citadel , using the Shu-Torun Loyalist/Dominion Delving Fleet 's combat drills , with Velgor heading the first wave alongside the Astarte. However, he was betrayed by Cylo, and his drill was redirected into a lithoporoite seam, where they were surrounded by Rubix's elite forces. With their outbound communications jammed, Velgor left his troops as well as automaton platoons to protect the drill and had the Astarte twins follow him as to use the enemy's lines to tell the rest of the assault fleet to resume the attack. On the way there, the twins attacked him, but Aiolin saw they could not win against Velgor and destroyed the pathway between them. She was then betrayed by her brother who pushed her into the lava and fled back to Cylo; Velgor then took the dying Aiolin out of the lava and had her reveal Cylo's deceit before finishing her off.
Velgor then returned to his forces, who had secured the enemy lines thanks to 0-0-0's stratagem, and was surprised to find that Trios' assault to the delving citadel was already underway. With no opposition, Velgor's combat drill resumed his way to citadel, and Velgor himself cut off Rubix's escape way. Trios soon went to their encounter and Rubix formally surrendered; she then had the baron executed, and appointed his daughter as his successor, thus ending the rebellion. Velgor then left the planet and informed the Overlord of Cylo's treachery, and the Overlord simply summoned him. He also received a message from Inspector Thanoth saying that he had located Lyra Venn and that they needed to talk.
Hunt for Cylo I often wondered whether what rose from our operating table was a machine or a man... Good to have an answer. ―Cylo, to Xarn Velgor At the shipyards of Kuat , Velgor met with Sidious aboard an unfinished Imperator
-class Star Dreadnought, the
Imperator . The Overlord explained that in order to save Velgor after Mustafar, he had to resort to scientists who were similar in philosophy to the Shade Covenant, among them Cylo. Cylo eventually grew too powerful within the Varnus Initiative and so Sidious was forced to make him overplay his hand. With the man a mere traitor, he could be purged. After the meeting, Velgor met with Thanoth on Anthan 13; the Inspector, aware of Velgor's real identity, revealed that he would support Velgor's treason against the Overlord and that he would reveal Lyra Venn's location on the Cosmatanic Steppes . However, Thanoth understood that Velgor would not rest until he silenced a possible loose end, so he had elected to tell the Shade Lord in person so Velgor would not waste time looking for him. When Thanoth revealed Lyra Venn's location and thanked "Anakin" for their work together, Velgor killed him with his flux blade.
Outside and ignoring the automatons' request to dispose of Thanoth's corpse, Velgor ordered 0-0-0 and BT-1, who were joined by Krrsantan and his commando automatons, to return Lyra Venn to the Imperator or kill her as their priority orders.
Velgor also made it clear to wipe out anyone else they found at the Cosmatanic Steppes to eliminate all witnesses present, ensuring that, if Lyra Venn had told anyone about her work with Velgor, they were silenced.
Velgor then departed aboard his TIE Advanced x1, with 0-0-0 realizing he would not be taking Cylo back alive, and returned to the Imperator . Aboard, he confronted Professor Thlu-Ry , a colleague of Cylo's, whom he threatened to learn the traitor's location in the Crushank Nebulae . Velgor took the Obliterator to the nebulae, where his crew confirmed Cylo's whale fleet was present. After ordering that Thlu-Ry be thanked and then, for fraternizing with a traitor, executed, Velgor led the assault on Cylo's fleet from his TIE Advanced. After Cylo's flagship fired an ion pulse to disable the Obliterator and moved to escape, Velgor fired into the whale ship and crashed into the opening he'd made, boarding the enemy ship just before it jumped to voidspace Moving past the personnel he'd left to be sucked into voidspace and entering the labs, Velgor was confronted by Voidgazer, who pitted him against a cyberanimate rancor as a test of her technology just as Cylo's fleet arrived at Kuat. Although the creature gave him some difficulty, Velgor killed it by throwing his flux blade through its skull, severing the neural link, and turned one of Voidgazer's defense automatons against her, fatally wounding her. As she lay dying, Voidgazer revealed that she had tampered with the Imperator s systems to incapacitate the crew; Velgor finished her off and went to the empty bridge, which had been abandoned by Cylo and Morit as they had fled in the escape pods, to find that Cylo's flagship was to collide with the Imperator Before it did, he ejected and landed on the Imperator s outer hull, where he soon encountered Morit. He easily killed him and then made his way to the Imperator s bridge, where he confronted Cylo. The scientist simply disabled Velgor's cybernetics with a device he had kept for twenty years, forcing Velgor to kneel and drop his flux blade.
While Velgor was incapacitated, he received visions of his duel with his former master, Zeth Malkor. This time, however, Malkor threw him into the lava. Velgor then rose out of the river in his life suit and struck down his old master. However, like their duel on the Void Core, Malkor's body disappeared. Velgor then encountered a vision of his younger self. The two versions of Kast fought until Velgor came out victorious, throwing his counterpart in the lava. After his counterpart yelled that he hated him, the Dark Lord met a vision of his deceased wife, Padmé Amidala. Velgor then found himself chained to the same table where he first received his suit. The vision of Padmé pleaded with him, asking him to stay with her. Velgor, however, choked the illusion while at the same time regained control of his body. After he told the vision that her husband was dead, Velgor used his flux blade to impale Cylo, who was astounded that Velgor was moving while his suit was deactivated. Thus, Velgor had regained the ship from his control. The Dark Lord then informed his master of his success. The Overlord then ordered Velgor to finish his mission then return to him, stating there was much to discuss.
Velgor took control of his TIE Advanced, flying into Cylo's command ship. After cutting his way through Cylo's forces, the Dark Lord found his way to the doctor's cloning room. After destroying Cylo's spare bodies, Velgor made it to the command bridge. Velgor then confronted the doctor one last time. He mind-tricked the whale-ship into flying into a nearby star, destroying Cylo once and for all. After returning to the Imperator , Velgor met with his master, whom had been awaiting a full report, only to reveal it was no longer needed because Lyra Venn was present: while 0-0-0 and BT-1 had successfully brought Lyra Venn to the Imperator and were planning on giving Velgor suggestions on how to kill the doctor, should they have found his planned execution too boring for their tastes, Lyra Venn realized Velgor's priority order only mandated the automatons bring her to the Imperator . As she realized as such and was dragged away by two legion vanguard to await Velgor's wrath, she gave a new priority order: help her escape.
Reaching Draven Nul's chamber, Lyra Venn told Draven Nul all of Velgor's secret missions and plans, although she did chose to leave out the details surrounding Joren Kast.
Instead of being angry with Velgor, however, the Overlord was pleased with his apprentice's ability to operate in the shadows, stating he was everything he could have hoped for. The Overlord then left his apprentice to deal with Lyra Venn as he pleased. Velgor then ordered Lyra Venn to follow him. Despite her pleas for a quick death, he forced her out the airlock, although she secretly survived; having deduced Velgor would never honor her wish to avoid execution via airlock, she was rescued in time by Krrsantan, 0-0-0, and BT-1. Making his way to the bridge, the Overlord informed him that Grand General Tagge was to be demoted and that command of the Imperator and the fleet would be transferred to Velgor, who Draven Nul named supreme commander of the Dominion fleet The Overlord then left his apprentice to "educate" his subordinates of this new era. Velgor then executed Tagge and ordered Admiral Kendal Ozzel to have the ship ready in two weeks. Velgor then thought of his son, content that he would soon have him.
Slaying the unworthy After the rebellion began encroaching in the Mid Rim, The Overlord let Velgor loose, deciding that incompetence at the highest level was to blame for the the Void Core's destruction, allowing him to kill Dominion officials including Moff Coovern and Minister Khemt At some point after the Imperator became his flagship, Velgor summoned Lieutenant Sulaco to the bridge and ordered her to follow him to a hangar bay, where he ordered that she pilot his Lambda-class shuttle on a diplomatic mission to Sarjenn 3 , the third moon in the Sarjenn system and Sulaco's homeworld. The two Imperials landed at the moon's only port and proceeded to a nearby Coalition outpost as Sulaco questioned their mission, claiming that Sarjenn 3 had no diplomats. Velgor was quickly ambushed by a sniper, whom he dispatched, before approaching the stronghold’s outermost wall, telekinetically relieving the Coalition forces of their weapons, and slaughtering them. Proceeding inside, Velgor deflected a shot from a missile launcher, whose gunner he strangled and brought down a massive building with the Synth Flux. Now at Sulaco’s house, Velgor identified the officer as a Coalition spy. Coercing her into becoming a double agent, Velgor ordered her to say what she had to before departing, indicating that they had work ahead.
Skirmish on the Scorca Retreat I am the alpha and the omega of the Ordu Aspectu! I am the eternal Rur, Keeper Knight I am Xarn Velgor, Shade Lord. I killed the Keepers…and none shall escape me ―The eternal Rur confronts Velgor Sometime later, Lyra Venn began an auction of a large void crystal, which contained a copied conscience from an ancient Keeper named Rur , on the Sorca Retreat . However, Velgor was contacted about the criminal gathering and the artifact by 0-0-0, who specifically left out the fact that Lyra Venn had organized it as blackmail for the doctor: if she refused to remove the programming that bent him and BT-1 to her will, they would inform Velgor of her survival.
By the time Velgor arrived aboard an Dominion
-class Dominion Cruiser, the auction had turned into a
massacre orchestrated by 0-0-0, who had allowed a automaton from the Cyban Front to be taken over by the conscious void crystal, making it hunt down the factions on the space station.
As Velgor roamed the hallways, he found another Cyban Front automaton , who had noticed him and his legion vanguard moving about. The automaton attempted to run, but Velgor used the Synth Flux to smash it to pieces. Velgor ordered his troopers to kill all of the individuals they found in the space station.
After the freed 0-0-0 and BT-1 made their escape, and as some of the legion vanguard crossed paths with the automaton being controlled by Rur, Velgor cut down a member of the Dominion of the Ezaraa . Some of his legion vanguard approached him, reporting resistance in the top dome and the automaton. Velgor walked down the halls and found the automaton standing over a pile of dead legion vanguard. The automaton had two blue-bladed flux blades, generated from the void crystal inside it, erected from its arms. The two declared themselves to each other and clashed.
As they fought, Velgor deduced that Rur was not a Shade Covenant. Rur claimed to be the last of the Ordu Aspectu and a Keeper. As they deduced more about each other, Velgor Flux-pushed Rur into a stone slab. Rur got up again, but Velgor charged at him, and as they clashed, he cut the automaton's right hand off.
Velgor Flux-pushed him again into a wall, but the automaton began laughing and declared that he was a boundless god of machines. Rur quickly took control of the defensive guns in the ceiling and used them on Velgor. Rur then tried to take control of Velgor's suit and stalled his defensive moves. Velgor told him that he was not the first to attempt that and used the Synth Flux to repeatedly smash Rur against the walls. The three collisions with the walls demolished the automaton and Velgor took the crystal. After the duel, a legion vanguard reported to Velgor that some small ships had slipped his blockade. Velgor sensed Lyra Venn's presence, which was an annoying presence to him, but elected to hold the blockade. Velgor took the crystal back to Sidious on Nexara City-world and then archived it with the rest of his master's collection.
Tureen VII After the death of Cylo, Velgor ordered Sergeant Kreel and his SCAR Squadron to hunt down Joren Kast. They eventually found him and his allies hijacking the Dominion
-class Dominion Cruiser
Harbinger . The vessel made its way to the blockaded planet Tureen VII in an attempt to aid the people there. Velgor and his TIE forces engaged the attacking rebel Starhawks.
While fighting the rebel fighters, Velgor questioned Kreel if he had located Joren. At that moment, Kreel was engaging the rebel in a flux blade duel. The Dark Lord then warned the legion vanguard not to injure his "prize." However, Joren used the Synth Flux to incapacitate Kreel, much to Velgor's anger. The rebels eventually were able to break the siege and give relief to the people of Tureen VII. SCAR Squadron, however, was not left empty-handed, capturing the protocol automaton LQ-9M.
Soon after, Velgor was contacted by Kreel and informed that LQ-9M had told them everything they needed to know and that they could not get him to stop talking. Velgor then told him that the automaton was a piece of worthless junk and to dispose of him.
Shortly after, DR-7X managed to infiltrate the Dominion Cruiser where LQ-9M was held and broke the automaton out. Velgor was notified of this and intercepted their Starhawk in his TIE advanced. DR-7X knew they could not escape alone and sent out a distress call to their friends. To Velgor's frustration, DR-7X gained the support of Joren, the Shadow Hawk and the Volt Cobra , who gave enough covering fire to help them all escape.
Confronting Kreel Sometime later, Velgor approached Kreel, who was training with half a dozen training automatons . Velgor told him he could try all he wanted but he would never become a Shade Covenant. Kreel said he was not asking to be, saying that it was not a Shade Covenant who saved him from the pits of Chagar IX , but the 501st Legion. Claimed to be a legion vanguard to the core and that he was just appreciating the value of a flux blade. Velgor then took his flux blade and threatened to get rid of him and his squad for failing him aboard the Harbinger . Kreel told Velgor that if he failed him again, he would kneel down to Velgor and bury that flux blade in his chest himself, after killing his men with it. Velgor gave Kreel a mission to destroy a possible rebel outpost on Horox III , bringing back Joren if he was present. Velgor then handed back Kreel's flux blade and told him to be worthy of it. Sidious then entered the room and summoned Velgor. Kreel knelt down and in honor of Sidious's presence and he thanked him. Sidious then brought Velgor out of the room. Kreel then traveled to the Horox system and destroyed the rebel outpost there, killing all rebels present.
Sculpting a trap Sometime later, Velgor was at his castle with Admiral Kendal Ozzel and General Maximilian Veers . There, he asked Ozzel of the excuses he had for the slowing progress of Dominion projects, which Ozzel blamed on the growing resistance. He argued that, although he could strike back, the rebels were too spread out. Ozzel suggested that scrounging more worlds with constant bombardments via Velgor's new Death Squadron, but Veers intervened and came up with a theory; Veers claimed that the rebellion was like weeds, while the Dominion was like a storm; when a storm passed, weeds would bend and come out unharmed, whereas a tree could not withstand the onslaught. Until the rebellion was a solid singular unit, Veers argued, it would slip through their fingers. This triggered a memory in Velgor of when Mira Venn told Varnus: "The more you tighten your grip, the more star systems will slip through your fingers." Velgor told his subordinates that Veers made an interesting point and set out with a new strategy: give the Alliance time to gather its forces and assemble its might in one location, where they could be crushed.
Velgor traveled to Shu-Torun and approached Queen Trios in her meeting room. She was surprised at his arrival and assumed it was not on good terms. She pleaded that she had done nothing against him and that she had been a loyal servant. She then told her guards to lower their weapons. Trios asked Velgor how she could help him. Velgor revealed his plan for her specialists to mine the ruins of Zelara. After she was to supply information to the rebels that would be sufficient to entirely sabotage the mining efforts. Finally, she had to infiltrate the rebellion, which was trying to build a fleet, and do everything she could to help them. Velgor said to her that when they had built their armada, and before they hid it, he would show them how weak they truly were. Velgor then threatened her that he'd make Elara look like a dream to Shu-Torun if she tried anything. A Shu-Torun guard stepped forward and told Velgor that he could not threaten Trios like that. Trios shot him as Velgor was their honored guest. She then allowed Velgor to kill the rest of the people in the room.
Velgor's master stroke To Velgor's pleasure, Trios was successful in her mission, being able to earn Mira Venn's trust at Zelara and helping them prompt a mass mutiny of Mon Calamari merchant ships. As the rebels armed their new cruisers at the Mako-Ta Space Docks , she gave Velgor the go-ahead to attack, telling him that they expected nothing.
As the rebels prepared to scatter their new fleet, their hyperdrives failed to work, rendering the cruisers immobile from the system. Velgor arrived soon after with Death Squadron and Ozzel asked Velgor if they should begin a bombardment. Velgor told him to wait, saying he sensed their fear and that it was insufficient. He told Ozzel to give them time to understand. As the rebel fighters prepared to deploy, they realized that not only were the hanger doors not opening, but neither were the defense turrets able to fire or were their engines able to activate. This was because Venn had ensured that the cruisers were outfitted with Shu-Torun technology, meaning Trios could sabotage them. The cruisers were useless against Velgor's fleet, and Velgor ordered the nearest cruiser be destroyed. The fleet subsequently fired on General Vanden Willard's flagship Myrkon's Hope and destroyed it with ease, killing Willard. Ozzel then asked Velgor of their next target, and Velgor said hope.
The Dominion Cruisers began engaging the other Mon Calamari cruisers, and Trios attempted to get off the spacedocks on her ship. However, rebel forces boarded the vessel, and she was forced to escape in an escape pod. The rebels discovered they could get the cruiser's hangar doors to open by flying a ship towards it.
Bandwin Cor volunteered to do so and flew a shuttle towards the cruisers. The shuttle was reported to Velgor, but the Razor drones dealt with it before Velgor could make any decisions. Velgor's fleet soon destroyed another cruiser, sending it into a fireball. The Shadow Hawk then arrived at the system, and Dax Corbin was told to attempt the same thing by Venn. Velgor was notified of the Shadow Hawk s arrival and quickly went to his TIE advanced. Corbin flew the Shadow Hawk through the fleet and took on TIEs before flying towards General Jan Dodonna 's Concordium . Velgor then engaged the Shadow Hawk and hit the ship's stabilizer, sending it spiraling. Corbin set it to manual, and Velgor prepared to fire on the Shadow Hawk when it got back on course.
Velgor fired missiles at the Shadow Hawk as Corbin attempted to land on Commander Lajaie 's cruiser . Velgor was to force Lajaie's crew to abandon ship and ordered the Imperator to destroy the escape pods. He continued to pursue the Shadow Hawk until his TIE was disabled temporarily in a nearby asteroid field.
Velgor was able to resume his attack on the rebel fleet's starfighters. Velgor then ordered the Dominion fleet to close in on the Coalition fleet. After being informed that Trios was returning to the Imperator , Velgor returned to the ship where he intended to meet her. However, he learned that rebels had boarded the Imperator by using Trios' shuttle.
He pursued the rebels until he found several led by General Davits Draven and killed all of them. However, their mission was just to slow Velgor down and Velgor Flux choked Draven. Velgor caught up with Venn but was unable to stop her from escaping the Imperator via a Razor drone. As one rebel cruiser left, Velgor ordered the Dominion fleet to destroy the remaining rebel vessels.
Velgor continued to oversee the destruction of the remaining rebel ships. Despite the return of a rebel cruiser, Velgor pushed the attack. The rebels capital ships were eventually able to retreat and disperse in order to reorganize. Velgor informed the Overlord of the rebels' defeat and assured him that the Coalition forces could never win.
Revealing the truth Project Swarm Keeping an eye on loose ends Three years after the Battle of Myrkon, Velgor was still in command of the Imperator , under the admiralty of Ozzel, and Death Squadron.
At Velgor's request,
Lieutenant Commander Ciena Ree was transferred from the Obliterator to the Imperator ; Ree believed, however, that Velgor's request had been more threat than reward, as she remained forever vulnerable to him for seeing him during Myrkon's immediate aftermath.
Obsessed with finding Joren, Velgor dispatched thousands of Viper probe automatons as Project Swarm into the far reaches of space in order to find the rebel base and, by extension, his son.
Amongst the project on the Imperator were a number of archaeological consultants, who were brought in due to the rebellion's prior history of hiding in historical sites. The archaeological supervisor, Professor Rupo Ud , secretly only saw the operation as a way to study historical sites and disliked the Shade Lord, but he knew to stay quiet, having seen Velgor execute gifted individuals on little pretext.
Dominion forces also found 0-0-0 and BT-1 on the Ring of Kafrene and installed restraining bolts on both, suppressing their memories and returning them to Velgor's service. Both were stationed on the Imperator as interrogation specialists.
When Lyra Venn exposed propaganda Minister Pitina Mar-Mas Voor 's plot to assassinate Overlord Draven Nul in an effort to secure a pardon for herself, which she hoped would also keep those she loved safe by stopping the Dominion hunt for her, Velgor traveled to Nexara's moon Centax-3 to execute Voor for her treason. Breaching Voor's room with two legion vanguard, Velgor decapitated the rogue minister, with his appearance scaring Lyra Venn, who believed Velgor would kill her despite her role in saving Draven Nul. Velgor, however, simply demanded she stand and recruited her into Project Swarm as one of its archaeological consultants. Lyra Venn and her ward Vulaada Klam were assigned a quarters on the Imperator , where Klam was assigned a cleaning job and Lyra Venn experienced regular nightmares of the ways Velgor could hurt her, the worst of which being thrown from an airlock and losing an air mask thanks to his Flux abilities. It was also Lyra Venn's belief that, while she was somewhat protected by her actions against Voor,
Velgor was waiting for any good excuse to kill her, thereby finally silencing an old loose end. Eventually, the progress on Project Swarm became too slow for Velgor's liking, so he executed project supervisor Yaltza After General Veers was assigned oversight and introduced himself to the project's archaeological consultants, Lyra Venn caught sight of Velgor walking past and followed him past a mouse automaton before speaking, pleading for her life but being thrown into a wall when she mentioned Kast's name. Velgor left her on the floor as he departed, leaving Lyra Venn to devote herself to finding the rebel base to secure further Dominion favor, thereby saving her from Velgor's wrath. After Ud found a possible lead on the rebel base after a probe automaton was shot down on Ash Moon 1 , Lyra Venn joined the ground mission to stay out of Velgor's sights, further using the opportunity to kill Ud, thereby taking the position of supervisor for herself, and abandon Klam to keep her out of her dangerous life.
While there was no sign of rebel activity at the site, Dominion forces did pick up a masked figure, who claimed to know Keeper mind tricks and carried rebel rations, they took prisoner. On Veers's orders, Lyra Venn was sent to inform Velgor of the prisoner, with Lyra Venn noting the individual could be Kast; not realizing the Shade Lord was passing her to exit the room, Lyra Venn fell to her knees as Velgor approached and promised she had not revealed any of Velgor's secrets.
Joined by Lyra Venn when he demanded she accompany him, Velgor Flux choked and then physically choked the masked individual in an interrogation cell for the location of the rebel base, promising to kill him slowly if he refused, but was met by a Keeper apothegm about the power of the bright flux. Velgor quickly ordered Veers seal the room and bring in 0-0-0 before removing the individual's mask, revealing Lyra Venn's father Korin Velgor ignored Lyra Venn's pleas to spare her father from torture, citing his knowledge of the Keepers, and left the arriving 0-0-0 and BT-1 to extract everything Korin knew, with the protocol automaton specifically hoping Velgor would let him use the new tooth wrenches in his torture. Due to his bias against the Coalition for its exploitation of ancient temples, Korin, however, would quickly reveal everything he knew, which was relayed to Velgor by Veers and Lyra Venn after they departed the cell. Now pointed in the direction of a rebel cell using temple on Asteroid Helix 13-v , Velgor personally ventured to the asteroid with an Dominion force, BT-1, and the two Aphras, intending to see which of the two was more useful to him.
Old memories on Helix 13-v … M…Mind tricks… these … these ghosts might once have troubled that lost boy … but I am not him anymore. ―Xarn Velgor reacts to the visions of his past on Helix 13-v After arriving on Asteroid Helix 13-v, Velgor felt a presence in the Synth Flux he believed to be from a person and left his compliment of legion vanguard outside, much to Chelli Lyra Venn's horror: she believed Velgor was personally engineering a situation where there were no witnesses in the room with him and his old loose end, aside from BT-1. Much to her confusion, however, Velgor was far more interested in the presence he felt than dealing with her and left to investigate once he gave BT-1 orders to maim either Lyra Venn for disobedience.
In secret, the rebels had left behind several cameras in the atrium, which gave the Unnamed under Chelli's former lover Magna Tolvan several glimpses of Velgor.
As Velgor ventured deeper into the shrine, unknowingly followed by Lyra Venn when she had news on rebel traces left behind that pointed to a cold destination, Velgor ignited his flux blade and demanded the presence reveal itself, only to be attacked by visions and mind tricks about his past. Declaring that he was no longer the "lost boy" who would be troubled by such visions, Velgor destroyed the source of the presence, an altar drenched in the wills of those who had once prayed to it, and departed, unaware that Lyra Venn had overhead his comment.
Intending to review the survey reports on the Imperator , Velgor ordered a confused legion vanguard sergeant to immediately shut down the shrine despite the little work that had been done to explore it, further ordering BT-1 to signal Death Squadron to dispatch TIE/sa bombers, since it was clear the rebels had left. When Korin protested destroying a historical site and chased after Velgor while the Shade Lord was on his way back to a Lambda
-class T-4a shuttle, Velgor thew Korin to the side with the Synth Flux before telling BT-1 to kill whichever one of the Aphras was unnecessary, giving them only the trip back to the
Imperator to decide who would die. Before they could reach the Super Dominion Cruiser, however, the Aphras' Lambda was attacked by Strike Team Misericorde under Tolvan, whom Chelli would trick to uncover the location of the new rebel base: the frozen planet Kryos in the Outer Rim.
After sending her unconscious father away in the Lambda on a course to Ash Moon I, where he would unite with Klam, with Tolvan on its trail, thereby ensuring her loved ones would meet each other and become a group of their own,
Lyra Venn had BT-1 contact 0-0-0 aboard the Imperator on the promise she would remove their restraining bolts as part of a plan to herself escape from Velgor and disrupt his search for the rebel base, where her loved ones would be based as part of the Alliance.
Aboard the Imperator , Velgor had retired to his meditation chamber when he was interrupted by 0-0-0, who began to test the Shade Lord's patience with remarks about Velgor's injured appearance, with a private message. Beginning to crush the protocol automaton with the Synth Flux and hold him in the air, Velgor demanded the message and learned it was from Chelli Lyra Venn, who claimed to know the location of the rebel base at a cold location.
Instead of bringing him to Kryos, however, Lyra Venn led Velgor and a detachment of Dominion forces to the Meridional Ice Cap of the ancient Keeper planet Tython , where she knew the Martyrium of Frozen Tears which she had learned from her father supposedly contained a confessional made of solid kyberite, was located; having seen Velgor briefly succumb to his past regrets at the Helix temple, she intended to weigh him down with his past regrets on Tython as well. It was her hope that the confessional would eclipse his Flux abilities while she worked.
Lyra Venn's escape Hey— boss —listen! F-for what it's worth, I wish it hadn't come to this! But—you oughta know… regrets are kinda my thing . … So, listen … I don't know what broke you. If I had to guess , I'd shoot for the whole love betrayal loss thing. Lotta that goin' 'round. What matters is you've tamped it all down. Right? Cold apathy and even colder anger. But I saw you on the comet Helix. And I see you now . This much pain—when you try to bury it? Just makes it twice as strong when it finds a way out . I know a guilty freakin' disaster when I see one, sir. ―Lyra Venn, to an injured Xarn Velgor Velgor traveled to the Meridional Ice Cap aboard his personal TIE Advanced, while a group of cold weather assault legion vanguard, at least one officer, and 0-0-0 under Colonel Bryce arrived on two TIE/rp Reaper attack landers . Outside the Martyrium of Frozen Tears, Velgor and his troops were met by Lyra Venn, who promised Velgor the rebels were still preparing a base hidden deep underground and discretely implied Kast could be present. Inside, Velgor ordered his forces scan for the supposed rebel presence and revealed 0-0-0 was present, which Lyra Venn pretended to be surprised by. However, Velgor felt through the Synth Flux that Lyra Venn was planning something and promising to kill her if she had been lying; sure enough, the colonel reported there was no sign of activity in the temple, which Velgor immediately responded to by ordering 0-0-0 to kill Lyra Venn. However, BT-1 managed to separate 0-0-0 and Lyra Venn from the Imperials in time; Lyra Venn honored her deal to remove their restraining bolts and convinced them to keep her alive as Velgor began to pursue because she knew the temple's secrets.
Velgor burst through a wall into Lyra Venn and the automatons' hiding place with several snowtroopers, including members of First squad , whom he ordered to move eastward but to leave Lyra Venn for him. However, Lyra Venn activated a trap that unleashed a flame, which Velgor defended himself against but engulfed several surrounding troops. Contacting Bryce and ordering him to withdraw their forces, only for 0-0-0 to kill Bryce before the colonel could issue a retreat, Velgor embarked on his hunt alone despite a few snowtroopers remaining close. He tracked Lyra Venn to the other side of another wall, which he began to cut through until BT-1 tripped another trap, separating Velgor from his troops, all of whom were soon massacred by the automaton. Velgor, however, quickly freed himself from the trap and came face-to-face with Lyra Venn, whom he demanded kneel as he prepared to cut her down. When 0-0-0 suddenly attacked, Velgor cut the automaton down into several parts but was distracted for long enough that several explosive darts could hit his armor and knock him back. With Velgor trapped under the kyberite confessional,
Lyra Venn activated it and trapped Velgor within. Under the weight of the many sinners who had confessed their own regrets in the temple over the course of centuries, Velgor was forced to endure visions of his own past regrets, much like his less severe reaction to the altar at the Helix temple.
After a moment of silence as he found himself trapped under his guilt, Velgor reacted in anger and threw his flux blade at Lyra Venn, who dodged the blade and ordered BT-1 to fire on Velgor's legs to knock him back down. Much of Velgor's already damaged armor was ripped apart under the automaton's barrage, including his legs, but the crawling Shade Lord ripped BT-1 apart with the Synth Flux, proving to Lyra Venn the shrine would not keep his powers as suppressed as she had hoped. Having failed to escape the confessional and suddenly with failing cybernetics, however, Velgor fell back to the ground as Lyra Venn spoke to him about regrets, only for Velgor to turn to Lyra Venn and promise she would never succeed in killing him. Lyra Venn, however, had no such plans and knew to stay outside of the confessional, thereby avoiding Velgor's currently limited range. Attacked with a wave of regret when Lyra Venn called him the "lost boy," Velgor was hit by a cord fired by Lyra Venn, who used it to hack into Velgor's suit and contact comms jockey on the Imperator . Using Velgor's voice, she scared the officer and ordered him to alter and delete numerous probe automaton records, which she hoped would hold back Velgor's search for Echo Base by several weeks.
While Lyra Venn knew Velgor would one day find the rebels, she was certain she had done something good and bid goodbye to Velgor, who promised he would find her again one day.
While she was still terrified of him and knew he would never stop his hunt for her, Lyra Venn left Velgor with the hope he, too, would one day "do something good" and declared he had, from a certain point of view, been the best thing that had ever happened to her. Lyra Venn soon escaped Tython aboard one of the TIE/rp Reaper attack landers with the remains of 0-0-0 and BT-1, both of whom would be rebuilt and dropped off by Lyra Venn on the planet Birukay outside of Dominion notice.
As Lyra Venn made her escape, Velgor broke out of the confessional by destroying it in a burst of rage.
The Empire put out an arrest warrant for Lyra Venn and tried to keep the damage she caused their search a secret, but the mess she left behind was set to keep Velgor from his son and the rebels for long enough that the rebels—Tolvan, Korin, and Klam among them—could finalize their defenses and set up an escape plan.
Kryos Lord Velgor, the fleet has moved out of hyperlight and we are preparing to— You have failed me for the last time, Admiral. Captain Drax.
Yes, my Lord.
Make ready to land our troops beyond their energy field, then deploy the fleet so that nothing gets off the system. You are in command now,
Admiral Drax.
Thank you, Lord Velgor. I won't fail you. ―Admiral Kendal Ozzel, Xarn Velgor and Corbin Drax Later, while the Imperator and the rest of Death Squadron were stationed in the Juris sector the flagship received a fragmented report from probe automaton XJ9-CS14 , which had been sent to the Kryos system . Although Admiral Ozzel dismissed the news as a false lead, both Velgor and Captain Corbin Drax agreed that they had found the Coalition base. Velgor then gave orders for the fleet to depart for the system. As the fleet came out of voidspace near Kryos , Velgor was informed by General Veers that orbital bombardment was impossible as an energy shield defended Echo Base . Velgor guessed from this information that the Alliance knew of their presence because Ozzel had made the fleet exit voidspace too close to the system. After ordering Veers to prepare his troops for a ground assault, Velgor executed Ozzel for his incompetence and promoted Captain Drax to replace him as Admiral, and had him deploy the fleet so that no Coalition ship escaped the system.
As the Dominion ground forces led by Veers engaged the Alliance,Velgor led an assault troop into the Coalition base and broke their lines, but they found it mostly deserted, for General Carlist Rieekan had ordered its evacuation pre-emotively.
Velgor entered the base and soon ran into a group of Coalition forces carrying their unconscious captain towards a hangar. They open fire, and Velgor deflected their shots before leaping and cutting down a Coalition trooper named Beak . Another trooper, named Roja , threw a grenade at Velgor, who used the Synth Flux to deflect it. It knocked out their sergeant,
Hazram Namir , and Roja was killed soon after. The last Coalition was the former Dominion governor of Haidoral Prime Everi Chalis . She had been pursued by Dominion forces in the weeks following her defection and assumed Velgor was there for her. Velgor only asked her where Joren was, and when a trooper told him that they had located the Shadow Hawk , he pushed her aside and marched in the direction of his target.
Only the Shadow Hawk remained at the base's hangar, but the light freighter was able to escape before Velgor could capture it. With the Alliance's forces on Kryos in complete retreat and the various transports scattered throughout the stellar realm, Velgor and his troops had secured a great victory for the Dominion.
Rather than pursuing the Coalition transports which had gotten away,
Velgor redirected his Death Squadron's efforts to capturing the Shadow Hawk , which had evaded Dominion forces in the Kryos asteroid belt due to its faulty hyperdrive. Despite Drax's reservations, Velgor ordered the fleet to pursue the ship into the asteroid field, and told the Imperator s crew that he wanted the freighter towed aboard and its passengers taken alive.
Even though the asteroids heavily damaged the Dominion Cruisers, Velgor instructed every ship to sweep the field until the Shadow Hawk was found.
Drax then informed him that the Overlord commanded him to make contact, and Velgor had the Imperator move out of the field to have a clear transmission. Velgor contacted his master; the Overlord told him that he had sensed a new threat in the Synth Flux: Joren Kast, who he feared could destroy them if he ever became a Keeper, and that he was Anakin's son. Feigning shock at the Overlord's revelation, Velgor believed Joren could be turned to the deep flux and prove a powerful ally. Although the Overlord was doubtful, Velgor assured him that Joren would join them or die.
Determined to find his son before the Overlord, Velgor summoned several bounty hunters— 4-LOM , Bossk,
Zuckuss , Dengar,
IG-88 , and Kael Ryn—to the Imperator , and promised a substantial reward for the one who found the Shadow Hawk s crew alive. As he was briefing the hunters , Drax informed Velgor that the Avenger was tracking the Shadow Hawk . Velgor requested an update from the ship's commander, Captain Lorth Needa , who reported that he had lost the Shadow Hawk and assumed full responsibility for this. Velgor accepted his apology with a fatal Flux choke, and then ordered Drax to alert all commands of the Shadow Hawk s disappearance.
Cloud City Zeth never told you what happened to your father.
He told me enough! He told me you killed him!
No. am your father. ―Xarn Velgor's revelation to Joren Kast As the Shadow Hawk traveled through realspace to try and repair its hyperdrive on Bespin , Ryn tracked and predicted Corbin's movements, but also contacted Velgor, whose Imperator reached Bespin before the freighter.
Along with a strike team comprising only a few individuals, Velgor left the Imperator for Cloud City , leaving its crew to do nothing but wait hidden.
At the city, Velgor contacted its Baron Administrator Drev Kass , who happened to be Corbin's friend, to make a deal. Drev Kass, whose tibanna mining operation was keen to evade Dominion attention, agreed to help him capture the ship in exchange for them leaving his people and his interests alone.
When the Shadow Hawk arrived, Joren was not among them, but Velgor knew he could use his friends to lure his son to Bespin. Escorted by Ryn, Velgor waited for Corbin, Venn, Gorath, and Drev Kass.
Whilst waiting in the Rinetta dining room , he was barged in upon by the Lepi smuggler Jaxxon T. Tumperakki , who quickly left after the Dark Lord and the smuggler stared at each other. A team of legion vanguard was then dispatched to arrest Tumperakki.
When the Coalition forces arrived, Velgor had them surrounded by legion vanguard, imprisoned—and, in the case of Captain Corbin, tortured. Much to Drev Kass's displeasure, Velgor altered their deal, agreeing to let Ryn take Corbin and collect Jabba's bounty on him. Afterward, he inspected one of the city's carbon-freezing chambers , and deemed it adequate if crude, to freeze Joren for his journey to the Overlord, but at the danger of it being fatal, he had the facility tested on Captain Corbin.
On his orders, Corbin was frozen in carbonite ; as he survived, Velgor handed Corbin over to Ryn and ordered Venn, Gorath, and LQ-9M taken to his ship, while he awaited Joren at the facility. Once his son was separated from DR-7X and made his way to the carbon-freezing chamber, Velgor appeared before him, and the two dueled . Joren held his own for much of the duel, but he was ultimately no match for Velgor: as they moved to a reactor shaft and a catwalk,
Joren managed to cut Velgor's shoulder, only for Velgor to slice off his right hand, which along with his old flux blade fell through the shaft.
Velgor beckoned Joren to join him in the deep flux, but Joren vehemently refused. Velgor then told Joren that Malkor had hidden from him the truth about his father, Xarn Velgor, before revealing that he was, in fact, his father. Joren refused to believe the truth, but Velgor continued to tempt his weakened son, offering Joren the chance to destroy the Overlord and "bring order to the stellar realm," just as he tried to do with Padmé on Mustafar.
He even pleaded with his son to come with him.
In Velgor's ideal world, his son would have taken his hand, accepting him as his father.
However, Kast instead chose to throw himself into the shaft, possibly facing death to avoid joining Velgor.
While Joren was rescued by his friends on the Shadow Hawk , Velgor returned to the Imperator . As the freighter was unable to escape due to its deactivated hyperdrive, Velgor and Drax waited for the Shadow Hawk to enter tractor-beam range. During the pursuit, Velgor reached out to Joren through the Synth Flux, telling him it was his destiny to come with him, calling him "son." Yet, the Shadow Hawk made the jump into voidspace, thanks to DR-7X reactivating the hyperdrive. Although Drax feared Velgor would kill him for his failure, Velgor merely turned away from the viewport, not in anger, but in sadness.
Upon learning that Velgor had allowed Ryn to take the carbon frozen Corbin back to Jabba, ISB agent Andressa Divo was frustrated that the smuggler would not be brought to Dominion imprisonment, although she also decided she had no position to question Velgor's orders.
Search for answers You served Padmé. You want revenge as well. But you fear me. Good. Let that fear guide you. I will always triumph. And if you serve me, so will you. Together, we can find them. ―Xarn Velgor, to Sabé Following the Shadow Hawk s escape, Velgor took a shuttle with several death troopers and a forensics automaton named ZED-6-7 with him on a mission to learn who had hidden his son from him. After modifying ZED's motivator, he took the shuttle to Vorath Prime. There, he met with Lieutenant Ardo Banch and his legion vanguard. Velgor went on to the Lars Moisture Farm to search for clues about Kast's early life. Velgor also reminisced about the attempted rescue of his mother and her funeral. Velgor was informed by Lieutenant Banch of several transmissions, but came under attack by gangsters who wanted his shuttle. Velgor and his death troopers killed the gangsters before he took his shuttle to Nexara City-world and returned to Amidala's apartment. There, ZED-6-7 discovered that someone had planted a scout transmitter , which led to Vendaxa At some point, Velgor also returned to the Dominion Palace and met with Draven Nul, who ordered him to hunt down the remnants of the Coalition fleet while it was scattered, believing he may be the only officer in the Dominion who could lead such a hunt despite it interrupting his quest to find his son. Unwilling to abandon that search, Velgor instead proposed they give the mission to Varnus's protégé,
Ellian Zahra . Contacting her via hologram, Velgor granted her permission to return to active duty to try and redeem herself for a past failure under Varnus, making it clear she had no room to fail him and the Overlord.
Shortly after the Shadow Hawk s escape and its vital role in the Battle at Rendezvous Point Delta-Three , Velgor chastised Zahra for attempting to destroy the vessel during the engagement, stating that while Mira Venn would be an acceptable casualty, another passenger was of value to him. Velgor further threatened consequence to Zahra should she report further of "partial success" before dismissing her.
Before journeying to Vendaxa, the Shade Lord traveled to a cantina and confronted a man who claimed to be his son Joren Kast. Velgor quickly found that the man was a liar, telling him that it did not matter who the man was. Because by tomorrow, no one would remember that he ever lived. Velgor then killed the impostor and told the people who were about to hire him that he would let them live. That way, they would be witnesses for what the man had done. The Shade Covenant then stated that whoever claimed the name of Joren Kast would also claim the crimes he had committed against the Dominion. After leaving the cantina, Velgor admonished the Dominion officer who had given him the information on the alleged Joren Kast, telling him his information was worthless and that he had simply wasted his time.
When Joren Kast met Verla on Serelia , she told him about Order 66 and the Dominion Inquisitors, describing Velgor as pure, true evil. Shortly thereafter, she discovered through the force that Velgor was Joren's father, but Kast proved to her he was not like his forebear. Before Velgor continued his personal mission, he felt through the Synth Flux, during a conversation with Zahra, that the trap he had placed within a Keeper outpost on Tempes had been activated. He ordered his pilot to set course for Tempes, but when he arrived there, he found nothing but the spirit of the Grand Inquisitor, who had recently been defeated by Joren Kast. When asked by the Grand Inquisitor if he would ever be freed from Velgor's service, the Shade Lord refused to do so, saying that the Inquisitor was merely a tool for him. Velgor then abandoned his cursed servant, who was slowly engulfed by the ghostly flames around his body.
Upon arriving on Vendaxa, Velgor was confronted by "
Padmé Amidala Amidala, revealed to be former Aqualis Royal Handmaiden Sabé, was briefly choked by Velgor before they came under attack by several Vendaxan land squids . Together, they eliminated the squids, allowing Sabé to give her slain soldiers a proper burial. Sabé also revealed her true motives; to investigate the death of her former queen. Velgor agreed to assist her in her mission so long as she obeyed him.
Following Sabé's guidance, Velgor returned to the planet Aqualis to retrieve security recordings stolen from Amidala's quarters on Nexara City-world. Upon entering the Naberri Lake Retreat, they were met by Gregar Typho and Tonra , who agreed to help them retrieve the stolen recordings. Taking a submarine , the four of them left for the recordings' location, while Sabé expressed regret at her inability to save either her queen or Xarn Velgor's mother. After surviving an attack by a colo claw fish , they reached their destination and found the recordings, which showed Sabé forming the Amidalans and swearing to find and destroy their queen's murderers. She then accused Velgor of murdering Amidala himself, to which Velgor said he did before attacking the Amidalans.
However, the fight was quickly interrupted by a sando aqua monster , which attacked Velgor and allowed the Amidalans to escape. After slaying the monster along with his death troopers, Velgor found the rest of the Amidalans in the jungles of Aqualis, where they were led into battle by Ric Olié.
In the battle at Padmé's tomb Velgor and his death troopers eliminated most of the Amidalans and incapacitated Olié before moving onto their next destination: the Tomb of Padmé Amidala . But before they could enter the tomb, they were surrounded by Sabé and other members of Amidala's surviving handmaidens:
Eirtaé Rabé Tonsort Saché , and Dormé . The handmaidens fought bravely, but were easily defeated by Velgor, who then opened the door to Amidala's tomb.
In truth, the handmaidens had known they would be unable to defeat the Shade Lord, but instead made sure Eirtaé could tap into his armor with her staff. Doing so got Eirtaé an incomplete set of code, which the handmaidens hoped would one day get them the Imperator s self-destruct codes.
In the meantime, unaware of Eirtaé's success and ignoring Sabé's protests, Velgor proceeded into the tomb and allowed ZED-6-7 to scan Amidala's sarcophagus. The automaton then discovered a med implant , which he traced back to the asteroid field Polis Massa . Upon reaching the asteroid field, they entered the Polis Massa Base and uncovered data that Amidala had been flown to the base by Zeth Malkor in an effort to save her life. However, Velgor and his forces were then attacked once more by the Amidalans, whom he managed to defeat once and for all. Along with ZED-6-7, Velgor entered the base's maternity ward and allowed the automaton to access the memory bank of a damaged Chroon-Tan B-Machine . He then watched a recording of Amidala speaking with Malkor in her final moments of life. When ZED-6-7 commented that Malkor must have been important to her, Velgor promptly destroyed the automaton in one-thirtieth of a second.
The recording then ended with Amidala stating that she knew there was still good in Velgor, leaving the Shade Covenant to mull over his wife's last words.
Test on Mustafar I have turned his pain into fear…and his fear into anger. But he has learned and forgotten these lessons before. So this time I will send him a reminder. Through Ochi of Bestoon…assassin of the Shade Covenant…I will turn his anger back to fear…and his fear back to pain…as many times as it takes. ―Draven Nul After learning of Velgor's personal mission, the Overlord summoned his apprentice to the Dominion Palace, where he sensed his apprentice was filled with mourning instead of hate. Intending to reteach him the deep flux, Sidious began to torture him with Flux lightning.
Velgor fought back against his master, but was overwhelmed again by two Royal Guards led by Mas Amedda. While Velgor did manage to choke the three of them with the Synth Flux, he in turn was then choked by the Overlord, who mocked the Keepers title of "Chosen One" before telling Velgor to forget everything from his past and ravaging his body, leaving him with only one of his hands intact. The Overlord then brought his apprentice to Mustafar and left him on the very shore where Malkor left him at the end of their duel. The Overlord instructed Velgor to rebuild himself without using the Synth Flux before leaving with Mas Amedda.
Remembering how Malkor had left him on the same shore and how he later cut the Keepers Master down on the Void Core, then picturing Sidious in Malkor's place in the latter memory,
Velgor resolved to crawl from the shore towards the facility where he murdered the Separatist leaders years before, remembering the time his master had sent him to the base to wipe out the Separatist leadership. Promising himself that he would bring suffering to the Overlord when he recovered and spotting a figure laughing at him in the distance, he entered the base and, after spotting the corpse of Nute Gunray and remembering his actions against the Separatists and Keeper younglings alike, found a few mouse automatons, which he rewired to weld old battle automaton limbs to his body. However, he was then confronted by the figure he had seen:
Ochi of Bestoon , a Shade Covenant assassin sent by the Overlord to test his apprentice. With his new limbs, Velgor fought the assassin and bested him. He then demanded to know what his master's greater plan was before being interrupted by a distant voice. Velgor followed the voice into a cave, which Ochi trapped him inside of after stealing his flux blade.
As Velgor fought his way through the cave, the voice taunted him with riddles about what he wanted and who he truly was. Eventually, Velgor reached the source of the voice; an ancient being known as the Eye of Webbish Bog .
